### [30] [Tracing the Cascade: A Topology-Aware Evaluation Framework for Scientific Agent Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00711)
*Xinshun Feng,Ziqi Miao,Lijun Li,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 36 pages, 7 figures and 5 tables

Selection reason: 科学agent轨迹幻觉的拓扑感知评估框架，轨迹流水线与反事实归因可迁移到coding agent可靠性验证。

TL;DR: 提出了SCHEMA，首个基于证据且拓扑感知的科学智能体幻觉评估框架，揭示幻觉集中在知识枢纽，且最终答案准确性与推理轨迹诚实性脱钩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准忽略知识间的拓扑结构，孤立评估事实，无法捕捉科学推理中错误传播的级联效应，导致单一准确率指标无法反映高风险场景下的真实可靠性。

Method: SCHEMA自动构建科学概念图，合成多类型图接地任务，并使用轨迹幻觉流水线（拓扑加权严重性评分）和多智能体反事实归因模块进行双重诊断。

Result: 幻觉集中在少量高连接知识枢纽；最终答案准确性与轨迹诚实性脱钩，模型常以有缺陷的推理达成正确结论。

Conclusion: 对于高 stakes 科学应用，终端准确性不足以作为可靠性信号，需采用基于知识拓扑的机制级评估。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地部署在科学研究中，其中可靠性至关重要，且底层知识密集互联。在此类场景中，幻觉尤其有害：一个关于基础概念的单一错误声明可能通过多步推理传播并破坏整个轨迹。现有的幻觉基准大多在表面层面运作，将事实孤立处理，并依赖忽略这种拓扑结构的统一准确率指标。我们通过SCHEMA来解决这一差距，这是首个针对科学智能体中幻觉的、基于证据且拓扑感知的评估框架。SCHEMA从基准种子和文献证据自动构建科学概念图，综合生成涵盖声明验证、多跳推理、开放式解释和实验代码生成的图接地任务，并通过两个互补的诊断模块评估智能体。轨迹幻觉流水线通过拓扑加权严重性评分大规模审计中间推理，而多智能体反事实归因模块则定位所选失败背后的因果机制。SCHEMA揭示，幻觉集中在少量高连接度的知识枢纽上，并且最终答案的准确性与轨迹的诚实性脱钩；模型经常通过结构上有缺陷的推理得出正确结论。这些结果表明，对于高风险的科学应用，终端准确性单独不足以作为智能体可靠性的信号，从而促使基于知识拓扑的机制级评估。代码可在https://github.com/circles-post/SCHEMA获取。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in scientific research, where reliability is critical and the underlying knowledge is densely interconnected. In such settings, hallucinations are particularly damaging: a single erroneous claim on a foundational concept can propagate through multi-step reasoning and corrupt entire trajectories. Existing hallucination benchmarks largely operate at the surface level, treating facts in isolation and relying on uniform accuracy metrics that ignore this topological structure. We address this gap with SCHEMA, the first evidence-grounded, topology-aware evaluation framework for hallucinations in scientific agents. SCHEMA automatically constructs scientific concept graphs from benchmark seeds and literature evidence, synthesizes graph-grounded tasks spanning claim verification, multi-hop reasoning, open-ended explanation, and experimental code generation, and evaluates agents with two complementary diagnostics. A trajectory hallucination pipeline audits intermediate reasoning at scale via a topology-weighted severity score, while a multi-agent counterfactual attribution module pinpoints the causal mechanism behind selected failures. SCHEMA reveals that hallucinations concentrate at a small set of highly connected knowledge hubs, and that final-answer accuracy decouples from trajectory honesty; models often reach correct conclusions through structurally flawed reasoning. These results indicate that for high-stakes scientific applications, terminal accuracy alone is an insufficient signal of agent reliability, motivating mechanism-level evaluation grounded in knowledge topology. Code is available at https://github.com/circles-post/SCHEMA.

</details>


### [31] [FinDeepIndicator: Benchmarking Deep Research Agents in End-to-End Financial Indicator Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00764)
*Chaoqun Yang,Fengbin Zhu,Xinyu Lin,Long Bai,Xiaoluan Liu,Ke-Wei Huang,Roger Zimmermann,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 金融深度研究agent分阶段评测benchmark，揭示检索与执行环节可靠性问题，可迁移到长程agent评测。

TL;DR: 提出了FinDeepIndicator，首个专门评估深度研究代理在端到端财务指标构建中的基准，覆盖四个阶段和21个子类别，含3350个QA对。实验表明LLM在数据检索和数值计算上表现不佳，DR代理更优但仍不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准只评估答案准确性并假设数据已提供，忽视指标构建的中间过程，缺乏对深度研究代理在此任务上的系统评估。

Method: 构建FinDeepIndicator基准，涵盖公式规范、数据收集、指标计算和答案生成四个阶段，包含基本面、技术面、宏观经济指标共21个细分类别，基于中美市场10年数据和800家公司，生成3350个QA对，并对搜索增强的LLM和DR代理进行实验评估。

Result: LLM在公式规范上表现良好，但在数据检索和数值执行时准确率大幅下降；DR代理一致优于搜索增强的LLM，但在真实场景中仍不可靠。

Conclusion: 当前DR代理在财务指标构建中尚不可靠，需要进一步提升其数据检索和数值执行能力，为开发更强大且可信的金融DR代理提供了方向。

中文摘要：财务指标是将原始财务数据转换为可解释度量以用于各种下游任务（如估值、风险评估和经济分析）的重要工具。然而，现有的金融基准大多侧重于答案级准确性，并且通常假设相关数据已提供，从而忽略了对指标构建中间过程的评估。在这项工作中，我们提出了FinDeepIndicator，这是第一个专门用于评估深度研究（DR）代理在端到端财务指标构建中表现的基准。具体来说，FinDeepIndicator在指标构建的四个阶段评估DR代理：公式规范、数据收集、指标计算和答案生成，并涵盖基本面、技术面和宏观经济指标，组织成21个细分子类别。它包含3,350个精选的问答（QA）对，来源于美国和中国市场，10年的历史财务数据，以及800家上市公司。对配备搜索功能的大型语言模型（LLM）和DR代理的大量实验表明，虽然LLM通常在公式规范方面表现良好，但在数据检索和数值执行过程中其准确率大幅下降。DR代理始终优于配备搜索功能的LLM，但在真实金融分析场景中仍然不可靠。这些发现为开发更强大且值得信赖的金融DR代理提供了见解。

Original abstract: Financial indicators are essential tools for transforming raw financial data into interpretable measures for various downstream tasks, such as valuation, risk assessment, and economic analysis. However, existing financial benchmarks largely focus on answer-level accuracy and often assume that relevant data are already provided, leaving the assessment of the intermediate process of indicator construction underexplored. In this work, we propose FinDeepIndicator, the first benchmark dedicated to evaluating Deep Research (DR) agents in end-to-end financial indicator construction. Specifically, FinDeepIndicator evaluates DR agents across four stages in indicator construction: formula specification, data collection, indicator calculation, and answer generation, and covers fundamental, technical, and macroeconomic indicators organized into 21 fine-grained sub-categories. It contains 3,350 curated question-answer (QA) pairs derived from both U.S. and Chinese markets, 10 years of historical financial data, and 800 listed companies. Extensive experiments on search-equipped Large Language Models (LLMs) and DR agents show that, while LLMs generally perform well in formula specification, their accuracy drops substantially during data retrieval and numerical execution. DR agents consistently outperform search-equipped LLMs, yet remain unreliable in realistic financial analysis settings. These findings provide insights for developing more capable and trustworthy DR agents in finance.

</details>


### [32] [Measurement Without Validity: The Compounding Reliability Problem in Agentic AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00794)
*William Caban*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 34 pages (review/double-spaced format), 2 figures, 5 tables. Submitted to Knowledge-Based Systems (Elsevier)

Selection reason: 系统分析agent评测中任务生成、模拟、评判三层可靠性复合问题，对coding agent benchmark设计有方法论价值。

TL;DR: 本文通过实证分析揭示智能体AI评估基准的分数存在系统性不可信问题，问题在任务生成、用户模拟和判断三个层面复合，导致有效信号大量丢失，并提出基于心理测量学的八项改进处方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统的分数被用于部署决策、安全认证和合规监管，但现有评估实践缺乏对分数可信度的验证，可能导致不安全的部署或错误的监管决策。

Method: 采用多方法实证分析：审计十个流行基准的有效性和报告质量；汇总LLM模拟器校准研究；对55篇论文进行结构化调查以评估IRR指标使用；并形式化构建有效性乘积模型$V_{\text{total}} \leq V_1 \times V_2 \times V_3$，同时分析相关故障下的影响。

Result: 发现七个基准存在有效性缺陷、十个均有报告缺失；模拟器间差异高达9个百分点且非标准英语使用者方向性校准错误；82%的论文IRR指标使用不当；在典型保留率下总有效性上限仅36%，范围0.22–0.54，相关故障下进一步收紧。

Conclusion: 评估管道的有效性受多层缺陷的乘积影响，需采用心理测量学标准：设定模拟校准下限（ICC(A,1)≥0.70）、分层信度阈值（α≥0.67/0.70/0.80）、结构化IRR指标选择规则，并强制报告IRR。现有工具已足够，关键在于实际应用。

中文摘要：智能体AI系统通过自动化基准进行评估，其分数用于证明部署决策、安全认证和合规性声明的合理性。我们提供了一项实证分析，表明这些分数在系统性上比当前实践所承认的更不可信。问题存在于三个叠加的层面：首先，任务越来越多地由语言模型生成：对十个流行基准的审计发现其中七个存在有效性缺陷，全部十个存在报告缺失。其次，人类用户被LLM模拟器取代，但校准研究记录了模拟器间差异高达9个百分点，以及系统性的方向性校准错误，尤其是针对非标准美国英语使用者。第三，我们对55篇论文的结构化调查发现，约82%的论文应用了结构不匹配、不完整或缺失的评分者间信度（IRR）指标。这些失败以乘法而非加法方式复合。在独立性假设下，一条在任务生成中保留70%有效信号、在模拟中保留80%、在判断中保留65%的流水线，其针对预期构念的有效性至多为36%；该界限在经验估计范围内跨越0.22–0.54。我们将其形式化为$V_{\text{total}} \leq V_1 \times V_2 \times V_3$，并表明当同一模型族在所有三个层面运行时，相关故障下该界限会进一步收紧。我们提出了基于心理测量科学的八项处方：模拟校准下限为$\text{ICC}(A,1) \geq 0.70$；按后果层级分层的信度阈值（$\alpha\geq 0.67$ / $0.70$ / $0.80$）；基于流水线设计的结构化IRR指标选择规则；以及将IRR作为强制性报告字段。测量工具存在；该领域的任务是应用它们。

Original abstract: Agentic AI systems are evaluated using automated benchmarks whose scores justify deployment decisions, safety certifications, and regulatory compliance claims. We present an empirical analysis demonstrating that these scores are systematically less trustworthy than current practice acknowledges.
  The problem operates at three compounding layers. First, tasks are increasingly generated by language models: audits of ten popular benchmarks found validity flaws in seven and reporting gaps in all ten. Second, human users are replaced by LLM simulators, but calibration studies document inter-simulator variance up to 9 percentage points and systematic directional miscalibration, particularly for non-Standard American English speakers. Third, our structured survey of 55 papers finds that approximately 82% apply structurally mismatched, incomplete, or absent inter-rater reliability (IRR) metrics.
  These failures compound multiplicatively rather than additively. Under independence, a pipeline retaining 70% of valid signal at task generation, 80% at simulation, and 65% at judgment is at most 36% valid against the intended construct; the bound spans 0.22--0.54 across the empirical estimate range. We formalize this as $V_{\text{total}} \leq V_1 \times V_2 \times V_3$ and show it tightens further under correlated failures when the same model family operates across all three layers.
  We derive eight prescriptions grounded in psychometric science: a simulation calibration floor of $\text{ICC}(A,1) \geq 0.70$; domain-stratified reliability thresholds ($α\geq 0.67$ / $0.70$ / $0.80$ by consequence level); structured IRR metric selection rules based on pipeline design; and IRR as a mandatory reporting field. The measurement tools exist; the field's task is to apply them.

</details>


### [33] [AgentSLABench: Evaluating and Benchmarking Agentic Systems Under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00805)
*Meher Bhaskar Madiraju,Meher Sai Preetam Madiraju*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 7 pages, 2 figures, 9 tables. Code, sealed test sets, and profiling artifacts available at: https://github.com/MeherBhaskar/agentslabench

Selection reason: 资源约束下多维agent评测框架，正确率与延迟/成本联合评估，对coding agent实用性评测有直接启发。

TL;DR: AgentSLABench是一个资源感知的AI智能体评估框架，在衡量正确性的同时，综合考虑延迟、成本、计算、内存和网络使用，并通过效率调整成功率（EASR）揭示高准确率在资源受限下的不可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅报告准确率，未考虑资源消耗，导致高准确率模型在实际生产环境中因成本或资源超限而不可用。需要像系统性能分析器一样，将资源维度与任务正确性结合，进行多维评估。

Method: 构建包含16个任务环境（6个类别）的框架，使用隔离Docker容器、声明资源预算、SHA256密封测试集和标准化分析协议；对5个通用基线和4个任务专用智能体进行性能分析，并提出效率调整成功率（EASR），即成功率按相对声明预算的资源消耗加权。

Result: 任务专用智能体在3/5核心任务上达到100%成功率，在retail和code_gen上为66.7-83.3%；通用基线在4/5领域任务上完全失败。EASR表明高准确率在无界成本下不可行。

Conclusion: AgentSLABench提供了可复现的、资源感知的智能体评估基础设施，强调了生产环境中资源效率的重要性，并推动评估范式从单一准确率转向多维资源感知。

中文摘要：我们提出了AgentSLABench，一个面向自主AI智能体的资源感知评估框架，它在声明的资源预算下，除了衡量正确性之外，还衡量延迟、成本、计算、内存和网络使用情况。与仅报告准确率的标准基准不同，AgentSLABench为每个智能体在每项任务上生成多维画像——就像系统性能分析器（perf、pprof、cProfile）衡量代码的资源消耗一样，但将任务正确性作为第一维度扩展进来。AgentSLABench提供6个类别（5个核心：多跳问答、零售替代、代码生成、网页购物、旅行规划；11个扩展）共16个任务环境，具有隔离的Docker容器、声明的CPU/内存/时间/网络预算、带有SHA256哈希的密封测试集，以及标准化的性能分析协议。我们对5个通用基线智能体（ReAct、PlanAndSolve、Reflexion、CoT、Random）和4个任务专用智能体进行了性能分析，发现专用智能体在5个核心任务中的3个（fact_qa、web_shopping、travel_planning）上达到100%的成功率，在retail和code_gen上达到66.7-83.3%，而通用基线在5个领域任务中的4个上完全失败。至关重要的是，我们报告了效率调整成功率（EASR）——相对于声明预算的资源消耗加权的成功率——表明在无界成本下的高准确率在生产环境中不可行。我们发布了完整的基础设施、密封测试集和性能分析结果，以实现可复现的、资源感知的智能体评估。

Original abstract: We present AgentSLABench, a resource-aware evaluation framework for autonomous AI agents that measures correctness alongside latency, cost, compute, memory, and network usage under declared resource budgets. Unlike standard benchmarks that report only accuracy, AgentSLABench produces a multi-dimensional profile per agent per task - the same way systems profilers (perf, pprof, cProfile) measure resource consumption of code, but extended with task correctness as a first-class dimension. AgentSLABench provides 16 task environments across 6 categories (5 core: multi-hop QA, retail substitution, code generation, web shopping, travel planning; 11 extended) with isolated Docker containers, declared CPU/memory/time/network budgets, sealed test sets with SHA256 hashes, and a standardized profiling protocol. We profile 5 general-purpose baseline agents (ReAct, PlanAndSolve, Reflexion, CoT, Random) plus 4 task-specialized agents, finding that specialized agents achieve 100% success on 3/5 core tasks (fact_qa, web_shopping, travel_planning) and 66.7-83.3% on retail and code_gen, while general baselines fail entirely on 4/5 domain tasks. Crucially, we report the Efficiency-Adjusted Success Rate (EASR) - success weighted by resource consumption relative to declared budgets - revealing that high accuracy at unbounded cost is not production-viable. We release the full infrastructure, sealed test sets, and profiling results to enable reproducible, resource-aware agent evaluation.

</details>


### [34] [Large language models improve physician accuracy but lead to false reliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00817)
*Tirtha Chanda,Christoph Wies,Franziska Schramm,Carina Nogueira Garcia,Nicolas B. Merl,Martin J. Hetz,Jochen S. Utikal,Phillip Tschandl,Cristian Navarrete-Dechent,Alexander Thiem,Jakob N. Kather,Consortium,Titus J. Brinker*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: agentic RAG在医生决策中的实证发现，引用证据既提升正确采纳也加重错误依赖，对agent输出可靠性设计有可迁移结论。

TL;DR: 检索增强LLM（CORA）能提高医生诊断准确率，但引用支持的错误答案会显著降低医生抵制，带来安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究检索增强LLM提供的来源链接证据是否真正引导医生决策，还是可能扭曲其判断，尤其是对错误建议的抵抗能力。

Method: 开发CORA智能体检索增强LLM，在基准测试和时序泛化测试中评估性能，并设计46名医生的对照研究，比较无辅助与使用CORA时的准确率及引用对采纳/抵制行为的影响。

Result: CORA将医生准确率从70.8%提升至82.6%；支持性引用使正确建议采纳率从34%升至76.9%，但错误建议的抵制率从92%骤降至34.8%。

Conclusion: 来源链接的LLM辅助可显著改善医生决策，但需警惕引用导致的过度信任，其构成一种依赖grounding的安全风险。

中文摘要：检索增强的大型语言模型（LLM）承诺提供带有来源链接的临床支持，但其价值取决于所展示的证据是引导而非扭曲医生的依赖。我们开发了CORA，一个智能体检索增强LLM，以研究来源链接的辅助如何影响医生的决策。CORA保持了基准性能，并在模型训练数据截止日期之后发表的病例上取得了更大的收益。在一项涉及46名医生的研究中，准确率从无辅助的70.8%提高到使用CORA的82.6%。支持性引用预测了正确答案（87.7%对65.5%），但引用产生了一个重要的不对称性：感知到的支持将正确建议的采纳率从34%提高到76.9%，但当错误的LLM答案得到引用支持时，医生的抵制率从92%下降到34.8%。这些发现表明，来源链接的LLM辅助可以提高医生的准确性，同时引入一种基于基础（grounding）的安全风险。

Original abstract: Retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs) promise source-linked clinical support, but their value depends on whether displayed evidence guides rather than distorts physician reliance. We developed CORA, an agentic retrieval-augmented LLM, to investigate how source-linked assistance affects physician decision-making. CORA maintained benchmark performance and achieved larger gains on cases published after the models' training-data cutoffs. In a study of 46 physicians, accuracy increased from 70.8% unaided to 82.6% with CORA. Supporting citations predicted correct answers (87.7% vs 65.5%), but citations created an important asymmetry: perceived support increased adoption of correct advice from 34% to 76.9% but when an incorrect LLM answer appeared citation-supported, physician resistance to it fell from 92% to 34.8%. These findings show that source-linked LLM assistance can improve physician accuracy while introducing a grounding-dependent safety risk.

</details>


### [35] [CADIR: A Cross-Backend Editable Intermediate Representation for Agentic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00891)
*Yu Liu,Jingzhe Ni,Yiming Chen,Junqi Huang,Ruofeng Tong,Min Tang,Peng Du*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出agent友好的可执行中间表示与构造图检索，支持生成agent利用历史模型，可迁移到程序生成agent的表示与检索设计。

TL;DR: 提出CADIR，一种基于OCCT的可执行中间表示，通过构建图记录建模过程和几何签名匹配实现跨后端编辑，支持图检索并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD生成方法要么是后端特定的顺序脚本（隐式依赖），要么是静态几何，难以同时保留构建历史、稳定拓扑引用和跨系统的特征级可编辑性。

Method: 构建CADIR表示，基于OCCT/OCP提供显式建模操作；通过执行时构建图记录操作、参数、约束和拓扑；提出几何签名匹配实现跨后端对应；并提出构建图检索方法（全图和子图）支持文本和图像查询。

Result: 实验表明CADIR在几何保真度和执行可靠性上优于现有表示；构建图检索提升生成质量；跨后端编辑在多个CAD环境中实现可靠重建和编辑。

Conclusion: CADIR为CAD生成和跨后端编辑提供了有效的可执行中间表示，支持构建历史保留和特征级可编辑性，具有良好的应用前景。

中文摘要：大型语言模型使得从自然语言描述或图像生成可执行的计算机辅助设计（CAD）程序成为可能。然而，现有方法将建模过程表示为具有隐式依赖关系的后端特定顺序脚本或静态几何，这使得在不同CAD系统之间难以同时保留构建历史、稳定的拓扑引用和特征级可编辑性。我们提出了CADIR，一种面向智能体的可执行中间表示，用于CAD生成和跨后端编辑。CADIR基于OCCT几何内核（通过OCP），提供显式、组合式的建模操作和细粒度的执行诊断。在程序执行过程中，CADIR在构建图中记录建模操作、参数依赖、约束和拓扑选择。为了实现可靠的跨后端重建，我们引入了几何签名匹配（Geometric Signature Matching），该算法在参数变化和后端差异的情况下识别对应的边和面，使适配器能够在FreeCAD、SolidWorks和Fusion 360中重建原生可编辑的特征历史。基于这一表示，我们进一步提出了一种用于文本和图像查询的构建图检索方法，支持全图检索和子图检索，使智能体能够利用完整模型和建模子结构。大量实验表明，CADIR相比现有CAD表示实现了更高的几何保真度和执行可靠性，构建图检索进一步提高了模型生成质量，并且跨后端编辑在多种CAD环境中实现了可靠的模型重建和重建后编辑。

Original abstract: Large language models have made it possible to generate executable computer-aided design (CAD) programs from natural-language descriptions or images. However, existing methods represent modeling processes as backend-specific sequential scripts with implicit dependencies or as static geometry, making it difficult to simultaneously preserve construction history, stable topological references, and feature-level editability across different CAD systems. We present CADIR, an agent-friendly executable intermediate representation for CAD generation and cross-backend editing. Built on the OCCT geometry kernel via OCP, CADIR provides explicit, compositional modeling operations and fine-grained execution diagnostics. During program execution, CADIR records modeling operations, parameter dependencies, constraints, and topology selections in a construction graph. To enable reliable cross-backend reconstruction, we introduce Geometric Signature Matching, which identifies corresponding edges and faces despite parameter changes and backend differences, allowing adapters to reconstruct native editable feature histories in FreeCAD, SolidWorks, and Fusion 360. Building on this representation, we further propose a construction-graph retrieval method for text and image queries that supports both full-graph and subgraph retrieval, enabling agents to leverage complete models and modeling substructures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CADIR achieves higher geometric fidelity and execution reliability than existing CAD representations, that construction-graph retrieval further improves model generation quality, and that cross-backend editing enables reliable model reconstruction and post-reconstruction editing across multiple CAD environments.

</details>


### [36] [PMMC: Prospective Multimodal Memory Compilation for Long-Term LVLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00962)
*Jingyu Sun,Yan Lin,Yuyang Xue,Yifan Wang,Zhengtao Yao,Rui Qian,Zefeng Xu,Jiachen Li,Xianyang Liu,Jiancheng Pan,Jingyuan Sun,Syed Murtuza Baker,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出前瞻式多模态记忆编译，将记忆推理转移到整合阶段并减少查询开销，对长程agent记忆设计有直接可迁移性。

TL;DR: 提出前瞻性多模态记忆编译框架，将记忆推理从查询时转移到记忆整合时，通过提问者、规划者和怀疑者生成验证过的问题-程序库，提升LVLM长期记忆的答案质量和视觉证据召回，同时降低查询延迟和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM智能体记忆系统依赖文本摘要或静态检索-推理，难以处理图像-文本绑定、时间更新和视觉细节，且查询时低效脆弱。

Method: 采用Questioner预测问题候选，Planner编译条件多模态记忆程序，Doubter验证证据路径，形成结构化问题库以支持高效查询路由和证据检索。

Result: 在多模态长期记忆基准上，方法提升答案质量和视觉证据召回，减少查询时token和延迟成本；消融验证了各组件贡献。

Conclusion: 将推理前移至记忆整合阶段可显著提升长期记忆性能与效率，为LVLM智能体提供了更鲁棒的记忆方案。

中文摘要：长期记忆对于LVLM智能体在扩展的多模态交互中保持一致性并整合信息至关重要。然而，现有的智能体记忆系统通常将视觉体验简化为文本摘要，或依赖静态的检索-然后-推理流水线，这在查询时效率低下，并且在问题需要图像-文本绑定、时间更新或视觉细节时显得脆弱。我们提出了前瞻性多模态记忆编译（Prospective Multimodal Memory Compilation），一个将部分记忆推理过程从查询时间转移到记忆整合时间的框架。给定累积的多模态交互，一个提问者（Questioner）预测未来的问题候选，一个规划者（Planner）编译基于问题的多模态记忆程序，一个怀疑者（Doubter）验证规划的证据路径是否能支持预测的答案。经过验证的问题-程序对形成一个结构化问题库，用于高效的查询时路由和证据检索。在多模态长期记忆基准上的实验表明，我们的方法提高了答案质量和视觉证据召回率，同时降低了查询时的令牌和延迟成本。大量的消融实验分析了自我反馈、动态规划、原始图像访问和问题库覆盖率的影响。

Original abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LVLM agents to maintain consistency and integrate information across extended multimodal interactions. Existing agent memory systems, however, often reduce visual experiences into textual summaries or rely on static retrieve-then-reason pipelines, which are inefficient at query time and brittle when questions require image-text binding, temporal updates, or visual details. We propose Prospective Multimodal Memory Compilation, a framework that shifts part of the memory reasoning process from query time to memory consolidation time. Given accumulated multimodal interactions, a Questioner predicts future question candidates, a Planner compiles question-conditioned multimodal memory programs, and a Doubter verifies whether the planned evidence path can support the predicted answer. The verified question-program pairs form a structured question bank for efficient query-time routing and evidence retrieval. Experiments on multimodal long-term memory benchmarks show that our method improves answer quality and visual evidence recall while reducing query-time token and latency costs. Extensive ablations analyze the effects of self-feedback, dynamic planning, raw-image access, and question bank coverage.

</details>


### [37] [TrajWiki: Source-Grounded Memory Trajectories for Long-Horizon Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00967)
*Jingyu Sun,Yuyang Xue,Mingyang Li,Zhengtao Yao,Jiachen Li,Yang Cui,Wenhao Cai,Haozhe Liu,Fangying Wang,Magdalene Katharina Montgomery,Syed Murtuza Baker,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 轨迹式源溯记忆与wiki化分层检索，解决长期对话agent记忆可追溯与更新问题，对coding agent经验管理有借鉴价值。

TL;DR: TrajWiki提出基于轨迹的外部记忆框架，通过不可变快照和声明级操作管理记忆演变，并利用Wiki中间层实现结构化检索，在长时程对话任务上提升性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强智能体将记忆存储为孤立记录或可覆盖状态，缺乏可追踪、可更新和诊断透明的外部记忆，难以处理信息起源、演变、冲突和过时问题。

Method: TrajWiki将每条记忆表示为源扎根的演变轨迹，采用不可变episodic快照和ADD/REVISE/DEPRECATE声明操作；引入Memory Wiki中间层增量编译对话历史为结构化、互链的wiki页面；推理时分层路由查询从wiki页到记忆轨迹、快照和源消息。

Result: 在LoCoMo和MedMT数据集上，TrajWiki在开源和闭源LLM骨架上均提高了长时程对话性能，并提供更强的可解释性和诊断可见性。

Conclusion: TrajWiki通过轨迹式记忆和Wiki中间层有效改善了长时程对话的记忆管理，验证了其在性能与可解释性上的优势。

中文摘要：大型语言模型智能体在生成连贯且上下文适当的响应方面表现出强大的能力，但稳健的长时程对话仍然受到缺乏可追踪、可更新且诊断透明的外部记忆的限制。现有的记忆增强智能体通常将记忆存储为孤立记录或可覆盖状态，这使得难以保留信息如何随时间起源、演变、冲突或过时。我们提出TrajWiki，一种用于长时程对话智能体的基于轨迹的记忆框架。TrajWiki不是将记忆视为静态条目，而是将每条记忆表示为一个源扎根的演变轨迹，通过不可变的 episodic 快照和声明级操作（如ADD、REVISE和DEPRECATE）来维护。为了减少碎片化和检索成本，TrajWiki进一步引入Memory Wiki，一个持久化的中间层，将对话历史增量编译成结构化且相互关联的wiki页面，捕获重要实体、事件、数量、主题和冲突。在推理时，查询从相关wiki页面分层路由到链接的记忆轨迹，然后到相应的快照和源消息，以进行基于证据的答案合成。在LoCoMo和MedMT上的实验表明，TrajWiki在开源和闭源LLM骨干上均改善了长时程对话性能，同时提供了对记忆演变、检索失败和答案生成的更大可解释性和诊断可见性。

Original abstract: Large language model agents have shown strong capabilities in generating coherent and contextually appropriate responses, yet robust long-horizon dialogue remains limited by the lack of external memory that is traceable, updatable, and diagnostically transparent. Existing memory-augmented agents often store memories as isolated records or overwritable states, making it difficult to preserve how information originates, evolves, conflicts, or becomes obsolete over time. We propose TrajWiki, a trajectory-based memory framework for long-horizon conversational agents. Instead of treating memory as static entries, TrajWiki represents each memory as a source-grounded evolution trajectory, maintained through immutable episodic snapshots and claim-level operations such as ADD, REVISE, and DEPRECATE. To reduce fragmentation and retrieval cost, TrajWiki further introduces Memory Wiki, a persistent intermediate layer that incrementally compiles dialogue history into structured and interlinked wiki pages capturing salient entities, events, quantities, topics, and conflicts. At inference time, queries are routed hierarchically from relevant wiki pages to linked memory trajectories, then to corresponding snapshots and source messages for evidence-grounded answer synthesis. Experiments on LoCoMo and MedMT show that TrajWiki improves long-horizon dialogue performance across both open-source and closed-source LLM backbones, while providing greater interpretability and diagnostic visibility into memory evolution, retrieval failures, and answer generation.

</details>


### [38] [PROGRESS: Coverage-guided RL to Train Search-augmented LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00969)
*Sudipta Paul,Vijay Srinivasan,Vivek Kulkarni,Aounon Kumar,Yashas Malur Saidutta,Wenbo Li,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted in Interspeech 2026

Selection reason: 用教师覆盖奖励显式监督搜索agent的查询分解，弥补稀疏奖励，该训练机制可迁移到代码检索agent。

TL;DR: 提出PROGRESS，通过教师引导的覆盖奖励在R1风格训练中显式监督查询分解，提升搜索增强LLM智能体的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索增强LLM智能体主要依赖结果级奖励，缺乏对搜索行为的过程监督，且未考虑智能体分解复杂查询的能力。

Method: 利用冻结的教师模型将复杂查询分解为必要搜索查询，并以此计算覆盖奖励，指导策略模型的查询分解生成，集成到R1风格训练框架中。

Result: 覆盖引导的强化学习提高了整体任务性能。

Conclusion: 显式监督搜索策略（尤其是查询分解）对智能体LLM至关重要，PROGRESS提供了一种轻量级且有效的方案。

中文摘要：现有的搜索增强型LLM智能体使用强化学习进行训练，以提升其推理能力。然而，这些方法主要依赖于结果级奖励，这对搜索行为提供的监督很少，并且忽视了智能体适当分解复杂查询的能力。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了PROGRESS，它利用教师引导的覆盖奖励来显式地塑造策略模型的分解查询生成。在训练期间，冻结的教师模型被用来将复杂查询分解为必要的搜索查询。这些必要的搜索查询被用来指导策略模型的搜索行为。集成到R1风格的训练框架中，我们的方法在不进行密集的过程级监督的情况下，为查询分解决策提供了轻量级指导。实验表明，覆盖引导的强化学习提高了整体任务性能，强调了在智能体LLM中显式监督搜索策略的重要性。

Original abstract: Existing search-augmented LLM agents are trained using Reinforcement Learning to boost its reasoning capabilities. However, these approaches primarily rely on outcome-level rewards, which provide little supervision over search behavior and overlook agent's ability to decompose complex queries properly. To mitigate this issue, we propose PROGRESS which utilizes teacher-guided coverage reward to explicitly shape decomposed query generation of the policy model. During training, frozen teacher models are used to decompose complex queries into essential search queries. These essential search queries are utilized to guide the search behavior of the policy model. Integrated into an R1-style training framework, our approach provides lightweight guidance over query decomposition decisions without dense process-level supervision. Experiments show that coverage-guided RL improves overall task performance, highlighting the importance of explicitly supervising search strategies in agentic LLMs.

</details>


### [39] [Search-GRT: Guided Retrieval Training of Search Agents to Optimize for Complex Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00974)
*Aounon Kumar,Sudipta Paul,Vivek Kulkarni,Vijay Srinivasan,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at Interspeech 2026

Selection reason: 通过ground truth约束检索路径增强RL训练信号，改进搜索agent多跳问答，训练策略对search-augmented coding agent有启发。

TL;DR: GRT是一种通过利用黄金文档限制RL训练检索过程的方法，有效缓解稀疏奖励，显著提升多跳问答性能（超40%），并提高训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在复杂多跳问答中使用搜索引擎时存在检索级联错误，而RL训练面临稀疏奖励导致学习困难。

Method: 在RL训练期间使用地面真值信息引导检索过程，只关注相关文档子集，为模型提供更强的学习信号。

Result: 在多个QA任务上一致优于Search-R1，多跳问答性能提升超40%，且更少训练步骤达到更好性能。

Conclusion: GRT有效缓解稀疏奖励，提升搜索代理的推理与综合能力，同时提高训练效率，优于现有方法。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）对搜索引擎的有效使用仍然是一个重大挑战，尤其是在复杂的多跳问答（MHQA）任务中。这些任务要求模型将问题分解为子查询、检索相关信息，并从多个来源综合答案，由于早期阶段的检索不佳，常常导致级联错误。强化学习（RL）在提升LLM的搜索能力方面显示出前景，但它通常在训练期间面临奖励稀疏的问题，阻碍了模型的有效学习。为应对这些挑战，我们引入了引导式检索训练（GRT），一种新颖的方法，通过利用地面真值信息限制RL训练期间的检索过程，来提升搜索代理的性能。通过聚焦于一组精选的相关文档，GRT为模型提供了更强的学习信号，缓解了奖励稀疏的问题，并提高了其生成准确子查询和综合正确答案的能力。我们的实验结果表明，GRT在广泛的问答（QA）任务中，相较于现有方法（如Search-R1）实现了持续的性能改进。值得注意的是，GRT在多跳问答任务中表现出色，实现了超过40%的性能提升。此外，GRT通过用更少的训练步骤获得更好的QA性能，提高了训练效率。

Original abstract: The effective use of search engines by large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge, particularly in complex, multi-hop question-answering (MHQA) tasks. These tasks require the model to decompose questions into subqueries, retrieve relevant information, and synthesize answers from multiple sources, often leading to cascading errors due to poor retrieval in early stages. Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise in improving LLMs' search capabilities, but it often suffers from sparse rewards during training, hindering the model's ability to learn effectively. To address these challenges, we introduce Guided Retrieval Training (GRT), a novel method that improves the performance of a search agent by restricting the retrieval process during RL training using ground truth information. By focusing on a curated set of relevant documents, GRT provides the model with a stronger learning signal, mitigating the problem of sparse rewards and improving its ability to generate accurate subqueries and synthesize correct answers. Our experimental results demonstrate that GRT achieves consistent performance improvements over existing methods, such as Search-R1, across a wide range of question-answering (QA) tasks. Notably, GRT excels in MHQA tasks, achieving over 40% improvements in performance. Additionally, GRT enhances training efficiency by achieving better QA performance with fewer training steps.

</details>


### [40] [Auditing Discovery Claims: A Two-Sided Criterion for Agentic Science, with the Negative Side Decidable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00981)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 51 pages, 10 figures, 10 tables

Selection reason: 提出双端审计标准区分真实能力增益与搜索/验证器膨胀，对agent评测可靠性与防作弊有通用方法论价值。

TL;DR: 提出了一个双面审计，通过形式化约束和外部评判者来区分自我改进AI系统的真实能力与oracle利用，揭示单个易错oracle可大幅夸大声明，智能体编写的程序在低计算下胜出，但机制不可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于基准差异、描述长度门槛或p值的证据无法区分真正进展与额外搜索、验证器更改或对易错oracle的适应，需要更严格的审计方法。

Method: 构建双面审计：利用无假结oracle的形式化限制界定验证器范围，并在RNA结构预测任务上测试虚构算子与智能体编写的程序，同时引入外部裁决者进行对照。

Result: 单个oracle可将成功率从0/60提升至43/60（但三个预测器下仅剩1/60）；智能体程序在外部评判下达到0.293对比0.095，且oracle调用减少4.6-10倍；但七个候选机制均无法转移统计量，自我审计也显示头条效应84%源于随机序列已解决的靶标。

Conclusion: 没有外部裁决者和形式化边界，基于oracle的自我改进声明不可靠；作者自身系统也未能通过更严格测试，凸显了独立验证和机制可转移性的必要性。

中文摘要：当一个人工智能驱动的科学自我改进系统宣称新能力时，证据通常是一个基准差异、一段描述长度门槛或一个p值。这些都未能将真正的收益与额外搜索、更改的验证器或对易错oracle的适应区分开来。我们构建了一个双面审计，其负面是一个形式化事实：一个无假结（pseudoknot-free）的oracle在形式上无法表示交叉碱基对，因此先验验证器的范围在任何运行之前就被精确且有界地确定。“新”是相对于智能体先前的自我，而非基础模型。首先，单个易错oracle能将能力声明夸大多少。一个虚构的、无求解器的算子在其优化的预测器下解决了43/60个交叉RNA靶标，高于上下文无关的0/60基线；在三个预测器下，只有1/60存活。在相同的43个靶标上配对，该算子从未见过的预测器确认了其2个设计，而最小自由能求解器则确认了26个（p = 8e-7）。从系统及其自身oracle计算的任何统计量都看不到这一差距。其次，智能体编写的程序可以在没有任何目标能够奉承的评判者下击败人类编写的程序，并且只消耗一小部分计算。在六个前沿模型中，两个没有超时运行的算子以0.293对比0.095（n = 951个配对单元，靶标聚类 [+0.108, +0.297]，p = 5e-5）实现迁移，同时oracle调用次数减少4.6-10倍。三个阶梯：在外部裁决者下的差异（达到），不是用计算购买的（达到，两个方向），机制被识别并可转移（未达到；测试了七个候选，没有一个能移动统计量）。上限是评审团本身：其三个预测器共享最近邻热力学参数，其中两个在kappa = 0.673处一致。审计同样毫不留情地对待我们自己的系统：匹配的无向搜索恰好为零，而无搜索探针将我们头条效应的84%放在随机序列已经解决的靶标上。

Original abstract: When a self-improving AI-for-science system claims a new capability, the evidence is usually a benchmark delta, a description-length gate, or a p-value. None separates a real gain from extra search, from a changed verifier, or from adaptation to a fallible oracle. We build a two-sided audit whose negative side is a formal fact: a pseudoknot-free oracle provably cannot represent a crossing base pair, so the prior verifier's range is bounded exactly, offline, before any run. "New" is relative to the agent's prior self, never to the base model. First, how far a single fallible oracle can inflate a capability claim. An invented, solver-free operator solves 43/60 crossing RNA targets under the predictor it optimizes, above a context-free floor of 0/60; under three predictors, 1/60 survives. Paired on the same 43 targets, a predictor the operator never saw confirms 2 of its designs against 26 for a minimum-free-energy solver (p = 8e-7). No statistic computed from the system and its own oracle sees that gap. Second, agent-written procedures can beat a human-written one under a judge no objective can flatter, at a fraction of the compute. Of six frontier models, the two whose operators ran without timeouts carry over at 0.293 against our 0.095 (n = 951 paired units, target-clustered [+0.108, +0.297], p = 5e-5) while spending 4.6-10x fewer oracle calls. Three rungs: difference under an outside adjudicator (reached), not bought with compute (reached, both directions), mechanism identified and transferable (not reached; seven candidates tested, none moves the statistic). The ceiling is the panel itself: its three predictors share nearest-neighbour thermodynamic parameters, two agreeing at kappa = 0.673. The audit is as unsparing about our own system: matched undirected search is an exact zero, and a search-free probe puts 84% of our headline effect on targets a random sequence already solves.

</details>


### [41] [SCHEDBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Constraint Faithfulness in Natural-Language Combinatorial Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00991)
*Shrenil Shaun Sharma,Avi Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 19 Pages

Selection reason: 以调度问题评测LLM约束忠实度与表面形式敏感性，其benchmark构造和硬约束违反分析可迁移到代码生成验证。

TL;DR: SCHEDBench是一个新基准，测试LLM在不同自然语言表述下是否保持调度约束的可行性，发现模型对语义等价改写不稳定，尤其是约束重排序影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估未系统检验LLM在表面形式变化下对调度约束的忠实性，而实际应用中用户可能以不同方式描述同一调度问题，需要量化模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建包含1,132个实例的基准，涵盖JSP、RCPSP、护士排班和课程时间表，通过模板化生成多种表面形式，并用求解器验证参考解；评测13个LLM的约束可行性保持情况。

Result: 模型对语义等价的调度问题表述不具备可靠的不变性；表面形式变化降低可行性，并导致硬约束违反显著偏移，其中约束重排序是最敏感的因素。

Conclusion: LLM在调度约束理解上仍易受表面形式干扰，需改进其语义鲁棒性；基准为后续评估和训练提供了基础。

中文摘要：本文介绍了SCHEDBench，一个用于评估组合调度约束在表面形式变化下的忠实性的自然语言基准。基于规范的调度实例和求解器导出的可行性与最优性，SCHEDBench评估大型语言模型（LLMs）是否能在不同的自然语言（NL）表面形式下生成具有相同约束可行行为的调度方案。SCHEDBench涵盖1,132个实例，涉及作业车间调度问题（JSP）、单模式和多种模式资源受限项目调度问题（RCPSP）、护士排班/调度以及难度各异的课程时间表问题。实例通过领域特定模板、主题化实体、词汇句法模板改写和约束级表面形式变化被模板化为自然语言问题，参考解经过可行性和目标最优性验证。在十三个前沿和开放权重LLM中，我们发现模型对于同一调度问题的语义等价表述并不可靠地保持一致。表面形式变化降低了可行性，并在匹配实例上引发了每个实例硬约束违反的超出噪声的偏移。在测试的独立轴中，约束重排序产生了最明显的超噪声敏感性。

Original abstract: This paper introduces SCHEDBench, a natural-language benchmark for evaluating combinatorial scheduling constraint faithfulness under surface-form variation. Grounded in canonical scheduling instances and solver-derived feasibility and optimality, SCHEDBench assesses whether large language models (LLMs) generate schedules with the same constraint-feasible behavior across varied natural-language (NL) surface forms. SCHEDBench spans 1,132 instances across job-shop scheduling problems (JSP), single and multi-mode resource-constrained project scheduling problems (RCPSP), nurse rostering/scheduling, and curriculum timetabling problems of varying difficulty. Instances are templated into natural language problems using domain-specific templates, themed entities, lexical-syntactic template rephrasing, and constraint-level surface-form variation, with reference solutions verified for feasibility and objective optimality. Across thirteen frontier and open-weight LLMs, we find that models are not reliably invariant to semantically equivalent renderings of the same scheduling problem. Surface-form variation reduces feasibility and induces above-noise shifts in per-instance hard-constraint violations on matched instances. Among the tested isolated axes, constraint reordering yields the clearest above-noise sensitivity.

</details>


### [42] [Judging Is Not Enumerating: Silent Omissions in LLM-Authored Acceptable Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01000)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Peiji Long,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 53 pages, 14 figures, 26 tables

Selection reason: 揭示LLM编写测试套件/验证器时遗漏支配错误且难以审计，直接影响LLM作为verifier的coding agent可靠性。

TL;DR: 研究发现语言模型作为考官时，其生成测试套件的能力远低于判断能力，主要缺陷是遗漏且难以审计，但通过参考执行修复可大幅提升产出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型被用来为其他系统定义正确性（如编写测试和奖励函数），但该能力未经充分测试，且常用协议缺乏测试时推理。

Method: 在四种参考构建（完整有限真值、可执行代码HumanEval+/MBPP+、词汇WordNet）上，对比模型生成集合与判断成员的能力，并设计对照实验（输出谓词vs外延），以及通过探针门控和参考执行修复验证器。

Result: 模型判断F1远高于生成F1；生成套件仅接受19-42%的Oracle正确解；失败源于遗漏错误；检测过度包含比遗漏高6-7倍；门控降低假拒绝但损失大量套件；修复使产出提升3.3-10.6倍。

Conclusion: 模型不是缺少知识或规范能力，而是无法将规范具体化为集合；建议用参考执行修复生成的验证器，而非直接门控。

中文摘要：语言模型越来越多地从考生晋升为考官：它们编写测试套件、答案键、评分标准和奖励函数，这些定义了其他系统的正确性。我们衡量了这一角色所承担的能力，并发现在该角色通常部署的协议下（一次性贪婪生成，无测试时推理）表现不足。在四个参考构建中——两个具有完整有限真值，一个具有强化的可执行参考（HumanEval+/MBPP+），一个具有明确不完整的词汇参考（WordNet）——模型判断候选是否属于（集合）的能力远好于它们自己生成集合。在不完备性证明的算法构建上，差距在24倍参数范围内为+0.34到+0.29 F1，且没有缩小；在可执行代码上，模型判断的F1为0.74-0.90，但生成的套件仅接受19-42%的Oracle正确解。一个对照实验定位了缺陷：当被要求输出谓词而不是其外延（集合）时，同样的模型能达到约0.99的F1。失败不是缺乏知识或无法指定，而是无法具体化规范所引发的区域。主要错误是遗漏，这抵抗审计：过度包含是一个审阅者可以质疑的标记，而缺失成员是一种缺失，其发现本身就是生成问题。模型检测植入的过度包含的频率比植入的遗漏高6-7倍，一个包含43,227项的生产部署以10:1的比例首先出现遗漏故障。接入RLVR后，生成的键相对于精确Oracle损失1.9个准确率点，相对于WordNet损失18.5个点（六对种子，p=0.031）。将生成的验证器门控在已知正确的探针上，可将假拒绝从58-92%降至最多5%，但仅保留5-39%的套件。相反，通过将每个错误期望值重写为参考执行返回的值来修复它们，在四个作者族中使产出提高了3.3-10.6倍。

Original abstract: Language models are increasingly promoted from examinees to examiners: they write the test suites, answer keys, rubrics, and reward functions that define correctness for other systems. We measure the capability that role assumes and find it lacking under the protocol the role is usually deployed with, one-shot greedy authoring with no test-time reasoning. Across four reference constructions - two with complete finite truth, one with a hardened executable reference (HumanEval+/MBPP+), one with an explicitly incomplete lexical reference (WordNet) - models judge whether a candidate belongs far better than they author the set itself. On the incompleteness-proof algorithmic construction the gap is +0.34 to +0.29 F1 over a 24x parameter range and does not close; on executable code, models judging at F1 0.74-0.90 author suites admitting only 19-42% of oracle-correct solutions. A control locates the deficit: asked to emit the predicate rather than its extension, the same models reach F1 about 0.99. The failure is not missing knowledge or an inability to specify, but an inability to materialise the region a specification induces. The dominant error is omission, which resists audit: an over-inclusion is a token a reviewer can challenge, a missing member an absence whose discovery is the authoring problem itself. Models detect planted over-inclusions 6-7x more often than planted omissions, and a production deployment of 43,227 items fails omission-first at 10:1. Wired into RLVR, an authored key costs 1.9 points of accuracy against an exact oracle and 18.5 WordNet-relative (six paired seeds, p=0.031). Gating authored verifiers on a known-correct probe cuts false rejection from 58-92% to at most 5%, but keeps only 5-39% of suites. Repairing them instead, by rewriting each wrong expected value to what a reference execution returns, raises yield 3.3-10.6x across four author families.

</details>


### [43] [Control Under Compression: Reliability Frontiers for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01056)
*Yinghan Hou,Zongyou Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures; includes an appendix

Selection reason: 系统研究压缩agent控制上下文对工具使用可靠性的影响，提供环境验证benchmark，对coding agent提示压缩与可靠性权衡有重要借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出CompressAgent基准，发现代理控制上下文压缩的可靠性随保留预算呈非线性变化，低预算下各方法表现差异巨大，且不同上下文间可靠性差异显著，因此应视为运行时可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示压缩评估只关注令牌减少或文本相似性，未验证压缩后的代理控制上下文是否仍能可靠地指导工具使用代理执行任务，导致实际操作中可能因压缩而出现功能失效。

Method: 构建CompressAgent，一个环境验证的基准，包含9个独立构建的ACC、3个任务族、3个固定的Qwen API模型、6个保留上下文预算和15,525次运行，系统评估多种压缩方法（如通用重写、基于部分压缩、义务感知压缩）在不同压缩率下的任务成功率。

Result: 在75%保留率下，压缩接近全上下文基线（约92-93%）；在50%-35%区间方法性能急剧分化（35%时成功率从19.9%到47.0%不等）；25%以下协议变得脆弱；可靠性因ACC而异，失败主要源于工具执行和动作解析错误。

Conclusion: ACC压缩不能仅被视为令牌减少问题，而应作为运行时可靠性问题来对待，需要通过可执行结果（如任务成功率）进行评估，并针对每个特定的ACC进行资格认定，而不是依赖通用的压缩器排名。

中文摘要：使用工具的语言模型代理不仅受任务提示词的支配，还受持久的系统端指令的支配，这些指令指定了工具、参数、策略、执行协议和恢复机制。压缩这些代理控制上下文（ACC）可以降低输入成本和上下文使用量，然而现有的提示压缩评估并未揭示由此产生的控制是否在操作上仍然可靠。我们引入了CompressAgent，这是一个环境验证的基准测试，用于在九个独立构建的ACC、三个任务族、三个固定的Qwen API模型标识符、六个保留上下文预算和15,525次运行中评估ACC压缩。我们发现了一个非线性的、依赖于方法的可靠性前沿。在保留75%上下文时，通用重写和基于部分的压缩分别达到92.7%和92.4%的成功率，接近93.8%的全上下文基线。在50%到35%之间，方法出现显著分化；在35%时，基于部分、义务感知和通用重写分别达到47.0%、39.0%和19.9%。在保留上下文预算从25%到10%时，可执行协议变得脆弱。可靠性在不同ACC之间也差异很大，这使得通用的压缩器排名不恰当，并促使进行逐上下文资格认定。失败分析表明，压缩主要导致工具执行和动作解析错误。这些发现将ACC压缩从令牌减少重新定义为运行时可靠性问题，必须通过可执行结果进行评估。

Original abstract: Tool-using language-model agents are governed not only by task prompts but also by persistent system-side instructions that specify tools, arguments, policies, execution protocols, and recovery. Compressing these agent control contexts (ACCs) can reduce input cost and context use, yet existing prompt-compression evaluations do not reveal whether the resulting control remains operationally reliable. We introduce CompressAgent, an environment-verified benchmark for ACC compression across nine independently constructed ACCs, three task families, three fixed Qwen API model identifiers, six retained-context budgets, and 15,525 runs. We uncover a nonlinear, method-dependent reliability frontier. At 75% retained context, generic rewriting and section-based compression achieve 92.7% and 92.4% success, close to the 93.8% full-context baseline. Between 50% and 35%, methods diverge sharply; at 35%, section-based, obligation-aware, and generic rewriting achieve 47.0%, 39.0%, and 19.9%. At retained-context budgets from 25% to 10%, executable protocols become fragile. Reliability also varies substantially across ACCs, making universal compressor rankings inappropriate and motivating per-context qualification. Failure analysis shows that compression primarily surfaces as tool-execution and action-parsing errors. These findings recast ACC compression from token reduction into a runtime-reliability problem that must be evaluated through executable outcomes.

</details>


### [44] [PATH-Bench: Path-Dependent Evaluation of Lifelong Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01149)
*Xidong Yang,Xingyi Zhang,Wenhao Li,Wenyan Liu,Junjie Sheng,Yun Hua,Wei Yin,Tao Fang,Chuyun Shen,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 构建路径依赖的终身agent基准，测量前向/后向迁移与遗忘，并提出选择性经验使用harness，对coding agent技能复用与记忆选择有直接指导。

TL;DR: 提出了PATH-Bench，用于评估终身LLM智能体的路径依赖学习；发现经验效用受表示方式和交互结构影响，强迁移不保证保留；并提出选择性经验使用（SEU）来减少遗忘并提升前向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身学习基准未考虑累积经验的路径如何影响智能体的迁移和保留，缺乏对路径依赖效应的受控评估。

Method: 构建PATH-Bench，使用多模型上下文学习估计任务间有向关系，生成探针中心序列并结合帮助性和干扰性历史，反复评估探针任务以量化路径依赖；并提出SEU调节经验使用。

Result: 八个智能体在代码生成和工具使用任务上显示：经验效用取决于表示与交互结构，强迁移不保证保留，后到经验可改变早期收益；SEU在多数设置下降低遗忘并提升前向迁移。

Conclusion: PATH-Bench提供了路径依赖评估框架，SEU为设计选择性、鲁棒的终身智能体提供了有效方法。

中文摘要：终身LLM智能体越来越多地通过外部学习状态来适应，这些状态将过去的交互存储为可检索的记忆或可复用的技能，然而现有基准很少考虑累积经验的路径如何影响智能体转移和保留的内容。在这项工作中，我们建立了PATH-Bench，一个用于终身智能体路径依赖评估的基准。PATH-Bench通过多模型上下文学习估计有向任务关系，构建具有受控帮助性和干扰性历史的探针中心序列，并反复评估探针任务以测量平均性能、前向迁移、后向迁移和遗忘。我们在正向主导和负向主导的历史下，评估了八个代表性智能体在单轮代码生成和多轮工具使用任务上的表现。基准结果表明，经验效用共同取决于经验的表示方式和任务的交互结构；强迁移并不能确保保留；后到的经验可以重塑学习路径早期获得的收益。基于这些发现，我们提出了选择性经验使用（SEU），一种智能体控制机制，用于调节路径累积经验如何影响每个新任务，允许有用项目进入同时过滤潜在干扰。SEU在大多数设置中持续减少遗忘，同时改善前向迁移。PATH-Bench既提供了受控评估框架，也为设计更具选择性和鲁棒性的终身智能体提供了可操作的指导。

Original abstract: Lifelong LLM agents increasingly adapt through external learning states that store past interactions as retrievable memories or reusable skills, yet existing benchmarks rarely account for how the path of accumulated experience shapes what agents transfer and retain. In this work, we establish PATH-Bench, a benchmark for path-dependent evaluation of lifelong agents. PATH-Bench estimates directed task relationships via multi-model in-context learning, constructs probe-centered sequences with controlled helpful and interfering histories, and repeatedly evaluates probe tasks to measure average performance, forward transfer, backward transfer, and forgetting. We evaluate eight representative agents on single-turn code generation and multi-turn tool-use tasks under positive- and negative-dominant histories. Benchmark results show that experience utility depends jointly on how experience is represented and on the task's interaction structure, that strong transfer does not ensure retention, and that later experience can reshape gains acquired earlier in the learning path. Based on these findings, we propose Selective Experience Use (SEU), an agent harness that regulates how path-accumulated experience influences each new task, admitting helpful items while filtering out potential interference. SEU consistently reduces forgetting while improving forward transfer in the majority of settings. The PATH-Bench provides both a controlled evaluation framework and actionable guidance for designing more selective and robust lifelong agents.

</details>


### [45] [Humans Are More Diverse: Frontier LLMs Show Extreme Policies in Idealised AI Development Races](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01193)
*Phu Hoa Pham,Duy Minh Dao Sy,Trung Kiet Huynh,Phu Quy Nguyen Lam,Chi Nguyen Tran,Minh Trung Le,Phong Hao Le,Dinh Nam Nguyen,Thien Ky Nguyen Dong,Elias Fernandez Domingos,Le Hong Trang,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 对LLM多智能体竞赛做有效性审计与轨迹级分析，属多智能体评测与可靠性研究，可迁移到agent行为验证。

TL;DR: 对LLM智能体在多玩家AI开发竞赛中的战略安全行为进行审计与比较，发现强规则回忆可能伴随弱状态追踪与收益计算，且聚合结果掩盖模型特异性轨迹差异，强调需有效性检查和轨迹级分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发竞赛构成多智能体安全困境，但现有研究缺乏对LLM智能体是否真正理解博弈的验证，直接解释行为可能误导，因此需要在行为解释前设置审计门。

Method: 基于重复博弈设计两到五名玩家的竞赛，先验证游戏引擎，再测试LLM的规则回忆、状态追踪、收益计算和任务描述稳定性；随后将动作序列与进化博弈论基准和人类数据对比，跨模型、风险条件、人设及玩家数量探索差异。

Result: 审计发现强规则回忆可与弱状态追踪和收益计算共存；提供验证算术或改变响应表示会改变后续动作；七个模型端点的聚合率掩盖了动作序列、对对手和位置反应的巨大差异，且三到五名玩家的模式具有模型特异性。

Conclusion: 多智能体AI竞赛模拟需进行有效性检查和轨迹级分析，才能声称输出具有战略性、类人或安全意识；当前发现仅适用于测试的模型、提示词和解码设置，属探索性结论。

中文摘要：人工智能开发竞赛会引发多智能体安全困境。每家公司可以选择缓慢而安全地开发，或者冒着可能丧失最终回报的风险更快地推进。我们使用这个重复博弈来研究两到五名玩家参与的竞赛中大型语言模型（LLM）智能体的战略安全行为。然而，一个有效的行动并不能证明智能体理解该博弈。因此，我们在行为解释之前设置了一个审计门。我们首先验证博弈引擎，然后测试规则回忆、状态追踪、收益计算以及在等价但不同任务描述下的稳定性。接着，我们将LLM的动作序列与进化博弈论基准和已发表的人类数据进行比较，并探讨不同模型、风险条件、人设以及两到五名玩家竞赛之间的差异。审计表明，强大的规则回忆可能与薄弱的状态追踪和期望收益计算共存。提供经过验证的算术并改变响应表示方式，即使在博弈规则不变的情况下，也可能改变后续动作。在七个测试模型端点中，聚合率掩盖了动作序列、对对手的反应以及对竞赛位置的反应方面的巨大差异。在测试的三到五名玩家竞赛中，模式也是模型特有的，而非添加竞争者的单一效应。这些结果表明，在多智能体AI竞赛模拟中，需要有效性检查和轨迹级分析，才能将其输出描述为战略性、类人的或具有安全意识的。我们的发现是探索性的，仅适用于所测试的模型、提示词和解码设置。

Original abstract: An AI development race creates a multi-agent safety dilemma. Each company can develop slowly and safely, or move faster while taking a risk that may remove its final reward. We use this repeated game to study strategic safety behaviour among large language model (LLM) agents in races with two to five players. However, a valid action does not show that an agent understands the game. We therefore place an audit gate before behavioural interpretation. We first verify the game engine, then test rule recall, state tracking, payoff calculation, and stability under different but equivalent task descriptions. We then compare LLM action sequences with an evolutionary game-theory benchmark and published human data, and explore differences across models, risk conditions, personas, and two- to five-player races. The audit shows that strong rule recall can coexist with weak state tracking and expected-payoff calculation. Providing verified arithmetic and changing the response representation can also change later actions, even when the game rules stay fixed. Across seven tested model endpoints, aggregate rates hide large differences in action sequences, responses to opponents, and responses to race position. Patterns across the tested three- to five-player races are also model-specific rather than a single effect of adding competitors. These results show why multi-agent AI-race simulations need validity checks and trajectory-level analysis before their outputs are described as strategic, human-like, or safety-aware. Our findings are exploratory and apply only to the tested models, prompts, and decoding settings.

</details>


### [46] [CT-PrepAgent: Bounded Policy and Controlled Execution for Adaptive CT Data Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01233)
*Xiaolin Fan,Yue Pei,Yingying Zhang,Haogang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: CT-PrepAgent将可控执行、边界恢复、安全隔离纳入LLM agent工作流，其确定性护栏可迁移到代码优化agent。

TL;DR: 提出CT-PrepAgent，利用LLM策略和受控确定性执行实现自适应CT数据准备，多个任务上提升Dice和产出率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定数据准备流程难以适应异构CT采集和多样任务，现有规则依赖人工干预；LLM代理潜力未充分探索。

Method: 基于LLM的CT-PrepAgent，通过确定性检查构建数据-任务档案，策略选择DICOM序列或预处理配置，受控执行流程进行守卫、解析、执行、验证，具有有界恢复和安全隔离。

Result: 三个公开CT分割任务上取得最高宏平均Dice；两个私有队列中验证输出产出率从61.7%提升到70.0%，常见验证输出配准指标相似；故障和漂移测试验证鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CT-PrepAgent能有效自适应CT数据准备，提升跨任务泛化性和产出率，通过有界恢复和安全隔离增强可靠性。

中文摘要：异构计算机断层扫描（CT）采集和多样的下游任务需求限制了固定数据准备流程在不同数据源和任务之间的可迁移性。现有方法通常依赖手动设计或特定于数据集的规则，难以在没有人工干预的情况下适应采集条件和分析目标的变化。基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体在自动化医疗工作流程方面显示出潜力，但其在自适应CT数据准备方面的潜力尚未得到充分探索。为弥补这一差距，我们提出了CT-PrepAgent，通过有界策略和受控的确定性执行实现自适应CT数据准备。确定性检查构建结构化的数据-任务档案，策略从中决定符合条件DICOM序列或预定义的预处理配置，而受控执行流程则对决策进行守卫、解析、执行和验证，在启用时有界恢复，否则安全隔离。在三个公开CT分割任务中，CT-PrepAgent生成了数据-任务自适应的预处理决策，并取得了最高的宏平均Dice。在两个私有原始DICOM队列中，CT-PrepAgent将验证输出产出率从61.7%提高到70.0%，并在常见验证输出上产生了相似的配准指标。受控故障和重放测试验证了在所测试的故障和漂移设置下有界恢复、安全隔离和无策略重放。

Original abstract: Heterogeneous computed tomography (CT) acquisitions and diverse downstream task requirements limit the transferability of fixed data preparation workflows across data sources and tasks. Existing approaches typically rely on manually designed or dataset-specific rules, making it difficult to accommodate changes in acquisition conditions and analytical objectives without manual intervention. Large language model (LLM)-based agents have shown promise for automating medical workflows, yet their potential for adaptive CT data preparation remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose CT-PrepAgent, which enables adaptive CT data preparation through a bounded policy and controlled deterministic execution. Deterministic inspection constructs structured data--task profiles, from which a policy decides an eligible DICOM series or predefined preprocessing profile, while the controlled execution flow guards, resolves, executes, and verifies the decision with bounded recovery when enabled and safe quarantine otherwise. Across three public CT segmentation tasks, CT-PrepAgent derived data-task adaptive preprocessing decisions and achieved the highest macro-average Dice. On two private raw-DICOM cohorts, CT-PrepAgent increased verified output yield from 61.7\% to 70.0\% and yielded similar registration metrics on common verified outputs. Controlled fault and replay tests validate bounded recovery, safe quarantine, and policy-free replay under tested fault and drift settings.

</details>


### [47] [Learning What to Remember and What to Internalize in LLM Self-Evolution via Adaptive Memory-Parameter Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01234)
*Tianyun Ji,Zhenya Huang,Jiayu Liu,Zirui Liu,Yu Su,Hongbin Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: COVE协调记忆/参数双通道的自进化策略，是通用的agent记忆与自我改进机制，对coding agent可迁移。

TL;DR: 提出COVE统一框架，融合基于外部框架和基于参数的自我进化，通过任务感知路由、阶段感知调度和知识优化实现灵活性与性能的平衡，实验优于单通道方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化方法要么通过外部化反馈快速适应，要么通过参数内化提升深层能力，但单独使用会在灵活性和性能之间产生权衡；核心问题是如何协调这两种通道以实现稳健的自我进化。

Method: COVE框架结合两种通道，包含任务感知路由（按任务特点选择学习机制）、阶段感知调度（按进化阶段调整学习策略）和知识优化（优化知识表示与整合）；将自我进化视为匹配任务和知识类型到合适机制的协调过程。

Result: 在多个任务类别上的实验显示，COVE优于单通道进化策略，在变化环境中展现出更稳健、更高效的改进。

Conclusion: 协调双通道的自我进化比单一通道更优，COVE验证了以协调方式整合外部化与参数化学习的有效性，为动态环境中的智能体自我进化提供了新范式。

中文摘要：大型语言模型智能体越来越多地运行在动态环境中，其中工具接口、API和用户需求在部署后发生变化。现有的自我进化方法主要遵循两种范式：基于外部框架（harness-based）的方法，将反馈外部化为可编辑的记忆或技能以快速适应；以及基于参数（parameter-based）的方法，将经验内化到模型参数中以实现更深层次的能力提升。然而，单独使用任何一种机制都会在灵活性和性能之间产生权衡。本文探讨智能体如何协调这两种通道以实现稳健的自我进化。我们提出了COVE，一个统一的智能体自我进化框架，通过任务感知路由、阶段感知调度和知识优化，将基于外部框架和基于参数的学习相结合。通过这种设计，COVE将自我进化视为一种协调过程，而不是对经验的无差别积累，该过程将任务和知识类型与合适的学习机制相匹配。跨多个任务类别的实验表明，COVE优于单通道进化策略，在变化的环境下展现出更稳健、更高效的改进。

Original abstract: Large language model agents increasingly operate in dynamic environments where tool interfaces, APIs, and user requirements change after deployment. Existing self-evolution methods mainly follow two paradigms: harness-based approaches, which externalize feedback into editable memories or skills for rapid adaptation, and parameter-based approaches, which internalize experience into model parameters for deeper capability improvement. However, using either mechanism alone creates a trade-off between flexibility and performance. This paper asks how an agent can coordinate both channels to achieve robust self-evolution. We present COVE, a unified agent self-evolution framework that combines harness-based and parameter-based learning through task-aware routing, stage-aware scheduling, and knowledge optimization. Through this design, COVE treats self-evolution not as indiscriminate accumulation of experience, but as a coordinated process that matches tasks and knowledge types to appropriate learning mechanisms. Experiments across multiple task categories show that COVE outperforms single-channel evolution strategies, demonstrating more robust and efficient improvement under changing environments.

</details>


### [48] [Cognitive Demand Steering for Adaptive Meta-Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01319)
*John Scoville,Shengzhuang Chen,Yejin Bang,Stefan Winzeck,Jonathan Richard Schwarz*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 21 pages, 3 figures

Selection reason: 提出无需训练的元推理框架CDS，用前向剩余需求信号控制推理干预，可零样本迁移到代码推理与修复任务。

TL;DR: 提出认知需求引导（CDS），一个无需训练的元推理框架，通过前向残差需求评估和认知尺度驱动的干预选择，显著提升LLM推理准确率，实现零样本跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元推理框架依赖回溯性奖励、粗糙搜索动作或需训练控制器，缺乏前瞻性诊断且需要大量监督。

Method: CDS使用基于LLM的进度评估器在每步估计残差推理需求，而非评估上一步；元控制器据此选择通用示例和推理动作，并利用16个认知科学维度的认知尺度对问题进行细粒度画像和干预设计。

Result: 在三个前端LLM和六个推理基准上，CDS相比直接调用提升21.9%准确率，相比标准CoT提升9%，在困难数学和编码任务上提升最大。

Conclusion: CDS通过前瞻性残差需求评估和认知尺度驱动的干预，无需训练即可实现高效、可迁移的LLM推理增强。

中文摘要：最近的元推理框架通过在迭代控制循环中包裹思维链生成来改进LLM推理，从而实现更有效的回溯、推理循环的终止以及提示性推理模式的注入等策略调整。尽管结果令人鼓舞，但方法往往依赖回溯性奖励函数，使用粗糙的搜索动作，或需要额外推理控制器训练，这需要大量少样本监督。我们引入了认知需求引导（CDS），一种无需训练的元推理框架，配备残差需求评估：在每一步，一个基于LLM的进度评估器刻画到达解决方案所需的残差推理，而不是仅仅评估上一步。这使得元控制器能够选择推理干预措施，包括通用示例和动作（例如，定量推理的一般指导），这些措施直接针对这一前瞻性需求信号。这一转变消除了对任何训练组件的需求，同时实现了跨模型和任务的零样本迁移，无需适应。我们不是依赖粗糙的特征描述，而是采用认知尺度来设计干预措施，并在16个受认知科学启发的维度上（例如，注意与扫描、学习与抽象、空间物理推理）刻画初始问题复杂性和残差需求信号，为控制器提供了精细的诊断词汇。在三个前沿LLM和六个推理基准上取平均值，CDS相比直接调用将准确率提高了21.9%，相比标准思维链推理提高了9%，在困难的数学和编码任务上提升最大。

Original abstract: Recent meta-reasoning frameworks improve LLM reasoning by wrapping chain-of-thought generation in an iterative control loop, allowing more effective backtracking, termination of reasoning loops, and injection of promising reasoning patterns, among other strategy adjustments. Despite promising results, methods often rely on backward-looking reward functions, utilize coarse search actions, or require additional reasoning controller training requiring many-shot supervision. We introduce Cognitive Demand Steering (CDS), a training-free meta-reasoning framework equipped with residual demand assessment: at each step, an LLM-based progress evaluator characterizes the residual reasoning required to arrive at a solution rather than merely evaluating the previous step. This allows a meta-controller to select reasoning interventions comprising both general-purpose exemplars and actions (e.g., general guidance for quantitative reasoning) that directly tackle this forward-looking demand signal. This shift eliminates the need for any trained component while enabling zero-shot transfer across models and tasks with no adaptation. Rather than relying on coarse characterizations, we employ cognitive scales to both design interventions as well as profile initial problem complexity and residual demand signal over 16 dimensions motivated by cognitive science (e.g., attention and scan, learning and abstraction, spatio-physical reasoning), giving the controller a fine-grained vocabulary for diagnosing. Averaged across three frontier LLMs and six reasoning benchmarks, CDS improves accuracy by $21.9\%$ over direct calls and $9\%$ over standard CoT reasoning, with the largest gains on difficult mathematics and coding tasks.

</details>


### [49] [G-ReAct: Graph-Guided Deep Search via Structure-State Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01324)
*Shaoxiong Yang,Mengyuan Zhang,Shaojun Lin,Chao Li,Wei Liu,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: G-ReAct以查询图状态引导深度搜索，缓解长程搜索漂移，对agent长程任务规划有直接参考价值。

TL;DR: G-ReAct 是一种通过固定拓扑查询图上的状态演化来组织深度搜索的推理框架，可同时支持监督微调和推理时引导，以少量数据超越大规模训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度搜索方法依赖线性顺序推理，难以在长程多跳搜索中保持中间状态和约束，导致上下文遗忘、搜索漂移和低效探索。

Method: G-ReAct 将推理组织为固定拓扑查询图上的状态演化，用图状态显式跟踪搜索进度并指导决策，将文本历史驱动的探索转化为图引导的约束推理；它既能生成高质量轨迹用于监督微调，也能在推理时提供结构化指导而无需额外微调。

Result: 仅用 1.9K 条生成轨迹微调，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 在 BrowseComp-ZH 上准确率达 52.6%，在 XBench 上达 79.0%，优于使用更大数据集训练的同类开源方法（包括 RL 增强方法）；推理时应用 G-ReAct 也能一致提升现有强 LLM 的性能。

Conclusion: G-ReAct 通过图状态演化有效缓解了深度搜索中的上下文保持与探索问题，显著提升了搜索准确率，且无需额外微调即可泛化到其他模型；代码和权重将开源。

中文摘要：深度搜索已成为大语言模型（LLM）解决开放域复杂任务的一项基本能力。然而，现有方法通常在线性序列推理的基础上进行轨迹生成和推理，难以在长时间、多跳搜索过程中一致地保留中间状态和约束。因此，它们经常遭受上下文遗忘、搜索漂移和低效探索的问题。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了 G-ReAct，一个用于深度搜索的推理框架，它将推理组织为在固定拓扑查询图上的状态演化。演化的图状态显式地跟踪搜索进度并指导后续决策，将由文本历史驱动的探索性搜索转化为在显式约束下图引导的推理。G-ReAct 同时支持训练和推理：它为监督微调生成高质量的深度搜索轨迹，并为推理时的搜索提供结构化指导，而无需额外的微调。实验表明，仅使用 1.9K 条生成的轨迹进行微调，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 在 BrowseComp-ZH 上达到了 52.6% 的准确率，在 XBench 上达到了 79.0%，优于在更大规模数据集上训练的同类开源方法，包括强化学习增强的方法。此外，当在推理时应用时，G-ReAct 持续提高了现有强 LLM 在深度搜索任务上的性能。我们将公开发布所有代码和模型权重。

Original abstract: Deep search has become a fundamental capability of large language models (LLMs) for solving open-domain complex tasks. However, existing approaches typically rely on linear sequential reasoning for both trajectory generation and inference, making it difficult to consistently preserve intermediate states and constraints throughout long-horizon multi-hop search. Consequently, they often suffer from context forgetting, search drift, and inefficient exploration. To address these limitations, we propose $\textbf{G-ReAct}$, a reasoning framework for deep search that organizes reasoning as $\textbf{state evolution over a fixed-topology query graph}$. The evolving graph state explicitly tracks search progress and guides subsequent decisions, transforming exploratory search driven by textual history into graph-guided reasoning under explicit constraints. G-ReAct supports both training and inference: it generates high-quality deep-search trajectories for supervised fine-tuning and provides structured guidance for inference-time search without additional fine-tuning. Experiments demonstrate that with only 1.9K generated trajectories for fine-tuning, Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 achieves $52.6\%$ accuracy on BrowseComp-ZH and $79.0\%$ on XBench, outperforming comparable open-source methods trained on substantially larger datasets, including RL-enhanced methods. Furthermore, when applied at inference time, G-ReAct consistently improves the performance of existing strong LLMs on deep-search tasks. We will publicly release all code and model weights.

</details>


### [50] [402Pilot: An x402 Decision Layer for Autonomous Agent Micropayments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01341)
*Yin Li,Yanbo He,Boo-Ho Yang,Rav Lawana,Ziyue Li,Wei Zeng,Jing Tang,Fugee Tsung*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 面向自主agent的小额支付决策层与策略学习，属通用agent资源受限决策与在线学习，对代码agent预算控制有启发。

TL;DR: 提出402Pilot，一个买方侧决策层，用支付感知的折扣上下文汤普森采样策略在有限钱包下选择可付费服务，并通过基准验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程支付协议只解决支付执行，未解决代理在有限钱包下应购买哪个付费服务的问题。

Method: 设计协议无关的买方侧决策层402Pilot，并使用支付感知的折扣上下文汤普森采样策略PA-DCT，以及冻结重放基准402Pilot-Bench。

Result: PA-DCT在非预言机策略中取得最佳固定钱包自适应权衡，仅花费钱包的39%-43%，并在质量、ROI和PA-gap/T等指标上表现最优。

Conclusion: 可编程支付需要辅以买方侧决策机制，以学习服务价值并自适应调整购买决策。

中文摘要：诸如x402之类的可编程支付协议支持按请求的微支付，但它们并未确定在有限钱包下自主代理应购买哪种可付费服务。我们将这一买方侧问题表述为代理原生的支付决策：在钱包压力下的上下文提供者选择、仅选择付费反馈以及不断变化的市场条件。我们提出402Pilot，一个位于自主代理与支付执行之间的协议无关的买方侧决策层，实现了用于选择可付费提供者的购买策略。我们以PA-DCT实例化它，一种支付感知的折扣上下文汤普森采样策略，在钱包压力下调整购买决策，同时从支付后反馈中学习。为了评估买方侧支付策略，我们引入了402Pilot-Bench，一个冻结重放基准，涵盖823个任务、五个异构提供者流水线和三种市场制度，每种制度在30对种子上进行评估。PA-DCT在非预言机策略中实现了最强固定钱包自适应权衡：它保持有竞争力的服务质量，同时仅花费钱包的39%到43%，并随市场条件变化重新分配支出。它在价格冲击下获得最佳非预言机PA-gap/T，并在质量、ROI和PA-gap/T的九种情景-指标组合中获得最佳均值和最差情况排名。与学习基线的比较和组件消融进一步支持所提出决策策略的有效性和设计。这些结果表明，可编程支付必须辅以能够学习服务价值并相应调整购买决策的买方侧决策机制。

Original abstract: Programmable-payment protocols such as x402 enable per-request micropayments, but they do not determine which payable service an autonomous agent should buy under a finite wallet. We formulate this buyer-side problem as agent-native payment decision-making: contextual provider selection under wallet pressure, chosen-only paid feedback, and changing market conditions. We propose 402Pilot, a protocol-agnostic buyer-side decision layer between autonomous agents and payment execution that implements purchasing policies for selecting among payable providers. We instantiate it with PA-DCT, a payment-aware discounted contextual Thompson-sampling policy that adapts purchasing decisions under wallet pressure while learning from post-payment feedback. To evaluate buyer-side payment policies, we introduce 402Pilot-Bench, a frozen-replay benchmark spanning 823 tasks, five heterogeneous provider pipelines, and three market regimes, each evaluated over 30 paired seeds. PA-DCT achieves the strongest fixed-wallet adaptive trade-off among non-oracle policies: it maintains competitive service quality while spending only 39 to 43 percent of the wallet and reallocates spending as market conditions change. It attains the best non-oracle PA-gap/T under the price shock and the best mean and worst-case ranks across the nine scenario-metric combinations of quality, ROI, and PA-gap/T. Comparisons with learning baselines and component ablations further support the effectiveness and design of the proposed decision policy. These results suggest that programmable payment must be complemented by buyer-side decision-making capable of learning service value and adapting purchasing decisions accordingly.

</details>


### [51] [Agentic Stage-One Stellarator Optimization: Autonomous Multi-Objective Search for Finite-Beta Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01344)
*Tingjia Zhang,Hongke Lu,Zhuoran Meng,Runlai Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用LLM agent作外环控制驱动恒星器数值优化，含确定性验证门与决策轨迹记录，是可直接借鉴SemOpt的agentic优化范式。

TL;DR: 提出一种语言模型智能体驱动的仿星器第一阶段优化方法，在有限预算下将门控有效构型从5个增至19个，Boozer QS RMS中位数降低约55%，并生成734条可重用决策记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿星器设计在高维空间中搜索，缺乏从规范到有效平衡的构造性映射；传统迭代优化依赖初始值和专家经验，计算昂贵且难以规模化产生一致评估的数据。

Method: 采用有界语言模型智能体与确定性DESC执行结合的agentic框架：智能体诊断当前平衡并选择下一个局部优化实验，DESC负责预设剖面、通量、对称性、度量评估、求解器有效性和接受性，形成闭环优化。

Result: 在公共预算子集上，门控有效构型从5个输入增加到19个输出；Boozer QS RMS中位数从2.39×10^-4降至1.07×10^-4；最大主曲率中位数从62.56降至33.00 m^-1。长路径实现9.10倍QS降低并修复磁阱和曲率缺陷，同时记录734条结构化父-动作-结果记录。

Conclusion: 证明智能体外环控制能够维持有限β多目标搜索，将重复优化转化为可扩展的平衡改进和数据生成来源，提升设计吞吐量并产生可复用决策数据。

中文摘要：第一阶段仿星器设计在一个高维的三维等离子体边界和固定边界MHD平衡族中搜索构型，以共同满足约束、磁力线拓扑、力平衡、稳定性代理和几何要求。这些规范并未提供到已验证有限β平衡的一般性构造映射。高质量目标通常通过迭代数值优化开发，其结果依赖于初始构型、活动Fourier分辨率、目标优先级和局部求解器预算。协调这一过程计算成本高且需要专家密集参与，限制了设计吞吐量和一致评估数据的产生。我们提出了一种用于“智能体式”第一阶段优化的概念验证。一个有界语言模型智能体诊断当前平衡并选择下一个局部优化实验，而确定性的DESC执行负责规定的剖面和通量、对称性、度量评估、求解器有效性和接受性。在一个来自扩展有限β活动的公共预算子集上，门控有效构型数从五个输入增加到十九个输出；Boozer QS RMS中位数从$2.39\times10^{-4}$降低到$1.07\times10^{-4}$，最大主曲率中位数从$62.56$降低到$33.00\,\mathrm{m}^{-1}$。一条互补的长路径实现了$9.10\times$的QS降低，同时修复了磁阱和曲率缺陷。该系统还记录每一个尝试的局部动作作为转换证据，在报告的实验中产生734条结构化的父-动作-结果记录。这些结果表明，智能体式外环控制能够维持有限β多目标搜索，并将重复优化转化为改进平衡和可重用决策数据的可扩展来源。

Original abstract: Stage-one stellarator design searches a high-dimensional family of three-dimensional plasma boundaries and fixed-boundary MHD equilibria for configurations that jointly meet requirements on confinement, field-line topology, force balance, stability proxies, and geometry. These specifications do not provide a general constructive map to a validated finite-beta equilibrium. High-quality targets are commonly developed through iterative numerical optimization whose outcome depends on the initial configuration, active Fourier resolution, objective priorities, and local solver budget. Coordinating this process is computationally costly and expert-intensive, limiting both design throughput and the production of consistently evaluated data. We present a proof of concept for \emph{agentic} stage-one optimization. A bounded language-model agent diagnoses the current equilibrium and selects the next local optimization experiment, while deterministic DESC execution owns prescribed profiles and flux, symmetry, metric evaluation, solver validity, and acceptance. On a common-budget subset from an expanding finite-beta campaign, the number of gate-valid configurations increases from five inputs to nineteen outputs; median Boozer QS RMS decreases from $2.39\times10^{-4}$ to $1.07\times10^{-4}$, and median maximum principal curvature decreases from $62.56$ to $33.00\,\mathrm{m}^{-1}$. A complementary long route achieves a $9.10\times$ QS reduction while repairing magnetic-well and curvature defects. The system also records every attempted local action as transition evidence, yielding 734 structured parent--action--outcome records in the reported experiments. These results show that agentic outer-loop control can sustain finite-beta, multi-objective search and turn repeated optimization into a scalable source of improved equilibria and reusable decision data.

</details>


### [52] [CRAFTS: Collaborative Role-Adaptive Fine-Tuning of LLM Agents for Chemical Process Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01369)
*Ziyun Zhang,Yuxin Lin,Eldin Wee Chuan Lim,Xinghao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: CRAFTS将化学流程建模拆成多角色子任务并用类型化中间表示+确定性门验证，工作流可迁移到代码agent。

TL;DR: CRAFTS是一个自动化化工过程模型构建框架，通过七个有界角色模拟化学工程师工作流程，使用类型化中间表示和确定性工程门，实现了91.5%的案例验证和执行契约完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可执行的化工过程模型需要大量手动工作且容易出错，现有方法缺乏结构化分解和工程验证机制。

Method: 将流程分解为七个角色，应用角色专业化微调，使用IDAES/Pyomo门进行确定性验证，并引入OpenIDAES-450数据集。

Result: 在82个案例的测试中，完成91.5%契约，单元/物流/连接F1分别为0.815/0.791/0.782。

Conclusion: 角色专业化、类型化中间表示和确定性工程门能有效提高自动化过程模型构建的可靠性。

中文摘要：构建可执行的化工过程模型仍然需要大量人工干预。化学工程师将不明确的请求转化为关于单元操作、热力学、物流、规格、自由度（DoF）、初始化、求解器修复和优化的耦合决策；一个错误就可能使模型失效。CRAFTS通过将仿真构建分解为分配给七个有界角色的有界子任务，并在阶段之间设置确定性的IDAES/Pyomo门，来镜像化学工程师的分阶段工作流程。给定自然语言请求、工艺流程图（PFD）证据和精选的化学工程知识，输入理解和意图模块恢复需求、约束和过程语义；视觉、拓扑和规格专家将其转化为类型化的模拟器契约；调试和优化模块支持有界的修复和符合条件的优化。微调应用于三个模式关键的视觉、拓扑和规格角色，而其余角色使用未调优的Qwen。由此产生的VisualGraphIR、TopologyIR、SpecIR、BuildPlan和SolveReport公开了单元、端口、热力学、数值和执行决策。兼容的构造函数、物性包和运行器仅在语义工件通过工程门后才被附加。我们引入了OpenIDAES-450，一个包含450个案例的IDAES过程仿真数据集，并通过对冻结的82个案例的留出分割进行求解和符合条件的优化来评估完整的七角色LangChain/LangGraph工作流程。CRAFTS完成了91.5%案例的规定验证和执行契约，并实现了单元、物流和有向连接F1分数分别为0.815、0.791和0.782。这些结果证明了角色专业化、类型化中间表示和确定性工程门在可靠的自动化过程模型构建中的有效性。

Original abstract: Constructing an executable chemical-process model remains manually intensive. Chemical engineers translate underspecified requests into coupled decisions about unit operations, thermodynamics, streams, specifications, degrees of freedom (DoF), initialization, solver repair, and optimization; one error can invalidate the model. CRAFTS mirrors the staged workflow of chemical engineers by decomposing simulation building into bounded subtasks assigned to seven bounded roles, with deterministic IDAES/Pyomo gates between stages. Given a natural-language request, process flowsheet diagram (PFD) evidence, and curated chemical-engineering knowledge, Input Understanding and Intent recover requirements, constraints, and process semantics; visual, topology, and specification specialists translate them into typed simulator contracts; and Debug and Optimization support bounded repair and eligible optimization. Fine-tuning is applied to the three schema-critical visual, topology, and specification roles, while the remaining roles use untuned Qwen. The resulting VisualGraphIR, TopologyIR, SpecIR, BuildPlan, and SolveReport expose unit, port, thermodynamic, numerical, and execution decisions. Compatible constructors, property packages, and runners are attached only after semantic artifacts pass engineering gates. We introduce OpenIDAES-450, a 450-case IDAES process- simulation dataset, and evaluate the complete seven-role LangChain/LangGraph workflow through solve and eligible optimization on its frozen 82-case held-out split. CRAFTS completes the prescribed validation and execution contract for for 91.5% of cases and achieves unit, stream, and directed-connection F1 scores of 0.815, 0.791, and 0.782. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of role specialization, typed intermediate representations, and deterministic engineering gates for reliable automated process-model construction.

</details>


### [53] [Scoring Rules! Statistical and Strategic Alignment for Text Evaluation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01423)
*Shengwei Xu,Yuxuan Lu,Yifan Wu,Jason Hartline,Grant Schoenebeck*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 文本评估指标的统计/策略一致性理论，识别指标被agent利用的风险，对构建可靠speedup验证与评测有参考。

TL;DR: 本文提出评估文本生成指标的对齐性测试原则，并设计统一互信息框架，发现基于互信息的指标比 LLM-as-a-Judge 更能抵抗策略性操纵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标主要关注与人工评分的相关性，但作为优化目标时易被策略性博弈；仅靠相关性无法保证指标在对抗性扰动下的可靠性。

Method: 提出两个对齐概念（统计对齐和策略对齐），设计三项测试原则（人类评分相关性、退化敏感性、操纵鲁棒性），并构建基于互信息的统一指标设计框架（四个选择：信息度量、估计方法、文本表示、预测机制）。

Result: 实验表明，高人类评分相关性不代表策略对齐；LLM-as-a-Judge 高相关但易受操纵，而基于互信息的指标显著提升操纵鲁棒性，新指标在整体鲁棒性上最优且相关性接近。

Conclusion: 评估指标不仅要统计对齐，还需策略对齐；基于互信息的框架能设计出更鲁棒的指标，为可靠评估和优化提供新方向。

中文摘要：基于参考的文本评估指标被广泛用于评估自然语言生成系统，其通过将候选回答与参考回答进行比较来打分。评估指标的可靠性通常通过其与人工评分的统计相关性来判断。然而，随着这些指标越来越多地被用作优化目标，仅凭相关性已不再足够：智能体可能策略性地博弈评估指标。我们通过两个互补的对齐概念来研究这一问题。如果指标与人工评分相关，则它在统计上对齐；如果它能抵抗不添加任务相关信息的扰动，则它在策略上对齐。我们做出两项贡献。首先，我们提出了基于参考的指标的测试原则，包括人工评分相关性、退化敏感性和操纵鲁棒性。这些原则评估指标是否与人类判断一致、是否惩罚低努力的信息丢失、以及是否抵抗策略性的分数膨胀。其次，我们开发了一个统一的基于互信息的指标设计框架，将现有和新的指标分解为四个选择：信息度量、估计方法、文本表示和预测机制。在同行评审、摘要生成和问答中，我们发现强人工评分相关性并不意味着策略对齐：LLM-as-a-Judge 实现了高相关性，但容易受到操纵。相比之下，基于互信息的指标显著提高了操纵鲁棒性。我们的框架还发现了一个新指标，该指标在我们的实验中实现了最强的整体鲁棒性，同时在人工评分相关性上保持竞争力。

Original abstract: Reference-based text evaluation metrics, which are widely used to assess natural language generation systems, score a candidate response by comparing it with a reference response. The reliability of an evaluation metric is usually judged by its statistical correlation with human ratings. However, as these metrics are increasingly used as optimization objectives, correlation alone is no longer sufficient: agents may strategically game the evaluation metric. We study this issue through two complementary notions of alignment. A metric is statistically aligned if it correlates with human ratings and strategically aligned if it resists perturbations that do not add task-relevant information. We make two contributions. First, we propose test principles for reference-based metrics consisting of human-rating correlation, degradation sensitivity, and manipulation robustness. These principles evaluate whether a metric agrees with human judgments, penalizes low-effort information loss, and resists strategic score inflation. Second, we develop a unified design framework for mutual-information-based metrics that decomposes existing and new metrics into four choices: information measure, estimation method, text representation, and prediction mechanism. Across peer review, summarization, and question answering, we find that strong human-rating correlation does not imply strategic alignment: LLM-as-a-Judge achieves high correlation but is susceptible to manipulation. In contrast, mutual-information-based metrics substantially improve manipulation robustness. Our framework also uncovers a new metric that achieves the strongest overall robustness in our experiments while remaining competitive on human-rating correlation.

</details>


### [54] [Where Reasoning Diverges: Localized Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01463)
*Weijun Gao,Xiang Ding,Tiancheng Xing,Haoyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出局部化多agent辩论协议，只针对分歧片段进行辩论，对多agent推理协作效率有直接启发。

TL;DR: 提出局部多智能体辩论（LMAD），只辩论最早冲突的局部片段，在多个基准和骨干上胜过常规基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辩论方法交换完整推理轨迹，效率低且可能重新引发已解决的分歧。

Method: 将轨迹表示为类型化节点，定位最早冲突并限制辩论到局部片段；通过受保护的解析扩展共享状态，避免重开已接受步骤。

Result: 在四个多跳QA基准上，使用十种骨干，LMAD的宏平均裁判准确率最高，比最强常规基线高出最多7.20个百分点。

Conclusion: LMAD通过局部化辩论，在多个模型和任务上显著提升推理准确率，是一种有效的推理时协议。

中文摘要：多智能体辩论通常交换完整的推理轨迹，即使分歧仅涉及少数中间声明。我们引入了局部多智能体辩论（LMAD），一种推理时协议，将智能体轨迹表示为类型化节点，定位其最早冲突，并将辩论限制在相应的局部片段。受保护的解析扩展了共享的已提交状态，使得后续冲突可以在不重新打开已接受步骤的情况下得到解决。我们使用来自四个模型家族的十个骨干，在四个多跳问答基准上评估了一个固定的LMAD配置。我们的方法在所有十个骨架上取得了最高的宏平均裁判准确率，比最强的常规基线高出最多7.20个百分点。

Original abstract: Multi-agent debate commonly exchanges complete reasoning traces even when disagreements concern only a few intermediate claims. We introduce Localized Multi-Agent Debate (LMAD), an inference-time protocol that represents agent traces as typed nodes, locates their earliest conflict, and restricts debate to the corresponding local segments. Guarded resolution extends a shared committed state so that later conflicts can be addressed without reopening accepted steps. We evaluate a single fixed LMAD configuration on four multi-hop question-answering benchmarks using ten backbones from four model families. Our method achieves the highest macro-averaged judge accuracy across all ten backbones, outperforming the strongest conventional baseline by up to 7.20 percentage points.

</details>


### [55] [Computing with Agentic Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01464)
*Jie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 25 pages, 0 figures

Selection reason: 形式化agentic oracle的token成本与目标损失风险，为agent计算开销与可靠性提供理论分析框架。

TL;DR: 提出了智能体预言机的SOTM框架，分析令牌成本，证明其相比固定随机预言机有成本优势，并研究目标丢失风险及其对质量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机预言机模型假设预言机每次响应独立同分布，无法建模自主追求目标、访问环境的智能体预言机。这种智能体预言机会产生隐藏的令牌成本，且其行为可能带来目标丢失风险，需要理论框架来分析。

Method: 扩展SOTM以纳入智能体预言机，区分编排令牌成本和智能体令牌成本；建立目标丢失避免准则，推导进度-重试-目标丢失公式，设置目标深度下界，分析零目标丢失情况，并研究目标丢失风险对任务质量上界的影响。

Result: 证明了使用可保留中间状态的智能体预言机的SOTM在相同任务和质量下，无论是否访问环境，都比固定随机预言机SOTM具有令牌成本优势；给出了目标丢失相关公式和下界，并发现目标丢失风险会限制涉及环境更新的任务的可实现质量。

Conclusion: 智能体预言机可以提供令牌成本优势，但引入目标丢失风险；通过内部调度顺序和风险准则可以部分缓解，但目标丢失风险本质上限制了任务质量上限。

中文摘要：本文将AI增强计算的随机预言机模型扩展到包含智能体预言机。与固定的随机预言机不同，智能体预言机可以自主追求目标，并且可能访问包含任务相关资源的环境。这些能力不仅影响响应分布，还会导致查询-响应接口之外可见的令牌成本。我们开发了一个框架，用于分析使用智能体预言机计算的随机预言机图灵机（SOTM）中的令牌成本。每次调用都有一个“编排令牌成本”（在查询-响应接口对调用者可见）和一个“智能体令牌成本”（由调用者不可见的内部操作产生）。我们证明，在解决相同任务且达到相同质量水平时，使用能够保留中间状态的智能体预言机的SOTM，在有无环境访问的情况下，都比使用固定随机预言机的SOTM具有令牌成本优势。我们还研究了目标丢失风险，包括内部调度顺序如何减少对不可逆操作的暴露。我们提供了一个目标丢失避免准则，推导了进度-重试-目标丢失公式，建立了令牌复杂度的目标深度下界，刻画了目标丢失概率为零时的令牌复杂度，并表明目标丢失风险可以对涉及环境更新的任务的可实现质量施加上限。

Original abstract: This paper extends the stochastic-oracle model of AI-augmented computing to include agentic oracles. Unlike a stationary stochastic oracle, which responds to the same query according to a fixed response distribution across calls, an agentic oracle can pursue a goal autonomously and may access an environment containing task-relevant resources. These capabilities affect both response distributions and token costs beyond what is visible at the query-response interface. We develop a framework for analyzing token costs in Stochastic-Oracle Turing Machines (SOTMs) that compute with agentic oracles. Each call has an \emph{orchestration token cost}, visible to the caller at the query-response interface, and an \emph{agentic token cost}, incurred by internal operations not exposed to the caller. We show that an SOTM computing with an agentic oracle that can retain intermediate state can have token-cost advantages over SOTMs using stationary stochastic oracles when solving the same task at the same quality level, both with and without environment access. We also investigate goal-loss risk, including how internal dispatch ordering can reduce exposure to irreversible actions. We provide a goal-loss avoidance criterion, derive progress--retry--goal-loss formulas, establish goal-depth lower bounds on token complexity, characterize token complexity when the probability of goal loss is zero, and show that goal-loss risk can impose an upper bound on the achievable quality of a task involving environment updates.

</details>


### [56] [V-Mem: Modality-Routed Retrieval for Long-Term Multimodal Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01543)
*Dingyi Kang,Dongming Jiang,Yi Li,Guanpeng Li,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 19 pages, 2 figures, 16 tables. Code: https://github.com/Dingyi-Kang/V-Mem

Selection reason: 提出按模态路由的多模态agent记忆检索系统，解决跨模态证据检索问题，对agent长程记忆与检索设计有借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出V-Mem，一种多模态智能体记忆系统，通过基于查询模态的路由和LLM生成的搜索锚点，解决多模态对话中视觉问题检索失败的问题，在Mem-Gallery和LoCoMo上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆多基于文本，即使支持多模态也难以回答视觉问题，原因在于相似性搜索假设在索引空间中查询与证据相近，但多模态场景下的模态间隙和相似性-相关性间隙破坏了这一假设。

Method: V-Mem根据查询和目标证据的模态（仅从查询识别）路由检索；将对话组织为轮次，从相同轮次返回目标模态内容以避免跨模态比较；使用LLM生成的锚点（纯文本查询生成假设性标题，图文查询生成结合文本和关键词的增强锚点）来弥合相似性-相关性间隙。

Result: 在Mem-Gallery上LLM评判分数0.82（第二好0.56），带图像问题0.87（无基线超过0.47）；在LoCoMo上0.69 vs 0.58。

Conclusion: V-Mem通过显式处理模态间隙和相似性-相关性间隙，显著提升了多模态对话中的视觉问题回答能力，证明了多模态记忆系统设计的有效性。

中文摘要：用户与LLM智能体之间的交互日益多模态化：对话中文本与图像交错出现，后续问题可能针对其中任何一种。然而，大多数智能体记忆是为文本设计的，即使是少数支持多模态对话的系统，在涉及视觉的问题上仍然失败。我们将这种失败追溯到它们所依赖的相似性搜索背后的一个假设：在索引空间中，查询与回答该查询的相关证据距离很近。在多模态设置中，两个间隙打破了这一假设。通过模态间隙，查询距离其自身模态的记忆内容比距离另一种模态的证据更近，即使在经过训练的共同嵌入空间中也是如此。通过相似性-相关性间隙，与查询最相似的内容往往不是回答该查询的证据，最严重的情况是当查询同时包含文本和图像，而其证据与任一单独部分都不相似时。我们提出了V-Mem，一种多模态智能体记忆系统，它根据查询的模态和目标证据的模态（两者都仅从查询中识别）来路由检索。为了跨越模态间隙，V-Mem将对话组织为轮次，并返回与匹配相同轮次中的目标模态内容，而不跨模态进行比较。为了弥合相似性-相关性间隙，它使用LLM生成的锚点进行搜索，该锚点比查询更接近相关证据：对于寻找图像的纯文本查询，使用一个假设性标题；当证据只能通过结合两者来获取时，使用一个增强的搜索锚点，即查询文本加上从查询图像中提取的相关关键词。在Mem-Gallery上，V-Mem达到了0.82的LLM评判分数，而第二名只有0.56，在携带图像的问题上差距最大（0.87，没有基线超过0.47）；在LoCoMo上，它得分0.69，而对比为0.58。

Original abstract: Interaction between users and LLM agents is increasingly multimodal: conversations interleave text with images, and a later question may target either. Yet most agent memories are designed around text, and even the few that support multimodal conversations still fail on vision-related questions. We trace this failure to an assumption behind the similarity search they rely on: in the index space, a query lies close to the relevant evidence that answers it. In multimodal settings, two gaps break it. By the modality gap, a query lies closer to memory content of its own modality than to evidence in another, even in a trained joint embedding space. By the similarity-relevance gap, the content most similar to a query is often not the evidence that answers it, most acutely when a query carries both text and image and its evidence resembles neither part alone. We present V-Mem, a multimodal agentic memory system that routes retrieval by the modality of the query and that of the target evidence, both recognized from the query alone. To cross the modality gap, V-Mem organizes the conversation into rounds and returns the target-modality content from the same round as the match, without comparing across modalities. To close the similarity-relevance gap, it searches with an LLM-generated anchor that sits closer to the relevant evidence than the query does: a hypothetical caption for a text-only query seeking an image, and an enriched search anchor, the query text plus relevant keywords extracted from the query image, when the evidence is reachable only by combining the two. On Mem-Gallery, V-Mem reaches an LLM-judge score of 0.82 versus 0.56 for the second best, with the largest margin on questions carrying an image (0.87, no baseline above 0.47); on LoCoMo it scores 0.69 versus 0.58.

</details>


### [57] [Securing Agentic AI: From Per-Action Checks to Trajectory Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01558)
*Alireza Lotfi,Subangkar Karmaker Shanto,Imtiaz Karim,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 6 pages. Accepted to the ACM AI Leadership Summit 2026 (Visionary Track)

Selection reason: 系统梳理agent安全从单动作检查到轨迹保证的框架，提出行为包容与可验证安全属性，对agent可靠部署直接相关。

TL;DR: 本文系统性地分析了LLM自主智能体在安全方面面临的跨层次挑战，涵盖单智能体攻击面、多智能体信任与委派、行为遏制、供应链完整性等，并提出安全应成为可验证的系统属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在受约束环境中执行关键任务，其安全性取决于整体行为是否符合规则和不变量，而非单个动作。随着LLM智能体自主性增强和跨组织委派，安全问题从单一挑战扩展为整个智能体栈的复杂格局，急需全面梳理和路线图。

Method: 采用概念性分析框架，从单智能体层、多智能体层、行为遏制、系统层等维度分类阐述攻击面和安全挑战，并提炼出统一原则：安全需作为架构、协议和运行时的可验证属性。

Result: 识别出关键安全挑战：不可信输入攻击面、多智能体身份与信任问题、行为遏制难题、供应链完整性与问责缺失，并指出当前这些方向大多为开放问题。

Conclusion: 安全必须从可选的指导层转变为治理智能体行为的可验证系统属性，通过架构和协议设计实现。所描绘的挑战地图为可信自主智能体部署提供了路线图。

中文摘要：自主智能体越来越多地被用于执行在受操作约束、组织政策、监管要求和技术标准支配的环境中的关键任务。因此，它们的安全性并非由单个动作的正确性决定，而是由其整体行为是否与运行其间的系统的规则和不变量保持一致来决定。随着基于大型语言模型（LLM）的智能体变得更加自主，并越来越多地在组织边界之间委派任务，保障其安全从单一挑战演变为涵盖整个智能体栈的广泛且相互关联的格局。在单智能体层面，通过提示、记忆、检索知识和工具接口传入的不可信输入构成了攻击面。在多智能体设置中，委派和通信引入了与身份、信任、能力控制和决策透明度相关的挑战，而底层的模型路由和执行控制平面仍然容易受到操纵以及未经验证的模型来源的影响。也许最根本的挑战是行为遏制：一系列单独允许的动作可能在集体上违反系统级约束和安全不变量。在更广泛的层面上，供应链完整性、来源、问责制和端到端可观测性在很大程度上仍是开放问题。一个共同的原则统一了这些方向：安全必须成为治理智能体行为的架构、协议和运行时的一个可验证属性，而不是一个可选指导层。描绘这些挑战为可信自主智能体部署提供了路线图。

Original abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly used to execute consequential tasks in environments governed by operational constraints, organizational policies, regulatory requirements, and technical standards. Their safety is therefore determined not by the correctness of individual actions, but by whether their overall behavior remains consistent with the rules and invariants of the systems in which they operate. As large language model (LLM)-based agents become more autonomous and increasingly delegate tasks across organizational boundaries, securing them evolves from a single challenge into a broad and interconnected landscape spanning the entire agentic stack. At the single-agent level, untrusted inputs through prompts, memory, retrieved knowledge, and tool interfaces create attack surfaces.
