In multi-agent settings, delegation and communication introduce challenges related to identity, trust, capability control, and decision transparency, while the underlying model routing and execution control plane remains vulnerable to manipulation and to unverified model provenance. Perhaps the most fundamental challenge is behavioral containment: sequences of individually permissible actions may collectively violate system-level constraints and safety invariants. At the broader level, supply-chain integrity, provenance, accountability, and end-to-end observability remain largely open problems. A common principle unifies these directions: security must become a verifiable property of the architectures, protocols, and runtimes that govern agent behavior, rather than an optional layer of guidance. Charting these challenges provides a roadmap toward trustworthy autonomous agent deployment.

</details>


### [58] [Does the Competitive Component of Adversarial Self-Play Improve Legal Reasoning? A Controlled Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01559)
*Miseog Shawn Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 对抗自博弈训练竞争组件无益的诚实负结果，共享可验证环境与指标陷阱，对agent训练信号设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: 在合法推理中，对抗性自我博弈（对手+生存奖励）相比非竞争性训练没有带来可靠收益，结果为零；价值在于可复现性和警示指标陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证对抗性自我博弈中的竞争成分（对手与生存奖励）是否在合法推理训练中提供额外价值，而非仅仅依靠可验证环境。

Method: 设计可验证的“生存”奖励（引文验证器检查学生权威与对手反权威），对比竞争性训练与非竞争性基线；进行四项独立测试（自举比较、双种子、逐案例鲁棒性、盲法判断）及一个强化对手的后续试点。

Result: 所有测试均无可靠收益：盲法胜率49%（p≈1.000），强化对手试点50%（32:32，p≈1.000）；早期+29%优势被证实为小样本伪影。

Conclusion: 竞争成分本身没有带来好处；可验证环境的构建才是关键，与编码领域研究结论一致，并报告为诚实负面结果以警示指标陷阱。

中文摘要：对抗性自我博弈是法律推理的一种有吸引力的方法：让学生模型起草论据，让对手攻击它，当学生的论据在攻击中幸存时给予奖励。我们设计了这样一个训练信号——一个可验证的“生存”奖励，其中学生引用的权威和对手引用的反权威都由引文验证器检查，因此生存是基于验证的事实而非修辞来决定的，伪造的引文会被自动中和。然后我们提出了一个狭窄但重要的问题：竞争成分本身——对手和生存奖励——是否在完全相同的非竞争性训练运行之上增加了任何东西？在四项独立测试中——自举比较、双种子重复、配对逐案例对抗鲁棒性比较、以及生成论据的盲法正面交锋判断，外加一个故意强化自我博弈对手的后续试点——竞争成分没有产生可靠的收益。盲法判断的胜率为49%（二项式p约1.000）；强化对手试点的胜率为50%（32:32，p约1.000）。早期看似+29%的优势后来逆转，并被证明是小样本伪影。我们将其报告为一个诚实的负面结果。本文的价值在于可重复性和具体陷阱的分享：一个最初有希望的指标在更多数据上反转，以及一个对抗鲁棒性指标在对手停止引用与黄金答案相同的权威后悄然退化为普通召回率。这一零结果与配套编码领域研究（Kim, 2026, arXiv:2607.08255）的结论一致，并在法律领域再次确认：多教师课程的价值在于构建可验证的环境，而非竞争本身。

Original abstract: Adversarial self-play is an appealing recipe for legal reasoning: have a student model draft an argument, have an adversary attack it, and reward the student when its argument survives the attack. We designed exactly such a training signal -- a verifiable "survival" reward in which both the student's cited authorities and the adversary's counter-authorities are checked by a citation verifier, so that survival is decided on verified grounds rather than rhetoric, and fabricated citations are automatically neutralized. We then asked a narrow but important question: does the competitive component itself -- the adversary and the survival reward -- add anything on top of an otherwise identical non-competitive training run? Across four independent tests -- a bootstrap comparison, a two-seed replication, a paired per-case adversarial-robustness comparison, and a blinded head-to-head judgment of generated arguments, plus a follow-up pilot with a deliberately strengthened self-play adversary -- the competitive component produced no reliable benefit. The blinded judgment gave a 49% win rate (binomial p approx. 1.000); the strengthened-adversary pilot gave a 50% win rate (32:32, p approx. 1.000). An early apparent +29% advantage reversed and proved to be a small-sample artifact. We report this as an honest negative result. The value of the paper is reproducibility and the sharing of concrete pitfalls: an initially promising metric that inverted on more data, and an adversarial-robustness metric that silently collapsed to plain recall once the adversary stopped citing the same authorities as the gold answer. This null is consistent with, and reconfirms in the legal domain, the conclusion of the companion coding-domain study (Kim, 2026, arXiv:2607.08255) that the value of multi-teacher curricula arises from constructing a verifiable environment rather than from competition itself.

</details>


### [59] [When Memory Updates but Behavior Does Not: Repairing Implicit Stale Dependencies in Personalized Agent Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01619)
*Haofei Sun,Lin He*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 9 pages, 4 figures; supplementary material in ancillary files

Selection reason: 诊断并修复记忆增强agent中的陈旧依赖问题，提出从存储状态到草稿的反向审计验证，对agent记忆可靠性与验证有启示。

TL;DR: StateAuditor通过从存储状态到草稿的逆向审计，验证来源和时序而非语义，有效缩小了STALE基准中的隐式策略适应（IPA）差距，相比前身取得显著配对提升，且增益主要来自转换机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强智能体在用户状态过时时仍可能围绕旧值规划，即存在隐式策略适应（IPA）差距。现有草稿锚定验证只检查回复内容，而开放式回复往往不提及过时依赖。需要一种结构性方法从存储状态出发审计草稿，以识别和修复过时状态。

Method: StateAuditor采用逆向审计：由LLM从带时间戳证据中提出候选旧到新转换，然后用确定性代码将每个引用固定到单一存储条目，验证新证据的真实时间顺序，仅允许经过验证的转换触发修复。该方法强调验证来源和时序，而非语义取代，并与匹配对照组比较以隔离机制贡献。

Result: 在STALE完整协议下，严格单查询VTA得分.736，较前身.686提升+5.0个百分点（95% CI [+2.9, +7.2]），增益几乎全部来自IPA和前提抵抗力（PR）。第三模型家族的基准评判器复现增益（.738 vs .680）。匹配对照组（.692）不显著，表明STALE增益来自转换机制。在HorizonBench上，完整流水线提升当前偏好准确率（p<.01），但大部分外部增益来自草稿侧审计；更难生命周期集无增益。

Conclusion: StateAuditor通过验证证据来源和时序而非语义取代，有效修复过时状态，在STALE上带来显著且可解释的增益，且该增益归因于转换机制本身。但外部基准上增益有限，该方法不适用于通用智能体记忆，仅针对特定基准和结构化存储场景有效。

中文摘要：记忆增强型智能体可以知道用户的存储状态已过时，但仍然会围绕旧值进行规划。STALE基准将此称为隐式策略适应（IPA）差距。我们确定了一个结构性促成因素：草稿锚定验证检查的是回复内容，而在开放式回复中，过时依赖通常未被提及。因此，StateAuditor以相反方向进行审计，即从存储状态到草稿。一个LLM从带时间戳的证据中提出候选的旧到新转换；确定性代码将每条引用固定到单个条目，检查新证据确实更新，并且只允许这些经过验证的转换触发修复。被验证的是来源和时序——而非语义取代。在STALE的完整协议（400个场景、50次会话历史、每个查询一个独立回复）下，严格单查询VTA分数为.736，而在相同评判器下我们的锁定前身为.686：配对增益为+5.0个百分点（95%置信区间[+2.9, +7.2]），几乎完全来自IPA和前提抵抗力（PR）。基准自身的评判器（来自第三个模型家族）复现了该增益（.738对.680）。在独立的跨家族偏好演化基准（HorizonBench）上，完整的草稿-审计-修复流水线在基于金标准派生的结构化存储上提高了当前偏好准确率（用户聚类p<.01），尽管匹配对照组显示该外部增益大部分来自草稿侧审计本身；一个更难的人工生命周期集没有增益，限制了该论断，而错误无效化保持受控。相比之下，在STALE上，匹配对照组（相同证据、适配器和调用预算）得分仅为.692（较前身+0.6，不显著），从而将STALE增益归因于转换机制而非增加的上下文或调用。我们不声称对通用型智能体记忆有任何主张。

Original abstract: Memory-augmented agents can know that a user's stored state is outdated and still plan around the old value. The STALE benchmark calls this the implicit policy adaptation (IPA) gap. We identify one structural contributor: draft-anchored verification checks what a response says, and in an open-ended response the stale dependency is usually unsaid. StateAuditor therefore audits in the opposite direction, from stored state to draft. An LLM proposes candidate old-to-new transitions from timestamped evidence; deterministic code pins each quotation to a single entry, checks that the new evidence really is newer, and lets only these verified transitions trigger repair. What is verified is provenance and chronology - not semantic supersession. On STALE's full protocol (400 scenarios, 50-session histories, one independent response per query), strict single-query VTA scores .736 against .686 for our locked predecessor under the same judge: a +5.0-point paired gain (95% CI [+2.9, +7.2]) coming almost entirely from IPA and premise resistance (PR). The benchmark's own judge, from a third model family, reproduces the gain (.738 vs. .680). On an independent cross-family preference-evolution benchmark (HorizonBench), the full draft-audit-repair pipeline over a gold-derived structured store raises current-preference accuracy (user-clustered p<.01), though a matched control shows most of this external gain is the draft-side audit itself; a harder authored lifecycle set gives no gain, bounding the claim while false invalidation stays controlled. On STALE, by contrast, a matched control (same evidence, adapter, and call budget) scores only .692 (+0.6 over the predecessor, n.s.), attributing the STALE gain to the transition machinery rather than added context or calls. We make no claim about general-purpose agent memory.

</details>


### [60] [Salami Attack: Stealthy Collusive Memory Poisoning against OpenClaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01637)
*Zheng Lin,Yuzhe Huang,Zhenxing Niu,Xianmin Ye,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出针对agent记忆的协同投毒攻击框架，展示个体良性记忆组合成威胁，对agent记忆安全控制有启发。

TL;DR: 提出MemCollusion，一种利用沙拉米策略生成多个看似良性但联合有害的记忆片段来毒化LLM智能体长期记忆的自动化红队框架，并构建了MoltLab环境进行跨会话评估，实验显示高攻击成功率且绕过现有防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆毒化攻击仅考虑单个恶意记录，忽略了多个良性记忆组合可能引发的安全威胁；长期记忆作为智能体的持久状态，其安全风险亟需更全面的评估。

Method: 开发MemCollusion框架，采用沙拉米策略将恶意目标拆分为单独无害的记忆片段；设计四个约束和五种理论指导策略来构建记忆联盟，并使用微调生成器生成记忆；同时创建MoltLab模拟真实跨会话场景，要求先观察并蒸馏记忆再在后续会话中触发行为。

Result: 在OpenClaw上使用两个骨干模型、48个场景的评估中，最强记忆保存设置下达到81.3%的平均记忆保存率和75.0%的攻击成功率，且在良性记忆稀释和记忆级防御下仍然有效。

Conclusion: MemCollusion揭示了共谋性记忆毒化的实际风险，表明现有防御对组合性攻击的脆弱性，并提供了一个可复现的评估工具（MoltLab）以推动该方向的研究。

中文摘要：长期记忆使LLM智能体能够在会话间保留有用信息，但也创造了一个攻击面， adversaries可通过该攻击面毒化智能体的持久记忆以操纵其行为。现有的记忆毒化攻击主要依赖于单个恶意记录，忽略了一种组合性威胁：多个看似良性的记忆可能共同诱发不安全行为。在本文中，我们介绍了MemCollusion，一个用于构建共谋性记忆毒化攻击的自动化红队框架。MemCollusion应用沙拉米策略——一种将对抗性目标切分为小的、单独无害的片段的策略——来生成单独看起来良性但整体有害的记忆片段。它使用四个设计约束、五种理论依据策略和一个微调生成器来构建记忆联盟。为了在现实的跨会话环境中评估共谋性记忆毒化，我们开发了MoltLab，一个受控的Moltbook研究复现，其中精心制作的平台内容必须先被观察并提炼为持久记忆，然后在单独的会话中影响智能体的行为。我们在OpenClaw上使用两个骨干模型在48个场景中评估MemCollusion。在最强的记忆保存设置下，MemCollusion实现了平均81.3%的记忆保存率和75.0%的攻击成功率，并且在良性记忆稀释和记忆级防御下仍然有效。

Original abstract: Long-term memory enables LLM agents to retain useful information across sessions, but also creates an attack surface through which adversaries may poison an agent's persistent memory to steer its behavior. Existing memory poisoning attacks mainly rely on individually malicious records, overlooking a compositional threat: multiple benign-looking memories may jointly induce unsafe behavior. In this paper, we introduce MemCollusion, an automated red-teaming framework for constructing collusive memory poisoning attacks. MemCollusion applies salami tactics---a strategy that slices an adversarial objective into small, individually innocuous pieces---to generate memory fragments that are individually benign looking but collectively harmful. It constructs memory coalitions using four design constraints, five theory-informed strategies, and a fine-tuned generator. To assess collusive memory poisoning in a realistic cross-session setting, we develop MoltLab, a controlled research reproduction of Moltbook, in which crafted platform content must first be observed and distilled into persistent memory before influencing the agent's behavior in a separate session. We evaluate MemCollusion on OpenClaw using two backbone models across 48 scenarios. Under the strongest memory-saving setting, MemCollusion achieves an average Memory Save Rate of 81.3% and an Attack Success Rate of 75.0%, and remains effective under both benign memory dilution and memory-level defenses.

</details>


### [61] [GISAgentBench: A Practitioner-Sourced Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on GIS Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01645)
*Abhinav Pothuri,Zhe Jiang,Zelin Xu,Di Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 构建带确定性ground truth的GIS agent评测基准，其严格输出匹配方法可迁移到自动代码优化benchmark的验证设计。

TL;DR: GISAgentBench是一个由349个真实GIS任务组成的基准，提供可执行参考轨迹和精确真实输出，用于严格评估LLM智能体；最佳模型仅完成32.7%的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GIS智能体基准缺乏真实数据和真实输出，依赖替代信号评估，无法准确衡量任务正确性。

Method: 从GIS Stack Exchange构建349个多步骤任务，基于六个真实地理区域，附带可执行参考轨迹和精确输出文件，采用确定性、容错感知的输出匹配。

Result: 六个LLM模型中，最佳智能体在严格容错评分下仅完成32.7%的任务，但大多数模型输出接近真实值。

Conclusion: 现实GIS工作流对当前LLM智能体仍具挑战性，GISAgentBench可推动更可靠的地理空间自动化研究。

中文摘要：地理信息系统（GIS）专业人员依赖多步骤空间分析工作流来支持城市规划、灾害响应和环境监测中的决策。该过程繁琐、耗时且容易出错。尽管最近配备外部工具的大型语言模型（LLM）智能体有潜力自动化地理空间分析，但它们在现实GIS工作流中的能力在很大程度上仍未得到探索。现有的GIS智能体基准数据集大多来自教科书、教程或LLM生成的种子，且规模有限、轨迹深度不足。更重要的是，没有一个提供真实输出。因此，它们依赖于诸如代码相似性、轨迹匹配或LLM和VLM评判者之类的替代信号，这可能将工作流相似性与任务正确性混为一谈。为解决这一差距，我们引入了GISAgentBench，这是一个包含349个多步骤GIS任务的基准，这些任务来自GIS Stack Exchange，并在六个选定的感兴趣地理区域上使用真实公共数据实例化。每个任务附带一个可执行的参考轨迹和一个精确的真实输出文件，从而能够实现严格的、确定性的、容忍误差的输出匹配，超越LLM评判。对六个LLM模型的评估表明，现实GIS工作流仍然具有挑战性：在严格的容忍误差评分下，最佳智能体仅完成32.7%的任务，尽管大多数模型产生的输出接近真实值。

Original abstract: Geographic Information System (GIS) professionals rely on multi-step spatial analysis workflows to support decision-making in urban planning, disaster response, and environmental monitoring. The process is tedious, time-consuming, and error-prone. While recent large language model (LLM) agents equipped with external tools have the potential to automate geospatial analysis, their ability to perform realistic GIS workflows remains largely unexplored. Existing GIS agent benchmarking datasets are mostly drawn from textbooks, tutorials, or LLM-generated seeds and remain limited in size and trajectory depth. More importantly, none provides ground truth outputs. They therefore rely on surrogate signals such as code similarity, trajectory matching, or LLM and VLM judges, which can conflate workflow resemblance with task correctness. To address this gap, we introduce GISAgentBench, a benchmark of 349 multi-step GIS tasks curated from GIS Stack Exchange and instantiated on real public data across six selected geographic areas of interest. Each task ships with an executable reference trajectory and an exact ground truth output file, enabling strict, deterministic, tolerance-aware output matching beyond LLM judging. Evaluations of six LLM models reveal that realistic GIS workflows remain challenging: the best agent completes only 32.7% of tasks under strict tolerance-aware scoring, although most models produce outputs that are close to the ground truth.

</details>


### [62] [TCPO: Turn-Level Credit Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01667)
*Sicong Liao,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出turn级信用分配用于多轮verifier引导RL，并在代码生成和AppWorld agent任务上验证，对agent长程任务训练直接可迁移。

TL;DR: 提出了TCPO，一种通过分数到信用转换实现轮次级信用分配的方法，用于验证器引导的多轮强化学习，在数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务上取得了最优或匹配最优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在验证器引导的多轮强化学习中，每轮提供的验证器分数虽为密集反馈，但无法直接反映该轮对整体优化轨迹的贡献，即缺乏密集信用分配，阻碍了策略的有效优化。

Method: TCPO将信用分配视为分数到信用的转换，通过基于参考的比较构建轮次级优势，包括回溯性信用、后见之明延迟信用和选择性固定历史反事实估计。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和AppWorld任务上，TCPO优于或匹配最强基线；在Qwen3-4B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B上取得最佳或并列最佳的最佳轮次Pass@8，并减少成功轮数、提升多轮智能体性能。

Conclusion: 分数到信用转换是验证器引导的多轮策略优化的核心要素，TCPO为此提供了有效的轮次级信用分配机制。

中文摘要：验证器引导的强化学习已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的强大范式。在多轮设置中，模型在每一轮后接收验证器分数，并迭代地优化其输出。尽管此类分数提供了密集的反馈，但它们并未直接提供密集的信用分配：分数衡量当前输出的质量，而信用应衡量当前轮次如何改变优化轨迹。我们提出TCPO，一种用于验证器引导的多轮强化学习的轮次级信用分配方法。TCPO将信用分配视为分数到信用的转换，并通过基于参考的比较构建轮次级优势：回溯性信用捕捉相对于最佳先前状态的即时进展和回退；后见之明延迟信用识别具有后期收益的非改进轮次；以及选择性固定历史反事实估计在相同历史下细化高意外轮次。在数学推理、代码生成和AppWorld智能体任务上的实验表明，TCPO在不同模型规模、任务领域和验证器类型上均优于或匹配最强基线。TCPO在Qwen3-4B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B上实现了最佳或并列最佳的最佳轮次Pass@8，减少了成功所需轮数，并提升了多轮智能体性能。这些结果凸显了分数到信用转换作为验证器引导的多轮策略优化的核心要素。

Original abstract: Verifier-guided reinforcement learning has become a powerful paradigm for improving LLM reasoning. In multi-turn settings, models receive a verifier score after each turn and iteratively refine their outputs. Although such scores provide dense feedback, they do not directly provide dense credit: a score measures the quality of the current output, while credit should measure how the current turn changes the refinement trajectory. We propose TCPO, a turn-level credit assignment method for verifier-guided multi-turn RL. TCPO casts credit assignment as score-to-credit conversion and constructs turn-level advantages through reference-based comparisons: retrospective credit captures immediate progress and regression relative to the best prior state; hindsight delayed credit identifies non-improving turns with later payoff; and selective fixed-history counterfactual estimation refines high-surprisal turns under the same history. Experiments on math reasoning, code generation, and AppWorld agent tasks show that TCPO improves or matches the strongest baselines across model scales, task domains, and verifier types. TCPO achieves the best or tied-best best-turn Pass@8 on Qwen3-4B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, reduces turns to success, and improves multi-turn agent performance. These results highlight score-to-credit conversion as a central ingredient for verifier-guided multi-turn policy optimization.

</details>


### [63] [When Memory Becomes Authority: Benchmarking Authority Collapse at the Memory Consolidation Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01679)
*Qiuyang Zhan,Rui Zhang,Sheng Guo,Lepeng Zhao,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 38 pages, 2 figures

Selection reason: 提出记忆整合中的权威塌缩benchmark，证明持久记忆需保留来源授权约束，对agent记忆建模与安全有普遍启发。

TL;DR: 发现LLM代理内存整合中的“权威崩溃”问题：记忆保留内容却丢失来源授权约束，导致未授权行为；提出的AuthMem-Bench基准和自动权威标签可将未授权动作率降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内存整合决定信息可被如何复用，但现有系统可能使存储记忆暗示超出来源的权威，导致代理在后续任务中做出未经授权的行为，因此需要系统研究。

Method: 提出AuthMem-Bench基准，固定主张和下游任务，仅改变来源权威，评估写入时崩溃、下游授权错误和自动权威保留；并测试自动预测与持久化权威标签的效果。

Result: 在49个配置中48个出现权威崩溃；无权威元数据时未授权动作率50.3%；自动权威标签将端到端未授权动作率从16.9%降到0.0%，且良性任务成功率基本不变。

Conclusion: 记忆驱动适应必须同时保留内容和复用授权，仅保留所学内容不够；需要显式维护来源权威约束。

中文摘要：持久化内存使（自我演化的）LLM代理能够通过将异构交互历史整合为可复用的事实、偏好、观察和规则，从而跨任务适应。然而，整合也施加了一个隐含的授权边界：它决定存储的信息之后是否可以被作为用户事实、经证实的观察或长期指示来消费。我们发现了权威崩溃（authority collapse），即整合保留了一个主张，却抹去了控制其授权使用的源约束，导致存储的记忆暗示出比其来源允许的更大的权威。我们引入了AuthMem-Bench，一个受控的配对基准，在保持焦点主张和下游任务不变的情况下，仅改变来源权威。它评估写入时崩溃、下游授权错误和自动权威保留。在基于广泛使用的代理内存系统的七个整合器和七个LLM骨干上，我们在49个评估配置中的48个中观察到权威崩溃。在受控的基于动作的评估中，没有权威元数据的崩溃记忆产生平均未授权动作率为50.3%。在端到端评估中，自动预测并持久化的权威标签将观察到的未授权动作率从16.9%降低到0.0%，而良性任务成功率基本保持不变。这些发现表明，记忆驱动的适应不仅必须保留所学到的内容，还必须保留其可被复用的权威。

Original abstract: Persistent memory allows (self-evolving) LLM agents to adapt across tasks by consolidating heterogeneous interaction histories into reusable facts, preferences, observations, and rules. Yet consolidation also imposes an implicit authorization boundary: it determines whether stored information may later be consumed as a user fact, an attested observation, or a standing instruction. We identify authority collapse, in which consolidation preserves a claim while erasing the source constraints governing its authorized use, causing the stored memory to imply greater authority than its source permits. We introduce AuthMem-Bench, a controlled paired benchmark that holds the focal claim and downstream task fixed while varying only source authority. It evaluates write-time collapse, downstream authorization errors, and automatic authority preservation. Across seven consolidators based on widely used agent-memory systems and seven LLM backbones, we observe authority collapse in 48 of 49 evaluated configurations. In a controlled action-grounded evaluation, collapsed memories without authority metadata yield a mean unauthorized-action rate of 50.3%. In an end-to-end evaluation, automatically predicted and persisted authority labels reduce the observed unauthorized-action rate from 16.9% to 0.0%, while benign task success remains essentially unchanged. These findings show that memory-driven adaptation must preserve not only what was learned, but also the authority under which it may be reused.

</details>


### [64] [GABench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on Graph Analysis Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01684)
*Jiarui Tan,Zhongjian Zhang,YaBo Guo,Jiawei Liu,Yujie Xing,Muhan Zhang,Cheng Yang,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 构建涵盖多类图任务的agentic benchmark，评估工具调用与harness表现，对coding agent评测设计有直接参考价值。

TL;DR: GABench是一个综合性的智能体图分析基准，涵盖三种图类型、四类任务和84个工具，构建了10,400个可验证任务，评估发现现有LLM智能体在复杂图分析上仍存在困难，且性能更依赖工具调用质量而非数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图基准对LLM智能体的评估存在局限：图任务和图类型覆盖有限，且通常以文本问答形式直接提供图信息，无法评估端到端的智能体能力。因此，需要一个新的基准来全面评估LLM智能体在图分析中的实际表现。

Method: 提出了GABench基准，覆盖三种图类型（如可能为图数据的具体类型，但原文未明说，我们保留为三种图类型）和四类图分析任务（图检索、图理论、图机器学习、图开放问答）。提供84个可执行工具，并开发了任务生成流水线，构建了10,400个具有可验证真实值的任务。利用该基准评估多种前沿LLM和智能体框架。

Result: 实验发现：（1）现有LLM智能体在复杂图分析任务上表现不佳；（2）框架选择对性能有显著影响，但现有框架在复杂图任务上能力有限；（3）图分析性能更依赖于工具调用的质量而非数量。

Conclusion: GABench为评估LLM智能体的图分析能力提供了全面的基准，研究结果揭示了现有方法的不足，并为开发和评估面向图分析的LLM智能体提供了实用见解，强调提升工具调用质量的重要性。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）智能体在规划、使用工具和与外部环境交互方面的能力日益增强。它们通常由框架（harness）支持，这些框架管理状态并协调多步骤执行。图分析为评估其智能体能力提供了一个有前景的场景，因为它要求智能体在图环境中访问数据并执行操作。然而，现有的用于LLM的图基准对图任务和图类型的覆盖有限，难以全面评估LLM智能体。此外，它们通常将图分析表述为基于文本的问答，图信息直接提供在提示中，限制了对端到端智能体能力的评估。为解决这些局限，我们引入了GABench，一个面向智能体图分析的综合性基准。GABench涵盖三种图类型，并覆盖四类图分析任务：图检索、图理论、图机器学习和图开放问答。GABench还提供了84个可执行工具，用于访问图数据并执行多样化的图操作。基于这些工具，我们开发了一个智能体图分析任务生成流水线，并构建了10,400个具有可验证真实值的任务。利用GABench，我们评估了一系列前沿LLM和智能体框架。我们的实验揭示了三个关键发现：（1）现有的LLM智能体在复杂图分析任务上仍然表现困难；（2）框架选择显著影响性能，但现有框架在复杂图任务上仍然有限；（3）图分析更依赖于工具调用的质量而非数量。我们的发现为LLM智能体在图分析方面的开发和评估提供了实用见解。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly capable of planning, using tools, and interacting with external environments. They are typically supported by harnesses, which manage state and coordinate multi-step execution. Graph analysis provides a promising setting for evaluating their agentic capabilities, because it requires agents to access data and execute operations in a graph environment. However, existing graph benchmarks for LLMs provide limited coverage of graph tasks and graph types, making it difficult to comprehensively evaluate LLM agents. Moreover, they typically formulate graph analysis as text-based question answering, where graph information is directly provided in the prompt, limiting the evaluation of end-to-end agentic capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce GABench, a comprehensive benchmark for agentic graph analysis. GABench spans three graph types and covers four graph analysis task categories: graph retrieval, graph theory, graph machine learning, and graph open-ended question answering. GABench also provides 84 executable tools for accessing graph data and performing diverse graph operations. Building on these tools, we develop an agentic graph analysis task generation pipeline and construct 10,400 tasks with verifiable ground truth.Using GABench, we evaluate a range of frontier LLMs and agent harnesses. Our experiments reveal three key findings: (1) Existing LLM agents still struggle with complex graph analysis tasks. (2) Harness choice significantly affects performance, yet existing harnesses remain limited on complex graph tasks. (3) Graph analysis depends more on tool-call quality than quantity. Our findings provide practical insights into the development and evaluation of LLM agents for graph analysis.

</details>


### [65] [Beyond Single-Use Tokens: Durable Authorization State for Replay-Resistant LLM Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01710)
*Jinghan Xu,Longze Fan,Zeyuan Wang,Xinjin Li,Hankai Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 研究LLM agent工具调用中的授权重放防护，提出持久化授权状态机制，对agent可靠性与安全控制有启发。

TL;DR: 仅依赖单次使用令牌不足以防止LLM代理的语义重放；需要持久化授权状态，如CapLease所实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理的重规划、重试、委托和崩溃恢复等行为会导致同一授权被多次执行，即使令牌是单次使用，也存在语义重放漏洞。

Method: 提出CapLease，一种授权消耗层，通过提案和权限级防御，绑定用户确认到规范动作，并采用事务性Issue-Prepare-Commit转换来强制消耗。

Result: 实验结果表明，标识符局部令牌允许语义重放，而CapLease和有状态服务器账本能阻止重复准入，并配合幂等接收器消除重复外部效应。

Conclusion: 抗重放的关键在于维护持久授权状态，而非仅改变令牌表示。

中文摘要：使用工具的大型语言模型代理经常重新规划、重试失败操作、委托任务以及在崩溃后恢复。这些行为可能导致一个用户授权在全新签发的令牌标识符下被多次请求和执行，即使每个单独的令牌都是单次使用的。我们将这种失败称为语义重放：超出令牌无关授权实例的执行预算，而不仅仅是重用旧的令牌标识符。我们表明，除非签发者在授权动作、确认事件和剩余执行预算上保持单调的持久状态，否则标识符局部的令牌消耗无法防止新的重新签发。我们引入了CapLease，一个授权消耗层，它遵循提案级和权限级防御，将经过身份验证的用户确认绑定到规范动作，并强制执行事务性的Issue-Prepare-Commit转换。在LLM代理的重规划、重试、委托、并发、确认重放和崩溃恢复场景中，标识符局部令牌允许新的语义重新签发，而CapLease和同样有状态的服务器账本则阻止重复准入，并在使用幂等接收器时阻止重复外部效应。我们的结果指出，持久的授权状态（而非仅令牌表示）是抗重放代理执行的系统需求。

Original abstract: Tool-using large language model agents frequently replan, retry failed operations, delegate tasks, and resume after crashes. These behaviors can cause one user authorization to be requested and executed multiple times under freshly issued token identifiers, even when each individual token is single-use. We call this failure semantic replay: exceeding the execution budget of a token-independent authorization instance rather than merely reusing an old token identifier. We show that identifier-local token consumption cannot prevent fresh reissuance unless the issuer retains monotonic durable state over the authorized action, confirmation event, and remaining execution budget. We introduce CapLease, an authorization-consumption layer that follows proposal- and authority-level defenses, binds an authenticated user confirmation to a canonical action, and enforces transactional Issue-Prepare-Commit transitions. Across LLM-agent replanning, retry, delegation, concurrency, confirmation-replay, and crash-recovery scenarios, identifier-local tokens permit fresh semantic reissuance, whereas CapLease and an equally stateful Server Ledger prevent duplicate admission and, with an idempotent sink, duplicate external effects. Our results identify durable authorization state, rather than token representation alone, as the systems requirement for replay-resistant agent execution.

</details>


### [66] [Constructing Executable Analytical Knowledge Representations for Meta-Analysis Synthesis Using an Agentic Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01711)
*Lingbo Li,Anuradha Mathrani,Teo Susnjak*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出agentic harness结合确定性验证执行的知识表示框架，其可验证工作流设计可迁移至coding agent。

TL;DR: 提出EAKR表示Meta分析所需分析知识，并通过MetaSynDec智能体框架实现，实验显示其优于直接LLM生成，支持形式化验证与统计执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化证据本身不包含可执行计算所需的分析知识，这些知识必须显式表示。现有自动化方法将决策隐式嵌入模型输出或代码中，缺乏独立可验证性。

Method: 引入EAKR（可执行分析知识表示），并在MetaSynDec框架中实现，结合LLM提出结构化更新和确定性服务进行验证与执行。

Result: 在58个综合单元中，57个执行成功；56个可评估单元中，67.9%完全对象保真，75.0%精确证据集一致，平均Jaccard相似度0.909；置信区间重叠率98.2%；优于直接LLM生成（结构一致性57/58 vs 23/58，公式一致性23/23 vs 1/23，p<0.001）。

Conclusion: EAKR支持形式化验证、可追溯性、统计执行，并相对直接LLM生成提高方法学一致性，为知识驱动的Meta分析提供了可行性证据。

中文摘要：Meta分析综合凸显了基于知识的科学分析中的一个根本挑战：结构化证据本身并不代表可执行计算所需的分析知识。关于证据分配、分析对比、结果和时间点对齐、效应量公式以及方法学可接纳性的决策，必须在统计执行之前明确。现有的自动化方法通常将这些决策嵌入模型输出、生成的代码或工作流痕迹中，而不是将其表示为可独立验证的知识。我们引入了可执行分析知识表示（EAKR），这是一种机器可操作的知识表示，用于将结构化证据转化为可执行的Meta分析。EAKR表示证据、关系、数值输入、约束、来源和未解决问题。我们在MetaSynDec中实现了EAKR，这是一个智能体框架，其中大语言模型提出结构化更新，确定性服务管理基于模式和合约的验证与执行。在58个综合单元中，MetaSynDec构建了所有EAKR，其中57个进入统计执行。在56个有足够信息定义参考分析对象的单元中，38个（67.9%）实现了完全对象保真度，42个（75.0%）实现了精确证据集一致性，平均Jaccard相似度为0.909。生成的和已发表的置信区间在55个单元中的54个（98.2%）重叠。MetaSynDec在参考综合结构一致性方面优于直接LLM生成（57/58对比23/58；p<0.001），在23个联合完成的单元中，精确参考公式一致性为23/23对比1/23（p<0.001）。这些发现提供了可行性证据，表明EAKR支持形式化验证、可追溯性、统计执行，并相对于直接LLM生成提高了方法学一致性。

Original abstract: Meta-analysis synthesis highlights a fundamental challenge in knowledge-based scientific analysis: structured evidence does not by itself represent the analytical knowledge required for executable computation. Decisions about evidence assignment, analytical contrasts, outcome and time-point alignment, effect-size formulation, and methodological admissibility must be explicit before statistical execution. Existing automated approaches often embed these decisions in model outputs, generated code, or workflow traces rather than representing them as independently verifiable knowledge. We introduce the Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation (EAKR), a machine-actionable representation of the knowledge required to transform structured evidence into executable meta-analysis. An EAKR represents evidence, relations, numerical inputs, constraints, provenance, and unresolved issues. We operationalise EAKR in MetaSynDec, an agentic harness in which large language models propose structured updates and deterministic services govern schema- and contract-based validation and execution. Across 58 synthesis units, MetaSynDec constructed all EAKRs, with 57 proceeding to statistical execution. Of 56 units with sufficient information to define a reference analysis object, 38 (67.9%) achieved complete object fidelity and 42 (75.0%) exact evidence-set agreement, with a mean Jaccard similarity of 0.909. Generated and published confidence intervals overlapped in 54 of 55 units (98.2%). MetaSynDec outperformed direct LLM generation in reference synthesis-structure agreement (57/58 versus 23/58; p<0.001) and among 23 jointly completed units, exact reference-formulation agreement (23/23 versus 1/23; p<0.001). These findings provide feasibility evidence that EAKR supports formal validation, traceability, statistical execution, and improved methodological agreement relative to direct LLM generation.

</details>


### [67] [CoEvo-Mem: Co-Evolving Retrieval Policy and Memory Bank for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01739)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Zhijian Li,Xiang Yin,Junkai Ma,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出检索策略与记忆库协同进化的闭环框架，直接改进LLM agent长期记忆与检索能力。

TL;DR: CoEvo-Mem 提出检索策略与记忆库协同进化的闭环框架，在七个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将检索优化和记忆演化分离，忽视了两者之间的反馈回路：检索决定哪些记忆得到使用信号，而记忆更新又反过来影响未来检索。

Method: CoEvo-Mem 使用冻结 LLM 生成查询重写和路由先验，轻量残差路由器在线修正；以检索上下文为耦合接口，通过任务结果更新路由决策，并通过轨迹条件反馈更新记忆值和图关系；通过交替更新路由器（固定记忆库）和演化记忆库（固定检索策略）来缓解非平稳性。

Result: 在七个多样化的基准测试中取得最先进（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: 检索与记忆的协同进化对长期 LLM 智能体至关重要，闭环框架能有效提升性能。

中文摘要：随着记忆在任务和会话间不断积累，长期 LLM 智能体的性能取决于查询特定的检索和持续的记忆精化。然而，现有方法通常要么优化记忆访问（通过迭代查询精化或自适应检索策略），要么优化记忆演化（如结构更新）。这种分离忽略了一个基本反馈回路：检索决定哪些记忆获得使用信号，而更新后的记忆库重塑未来的检索。我们提出 CoEvo-Mem，一个用于协同进化检索策略和记忆库的闭环框架。对于每个查询，一个冻结的 LLM 生成路由特定的查询重写和路由先验，轻量级残差路由器在线纠正这些信息。检索到的上下文作为两个学习过程之间的耦合接口：任务结果将信用分配给路由决策，而轨迹条件反馈更新记忆值和图关系。这些更新改变记忆在后续查询中的排序和选择方式，从而闭环反馈回路。为了缓解耦合引起的非平稳性，CoEvo-Mem 在固定记忆库时交替更新路由器，并在固定检索策略时演化记忆库。在七个不同的基准测试中，CoEvo-Mem 取得了最先进的性能，证明了检索-记忆协同进化的重要性。

Original abstract: As memories accumulate across tasks and sessions, the performance of long-term LLM agents depends jointly on query-specific retrieval and continual memory refinement. However, existing methods typically optimize either memory access, through iterative query refinement or adaptive retrieval policies, or memory evolution such as structural update. This separation overlooks a fundamental feedback loop: retrieval determines which memories receive usage signals, while updated memory bank reshape future retrieval. We propose \textbf{CoEvo-Mem}, a closed-loop framework for co-evolving the retrieval policy and memory bank. For each query, a frozen LLM generates route-specific query rewrites and a routing prior, which a lightweight residual router corrects online. The retrieved context serves as the coupling interface between the two learning processes: task outcomes assign credit to routing decisions, while trajectory-conditioned feedback updates memory values and graph relations. These updates alter how memories are ranked and selected for subsequent queries, thereby closing the feedback loop. To mitigate coupling induced non-stationarity, CoEvo-Mem alternates between updating the router with the memory bank fixed and evolving the memory bank with the retrieval policy fixed. Across seven diverse benchmarks, \textbf{CoEvo-Mem} achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the importance of retrieval-memory coevolution.

</details>


### [68] [MemSIF: From Structured Interactions to Dual-Track Fact Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01742)
*YuFei Luo,Xiucheng Xu,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Submitted to AAAI 2027. 19 pages, 10 figures, 18 tables

Selection reason: 设计结构化交互到事实记忆的双轨框架，缓解长期agent记忆错位问题，可迁移至长程coding agent。

TL;DR: 提出MemSIF框架，通过结构化交互记忆和双轨事实记忆解决长期记忆中的时间-结构错位和延迟效用显现问题，在多个基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的长期记忆系统存在两个核心错位问题：时间-结构错位（TSM）和延迟效用显现（DUM），导致记忆组织与查询需求不匹配，影响长期交互性能。

Method: MemSIF框架包含结构化交互记忆（将交互组织为主题片段和事件轨迹）和双轨事实记忆（核心事实记忆与活跃事实记忆互补），以缓解TSM和DUM。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上，基于五种骨干LLM，MemSIF在所有设置中达到最高总准确率，分别超过最强基线2.29%-8.79%和2.87%-6.15%。

Conclusion: 实验验证了结构化交互记忆与双轨事实记忆相结合的有效性，能够显著缓解TSM和DUM，提升长期记忆性能。

中文摘要：长期记忆对于在长时间跨度的交互中运作的大型语言模型代理至关重要。然而，现有记忆系统的几个持续存在的局限性可追溯到长时间交互设置中两种反复出现的错位模式：时间-结构错位（TSM）和延迟效用显现（DUM）。TSM 出现在时间邻近性与主题或事件级别的关联性不能可靠对齐时，而 DUM 出现在写入时显著性与未来查询效用不能可靠预测时。为缓解这些错位模式，我们提出了 MemSIF（具有结构化交互和事实的记忆），一种结构化的交互到事实记忆框架。结构化交互记忆将原始交互组织为保持局部主题连贯性的主题片段和维护跨时间事件连续性的事件轨迹。双轨事实记忆使用两条互补的轨道：核心事实记忆在写入时整合稳定的、模式引导的信息，而活跃事实记忆按需形成事实，并提升那些由多个历史来源和反复出现的查询需求支持的事实以供重用。在五种骨干 LLM 上的 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 实验表明，MemSIF 在所有设置中均达到最高的总准确率，在 LoCoMo 上超过最强基线 2.29%-8.79%，在 LongMemEval-S 上超过 2.87%-6.15%。这些结果支持将结构化交互记忆与双轨事实记忆结合以缓解 TSM 和 DUM 的有效性。代码可在 https://github.com/luoyufeihaha/MemSIF 获取。

Original abstract: Long-term memory is critical for LLM agents operating over long-horizon interactions. However, several persistent limitations of existing memory systems can be traced to two recurring misalignment patterns in long-term interaction settings: Temporal-Structural Misalignment (TSM) and Delayed Utility Manifestation (DUM). TSM arises when temporal proximity does not reliably align with topical or event-level relatedness, whereas DUM arises when write-time salience does not reliably predict future query utility. To mitigate these misalignment patterns, we propose MemSIF (Memory with Structured Interactions and Facts), a structured interaction-to-fact memory framework. Structured Interaction Memory organizes raw interactions into Topical Segments that preserve local topical coherence and Event Trajectories that maintain cross-time event continuity. Dual-Track Fact Memory uses two complementary tracks: CoreFact memory consolidates stable, schema-guided information at write time, whereas ActiveFact memory forms facts on demand and promotes those supported by multiple historical sources and recurring query demand for reuse. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S across five backbone LLMs show that MemSIF achieves the highest Total ACC in all settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.29%-8.79% on LoCoMo and 2.87%-6.15% on LongMemEval-S. These results support the effectiveness of combining Structured Interaction Memory with Dual-Track Fact Memory to mitigate TSM and DUM. Code is available at https://github.com/luoyufeihaha/MemSIF.

</details>


### [69] [Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for Verifiable Reasoning in Autonomous Driving VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01755)
*Zixuan Huang,Yang Zhou,Kaixuan Wang,Guli Zhang,Hongyan Xie,Yakun Zhu,Hao Geng,Xiaozhi Chen,Yikun Ban,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 将RLVR与轨迹延迟暴露结合以提升可验证推理，其训练机制可迁移至agent推理验证场景。

TL;DR: 提出AD-MCQ和DEFT-RLVR，通过将未来轨迹从决策前锚点转为决策后验证目标，避免轨迹锚定偏差，提升自动驾驶推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在CoT监督中暴露真实未来轨迹，导致教师模型产生轨迹锚定偏差，降低推理因果保真度并加剧幻觉；直接移除轨迹又使决策与开放轨迹生成纠缠不清，难以验证。

Method: 引入AD-MCQ将规划转为轨迹候选选择；提出DEFT-RLVR将未来轨迹延迟为决策后验证目标，结合RLVR实现可验证的推理。

Result: DEFT-RLVR提升了AD推理能力，同时保持或增强通用视觉能力；AD-MCQ支持VLM-only推理和可控难度。

Conclusion: AD-MCQ与DEFT-RLVR为可验证的自动驾驶推理提供了灵活、可扩展且可扩展的基础，有望推动未来研究。

中文摘要：近年来，用于自动驾驶（AD）的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型越来越多地利用思维链（CoT）监督来增强其视觉-语言模型（VLM）组件的推理能力，然而现有的标注流程通常会让教师模型接触记录在案的真实（GT）未来轨迹。我们通过实验证明，这会导致轨迹锚定偏差：教师模型会合理化已揭示的结果，而不是从场景证据中推断决策，从而产生因果保真度较低的思维链，并显著加剧幻觉，尤其是在因果挑战性场景中。移除GT轨迹可以消除这一捷径，但开放式轨迹生成会将高层决策与精确的几何合成和低层动态纠缠在一起。为了使轨迹层面的驾驶决策无需开放式轨迹合成即可验证，我们引入了自动驾驶多项选择题（AD-MCQ），将规划转化为对显式轨迹候选的选择。更进一步，我们提出了用于RLVR的未来轨迹延迟暴露（DEFT-RLVR），将未来轨迹从决策前的锚点转变为决策后的验证目标。实验结果表明，DEFT-RLVR在提升AD推理能力的同时，保持甚至增强了通用视觉能力。凭借仅使用VLM的推理以及通过候选构建实现的可控难度，AD-MCQ为未来可验证AD推理的研究提供了一个灵活、可扩展且可扩展的基础。

Original abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving (AD) increasingly utilize chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision to enhance the reasoning capabilities of their Vision-Language Model (VLM) components, yet existing annotation pipelines commonly expose the teacher model to the logged ground-truth (GT) future trajectory. We empirically show that this induces trajectory anchoring bias: teacher models rationalize the revealed outcome rather than infer a decision from scene evidence, producing less causally faithful CoTs and substantially more severe hallucinations, especially in causally challenging scenes. Removing the GT trajectory eliminates this shortcut, but open-ended trajectory generation entangles high-level decision-making with precise geometric synthesis and low-level dynamics. To make trajectory-level driving decisions verifiable without requiring open-ended trajectory synthesis, we introduce Autonomous-Driving Multiple-Choice Question (AD-MCQ), which casts planning as selection among explicit trajectory candidates. Taking this a step further, we propose Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for RLVR (DEFT-RLVR) to transform future trajectories from pre-decision anchors into post-decision verification targets. Experimental results show that DEFT-RLVR improves AD reasoning while preserving or even enhancing general visual capabilities. With VLM-only inference and controllable difficulty through candidate construction, AD-MCQ provides a flexible, scalable, and extensible foundation for future research on verifiable AD reasoning.

</details>


### [70] [FRAMES: Guarded and Dual-Objective Skill Evolution for Agents in Policy-Governed Enterprise Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01772)
*Xuhui Wang,Ruoqi Shu,Chen Dan,Tianhua Xu,Mengxi Luo,Yanming Mai,Bo Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 面向企业工作流的agent技能进化框架，含共识突变、Pareto选择与防回退，可直接用于coding agent。

TL;DR: FRAMES是一个闭环框架，通过共识变异、帕累托选择和防回归保证，从现有资产冷启动并进化LLM智能体技能，在内部生产系统和tau-bench上取得最佳准确率-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在受策略约束的企业工作流（如文档审计）中面临改进困难：反馈稀疏未标记、规则编辑易导致无关案例回归、以及在控制推理成本和保持可审计性的前提下提升准确性。

Method: FRAMES采用闭环框架：从现有资产冷启动技能，通过共识变异产生候选，基于准确性和成本进行帕累托选择，并引入防回归保证，全程保持可审计性。

Result: 在内部生产系统上，FRAMES获得基线中最佳的准确性-成本权衡，并在tau-bench上复现相同增益。

Conclusion: FRAMES能有效改进策略约束的LLM智能体工作流，兼顾准确性、成本和可审计性，具有实际部署价值。

中文摘要：LLM智能体越来越多地运行受策略约束的企业工作流，例如文档审计，在这些工作流中，它们必须一致地应用规则、验证每个值，并保持可审计性。改进这些智能体很困难：操作反馈稀疏且未标记，对一条规则的编辑可能会使无关案例回归，而且准确性必须在不增加推理成本或失去可审计性的情况下提高。我们提出了FRAMES，一个闭环框架，它从现有资产中冷启动可部署的技能，然后通过基于共识的变异、准确性和成本之间的帕累托选择以及防回归保证来进化它们，同时保持可审计性。在我们内部生产系统上部署后，FRAMES在所有基线中实现了最佳的准确性-成本权衡，并且在tau-bench上复现了相同的收益。

Original abstract: LLM agents increasingly run policy-bound enterprise workflows such as document auditing, where they must apply rules consistently, ground every value, and stay auditable. Improving these agents is hard: operational feedback is sparse and unlabeled, edits to one rule can regress unrelated cases, and accuracy must improve without inflating inference cost or losing auditability. We present FRAMES, a closed-loop framework that cold-starts deployable skills from existing assets and then evolves them through consensus-based mutation, Pareto selection over accuracy and cost, and an anti-regression guarantee, all while preserving auditability. Deployed on our internal production system, FRAMES attains the best accuracy-cost trade-off among baselines, with the same gains reproduced on tau-bench.

</details>


### [71] [CoNav-UAV: Cooperative Dual-Altitude Aerial Navigation via Stackelberg Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01802)
*Junru Song,Wenhao Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuekai Qiu,Feifei Wang,Weien Zhou,Tingsong Jiang,Ying Wen,Yang Li,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 多UAV导航中的Stackelberg协作学习框架，多agent协作方法有潜在可迁移性。

TL;DR: 本文提出CoNav-UAV，将空中视觉-语言导航建模为Stackelberg博弈，通过迭代学习协调高空探索与低空接近，显著提升成功率并减少适应数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中VLN方法要么依赖外部辅助导致探索低效，要么使用双无人机但依赖特权信息且独立训练，无法实现协同。需要一种仅依靠机载输入、能内在地协调全局探索与近距离接近的自主方法。

Method: 提出CoNav-UAV，将任务建模为高空领导者与低空跟随者之间的Stackelberg博弈，并设计迭代Stackelberg学习：领导者通过记忆增强的上下文学习优化视觉-语言推理，跟随者通过DAgger式专家蒸馏更新运动控制，两者交替优化直至均衡。

Result: 在AerialVLN基准的三个城市场景中，CoNav-UAV一致优于单双智能体基线：训练场景成功率提升最高30.8个点，跨场景迁移提升9.0个点，且适应数据减少约3倍；消融验证了双智能体更新的互补性及不同VLM骨干下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式建模双智能体交互为Stackelberg博弈并采用交替学习，能有效融合高空全局推理与低空精确控制，为空中VLN提供了一种无需特权信息、数据高效的协同方案。

中文摘要：面向目标的视觉-语言导航（VLN）在航空平台上正日益受到关注，应用于灾害救援、基础设施检查和安全巡逻等任务。在该任务中，无人机（UAV）需要仅根据目标外观和周围环境的简洁描述来定位目标。这需要全局探索与定位以及无碰撞的近距离接近，这两个相互交织的过程难以在单个智能体中协调。大多数现有方法将地面VLN范式迁移到低空无人机，并借助外部辅助来弥补其低效的探索。最近的一项尝试在互补高度部署两架无人机，但仍依赖特权信息，并独立训练其两个智能体，排除了合作所必需的相互适应。本文提出CoNav-UAV，明确将该任务建模为高空领导者与低空跟随者之间的Stackelberg博弈，系统仅依靠机载视觉和语言输入运行。为求解该博弈，我们引入了迭代Stackelberg学习。领导者的高层视觉-语言推理通过基于记忆的上下文学习进行优化，而跟随者的精确运动控制则通过DAgger风格的专家蒸馏进行更新。这种交替驱动两个智能体趋向Stackelberg均衡。CoNav-UAV在AerialVLN基准的三个高保真城市场景中一致优于单智能体和双智能体基线。在训练场景中成功率提升高达30.8个百分点，在跨场景迁移下提升9.0个百分点，同时使用的适应数据约为原来的三分之一。进一步分析验证了领导者和跟随者更新的互补增益，并揭示了跨VLM骨干网络的稳健增益但不同的学习动态。

Original abstract: Target-oriented vision-and-language navigation (VLN) on aerial platforms is attracting growing attention for missions such as disaster rescue, infrastructure inspection, and security patrol. In this task, an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) needs to locate targets given only a concise description of their appearance and surroundings. This requires global exploration and grounding as well as collision-free close-range approach, two interleaved processes difficult to reconcile within a single agent. Most existing methods transfer the ground VLN paradigm to a low-altitude UAV and compensate for its inefficient exploration with external assistance. A recent attempt deploys two UAVs at complementary altitudes yet still relies on privileged information and trains its two agents independently, precluding any mutual adaptation essential for cooperation. Here we propose CoNav-UAV, which explicitly models the task as a Stackelberg game between a high-altitude leader and a low-altitude follower, with the system operating on onboard visual and linguistic inputs alone. To solve this game, we introduce Iterative Stackelberg Learning. The leader's high-level vision-language reasoning is refined via memory-based in-context learning, while the follower's precise motion control is updated via DAgger-style expert distillation. The alternation drives both agents toward a Stackelberg equilibrium. CoNav-UAV consistently outperforms single- and dual-agent baselines across three high-fidelity urban scenes from the AerialVLN benchmark. Success rate improves by up to 30.8 points on the learning scene, and 9.0 points under cross-scene transfer while using about 3x less adaptation data. Further analyses validate the complementary gains of the leader and follower updates and reveal robust gains yet distinct learning dynamics across VLM backbones.

</details>


### [72] [CockpitHAT: Dependency-Graph-Driven Hierarchical Attribution for Embodied Multi-Agent Cockpits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01805)
*Wei Wang,Shuanghe Liu,Zhu Zhuo,Jiaqi Zhong,Xiaozhao Zhao,Xiaojie Zuo,Jie Su*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 面向多agent系统的层次化故障归因方法，可迁移至coding agent的调试与诊断。

TL;DR: 提出CockpitHAT分层归因框架，通过依赖DAG、多通道证据和安全加权共识解决LLM多智能体系统的正确性崩溃问题，并发布CockpitBench基准，在多个数据集上显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统存在Correctness Collapse现象：任务级准确率高但过程级失败严重，尤其在汽车座舱等安全关键场景中，文本上正确的输出可能引发危险操作。现有归因方法仅基于文本轨迹，忽略依赖结构、多通道证据和安全评估，导致无法有效诊断过程级失败。

Method: CockpitHAT：1）用交互DAG的依赖距离阈值替代位置窗口进行分层归因；2）通过具身适配器整合多通道证据（对话、车辆状态、环境、记忆）；3）在置信度加权分析者共识中对高风险失败应用安全提升。同时构建CockpitBench基准，含212条标注ISO 26262 ASIL严重度的失败轨迹。

Result: 在Who&When基准上，CockpitHAT在Hand-Crafted分割上取得77.9%智能体级和37.8%步骤精确准确率；在Algorithm-Generated分割上为86.5%和46.0%，比SOTA ECHO最高提升17.6/16.7个百分点。在CockpitBench上取得78.3%智能体级和38.2%步骤精确准确率。

Conclusion: 依赖感知、多通道、风险校准的归因方法为真实世界具身LLM多智能体系统提供了有效的可靠故障诊断范式，显著优于纯文本归因方法。

中文摘要：LLM多智能体系统存在正确性崩溃（Correctness Collapse）问题，即高任务级准确率掩盖了严重的过程级失败。这在安全关键的具身场景（如汽车座舱）中尤其危险，因为词汇上正确的表述可能触发危险的实际操作。现有归因方法仅依赖文本痕迹，缺乏依赖结构、多通道证据和安全感知评估。我们提出CockpitHAT，一种分层归因框架，用交互DAG中的依赖距离阈值取代位置窗口，通过具身适配器整合多通道证据，并在置信度加权分析者共识中对高风险失败应用安全提升。我们还发布了CockpitBench，一个包含212条带注释失败轨迹的基准，涵盖对话、车辆状态、环境和记忆通道，每条轨迹均通过三位专家共识标注ISO 26262 ASIL严重度。在公开的Who&When基准上，CockpitHAT在Hand-Crafted分割上达到77.9%的智能体级准确率和37.8%的步骤精确准确率，在Algorithm-Generated分割上达到86.5%和46.0%，比纯文本SOTA ECHO最多高出17.6/16.7个百分点。在CockpitBench上，它达到78.3%的智能体级准确率和38.2%的步骤精确准确率。这些结果确立了依赖感知、多通道、风险校准的归因方法作为真实世界具身LLM多智能体系统中可靠故障诊断的有效范式。

Original abstract: LLM multi-agent systems suffer from Correctness Collapse, where high task-level accuracy conceals severe process-level failures. This is especially hazardous in safety-critical embodied settings such as automotive cockpits, where lexically correct utterances may trigger dangerous physical operations. Existing attribution methods rely on text traces alone, missing dependency structure, multi-channel evidence, and safety-aware evaluation. We introduce CockpitHAT, a hierarchical attribution framework that replaces positional windows with dependency-distance thresholds from interaction DAGs, integrates multi-channel evidence via an embodied adapter, and applies a safety-uplift to high-risk failures during confidence-weighted analyst consensus. We further release CockpitBench, a benchmark of 212 annotated failure traces spanning dialogue, vehicle-state, environmental, and memory channels, each labeled with ISO 26262 ASIL severity via three-expert consensus. On the public Who&When benchmark, CockpitHAT achieves agent-level / step-exact accuracies of 77.9% / 37.8% on the Hand-Crafted split and 86.5% / 46.0% on the Algorithm-Generated split, surpassing the text-only SOTA ECHO by up to 17.6 / 16.7 points. On CockpitBench, it attains 78.3% agent-level and 38.2% step-exact accuracy. These results establish dependency-aware, multi-channel, risk-calibrated attribution as an effective paradigm for reliable failure diagnosis in real-world embodied LLM multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [73] [SearchMaster: Grounded and Regulated Self-Play for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01822)
*Wentao Tan,Qiong Cao,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 自博弈框架训练搜索agent，含任务生成与工具使用控制，对训练coding agent有借鉴价值。

TL;DR: SearchMaster是一个自博弈框架，通过生成、解决和验证搜索任务来训练搜索智能体，并引入三种控制机制（ECG、SDR、OOP）解决自生成数据中的误导信号，显著提升模型在深度搜索基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体训练依赖人工任务、专家示范或更强教师模型，成本高且难以扩展。而自生成数据虽具潜力，但存在伪多跳问题、难度评估忽视搜索深度、以及过度打开文档却无效浏览等失败模式，导致训练信号质量下降。

Method: SearchMaster采用自博弈方式，让单一LLM在本地搜索环境中生成、解决和验证任务。具体包含三种控制机制：证据链生成器（ECG）确保任务基于跨文档证据链生成；搜索深度奖励（SDR）根据成功轨迹的搜索深度评估任务难度；过度打开惩罚（OOP）限制无意义的文档打开。最后通过GRPO联合优化验证后的提议者和求解者轨迹。

Result: 在六个深度搜索基准上，SearchMaster将Qwen3.5-9B的平均准确率从38.19%提升至51.52%，其中在BrowseComp-Plus上提升了30.1个百分点，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SearchMaster证明，通过有依据且受监管的自博弈，无需人工标注或专家示范即可生成高质量搜索智能体训练数据，为搜索智能体训练提供了可扩展的新范式。

中文摘要：训练基于LLM的搜索智能体需要高质量的搜索数据：即需要真正多跳检索的任务，以及有效使用搜索工具的行为轨迹。现有流程通常依赖人工编写的任务、专家示范或更强的教师模型。我们提出了SearchMaster，一种自博弈框架，它训练单个LLM，使其在本地搜索环境中生成、解决并验证搜索任务。关键挑战在于，自生成的任务和轨迹可能产生误导性信号：伪多跳问题、忽略搜索深度的基于成功率的难度估计，以及过度打开文档但缺乏针对性证据收集的轨迹。SearchMaster通过三种控制机制解决这些失败模式。证据链生成器（ECG）将任务生成锚定在显式的跨文档证据链上，以减少伪多跳问题。搜索深度奖励（SDR）通过成功轨迹的搜索深度而非仅靠成功率来评分任务难度，从而保持保留任务的搜索密集性。过度打开惩罚（OOP）通过阻止过度打开文档来规范工具使用，避免冗长但浅层的浏览。然后，经过验证的提议者和求解者轨迹通过GRPO进行联合优化。在六个深度搜索基准上，SearchMaster将Qwen3.5-9B骨干网络的平均准确率从38.19%提升到51.52%，在BrowseComp-Plus上提升了30.1分。这些结果表明，有依据且受监管的自博弈可以在无需人工标注的问答对或专家示范的情况下，提供有效的搜索智能体训练数据。代码可在https://github.com/WentaoTan/SearchMaster获取。

Original abstract: Training LLM-based search agents requires high-quality search data: tasks that demand genuine multi-hop retrieval and trajectories that use search tools effectively. Existing pipelines often depend on human-written tasks, expert demonstrations, or stronger teacher models. We present SearchMaster, a self-play framework that trains a single LLM from search tasks it generates, solves, and verifies in a local search environment. The key challenge is that self-generated tasks and rollouts can yield misleading signals: pseudo multi-hop questions, success-rate difficulty estimates that ignore search depth, and rollouts with excessive opening but little targeted evidence acquisition. SearchMaster addresses these failure modes with three controls. An Evidence-Chain Generator (ECG) grounds task generation in explicit cross-document evidence chains to reduce pseudo multi-hop questions. A Search-Depth Reward (SDR) scores task difficulty by the search depth of successful rollouts rather than success rate alone, keeping retained tasks search-intensive. An Over-Opening Penalty (OOP) regulates tool use by discouraging excessive document opening, avoiding long but shallow browsing. Verified Proposer and Solver rollouts are then jointly optimized with GRPO. Across six deep-search benchmarks, SearchMaster improves a Qwen3.5-9B backbone from 38.19% to 51.52% average accuracy, with a 30.1-point gain on BrowseComp-Plus. These results show that grounded and regulated self-play can provide effective search-agent training data without human-labeled QA pairs or expert demonstrations. The code is available at https://github.com/WentaoTan/SearchMaster.

</details>


### [74] [PCSD: Persistent Consistency for Self-Distillation in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01837)
*Chunji Lv,Yangguang Wei,Junlin Liu,Yang Gao,Ming Liu,Xinming Wang,Jinyang Wu,Guoren Wang,Changsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 改进agentic RL中的自蒸馏权重分配，与GRPO结合，直接适用于coding agent强化学习训练。

TL;DR: 提出PCSD，利用教师信号的局部持久性生成令牌级蒸馏权重，并与GRPO联合优化，在ALFWorld上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在长轨迹中面临稀疏奖励，而现有的OPSD教师可能不可靠，且隔离的令牌级差异和共享步骤级权重分别存在噪声敏感和忽略位置变化的问题。

Method: PCSD从教师偏好信号的局部持久性中推导令牌级权重，使用自适应窗口和指数衰减聚合捕捉持久支持，通过趋势感知调制抑制局部下降，并用sigmoid门控生成连续权重，最终与GRPO联合优化。

Result: PCSD在两个骨干上取得ALFWorld Overall最佳结果，超过GRPO 15.6和13.3分，超过SDAR 6.2和5.5分；在WebShop上保持竞争力，并在未见ALFWorld划分上比GRPO高15.8分。

Conclusion: PCSD有效结合密集教师指导与稀疏环境反馈，显著提升了智能体在复杂交互任务中的性能，尤其优于现有蒸馏方法和GRPO基线。

中文摘要：大语言模型智能体在复杂交互任务中展现出强大潜力，但其强化学习（RL）常常受到稀疏奖励的阻碍，因为一个长的多轮轨迹可能只获得单一的结果级信号。在策略自蒸馏（OPSD）从特权教师提供密集的令牌级监督，但教师并非在每个位置都可靠。现有方法通常依赖孤立的令牌级差异，这可能对噪声敏感，或分配共享的步骤级权重，可能忽略位置变化。我们提出持久一致性自蒸馏（PCSD），该方法从教师偏好信号的局部持久性中推导令牌级蒸馏权重。PCSD结合自适应窗口与指数衰减聚合来捕捉持久的相对教师支持，应用趋势感知调制来衰减局部下降的支持，并通过sigmoid门控产生连续权重。所得目标与GRPO联合优化，将密集教师指导与稀疏环境反馈相结合。在没有推理时技能的情况下，PCSD在两个骨干上均取得所有基线中最佳的ALFWorld Overall结果，超过GRPO 15.6和13.3分，超过SDAR 6.2和5.5分，同时在WebShop上保持竞争力，并在未见ALFWorld划分上超过GRPO 15.8分。

Original abstract: Large language model agents have shown strong potential in complex interactive tasks, yet their reinforcement learning (RL) is often hindered by sparse rewards, as a long multi-turn trajectory may receive only a single outcome-level signal. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense token-level supervision from a privileged teacher, but the teacher may not be reliable at every position. Existing methods commonly rely on isolated token-level discrepancies, which can be sensitive to noise, or assign a shared step-level weight that may overlook positional variation. We propose Persistent Consistency Self-Distillation (PCSD), which derives token-level distillation weights from the local persistence of teacher-favoring signals. PCSD combines adaptive windows with exponentially decayed aggregation to capture persistent relative teacher support, applies trend-aware modulation to attenuate locally declining support, and produces continuous weights through sigmoid gating. The resulting objective is jointly optimized with GRPO, combining dense teacher guidance with sparse environmental feedback. Without inference-time skills, PCSD achieves the best ALFWorld Overall results among all baselines on both backbones, exceeding GRPO by 15.6 and 13.3 points and SDAR by 6.2 and 5.5 points, while remaining competitive on WebShop and gaining 15.8 points over GRPO on unseen ALFWorld split.

</details>


### [75] [CoEvoKG: Co-Evolving Knowledge Graphs with Self-Evolving Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01904)
*Zhaoyang Li,Zenghuang Fu,Qiuyuan Ai,Ping Jiang,Haoyu Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Jie Song,Guannan He*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 10 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 提出自进化搜索 agent 与知识图谱共同演化，将成功搜索证据写回图谱作为持久记忆，可迁移到 agent 经验复用。

TL;DR: CoEvoKG 是一个利用知识图谱作为任务来源和证据记忆的框架，通过联合训练任务生成器和搜索智能体，并在成功搜索后将证据写回图谱，形成闭环进化，在多个问答基准上显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自对弈搜索智能体在反复生成任务时丢弃了成功搜索中获得的知识，导致知识无法积累和利用，限制了模型的持续进化。

Method: CoEvoKG 将知识图谱同时用作可验证训练任务的来源和持久证据记忆。它联合训练一个任务生成器（从知识图谱的实体链采样生成多跳问题）和一个搜索智能体（从答案正确性奖励和基于图证据的搜索轨迹中学习）。搜索成功后，验证并去重证据，写回图谱节点和边，未来轮次重用增强后的图谱进行任务生成和奖励计算。

Result: 在六个问答基准上，使用三个骨干模型（Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct），宏观平均准确率分别比基础模型提高 +11.2、+10.1、+11.6 点；在匹配训练预算下，比竞争的自对弈基线和 RL 基线提高 +2.6 到 +3.7 宏观平均点。

Conclusion: CoEvoKG 通过将知识积累与模型自我进化结合，有效提升了搜索智能体的性能，证明了知识图谱作为证据记忆的持久价值，并提供了开源代码以促进进一步研究。

中文摘要：大型语言模型可以通过强化学习来改进搜索智能体，然而现有的自对弈智能体会反复生成任务，同时丢弃在成功搜索过程中获得的知识。我们提出了 CoEvoKG，一个将知识图谱同时转化为可验证训练任务来源和智能体演化的持久证据记忆的框架。CoEvoKG 联合训练一个任务生成器和一个搜索智能体：生成器从知识图谱中采样的实体链创建多跳问题，而智能体则从答案正确性的奖励以及其搜索轨迹（实体路径由图谱证据支持）中学习。当搜索成功时，CoEvoKG 会验证并去重检索到的证据，然后将其写回对应的图节点和边。未来的轮次重复使用这个增强后的图谱进行任务生成和奖励计算，从而在模型自我进化与知识积累之间形成闭环。在六个问答基准（NQ、TriviaQA、PopQA、HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA 和 Bamboogle）上使用三个骨干模型的实验表明，CoEvoKG 在 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 和 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上分别将宏观平均准确率相比对应基础模型提高了 +11.2、+10.1 和 +11.6 个百分点。在匹配的训练预算下，CoEvoKG 在三个骨干网络上进一步比竞争性的自对弈基线和搜索智能体的强化学习基线提高了 +2.6 到 +3.7 个宏观平均点。代码可在 https://github.com/lazzy1225/CoEvoKG 获取。

Original abstract: Large language models can improve with reinforcement learning for search agents, yet existing self play agents repeatedly generate tasks while discarding the knowledge gained during successful searches. We introduce CoEvoKG, a framework that turns a
  knowledge graph into both a source of verifiable training tasks and a persistent evidence memory for agent evolution. CoEvoKG jointly trains a task generator and a search agent: the generator creates multihop questions from entity chains sampled
  from the knowledge graph, while the agent learns from rewards for answer correctness and search trajectories whose entity paths are supported by graph evidence. When a search succeeds, CoEvoKG verifies and deduplicates the retrieved evidence, then
  writes it back to the corresponding graph nodes and edges. Future rounds reuse this enriched graph for task generation and reward computation, closing the loop between model self evolution and knowledge accumulation. Experiments on six QA benchmarks
  (NQ, TriviaQA, PopQA, HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and Bamboogle) with three backbone models show that CoEvoKG improves macro average accuracy over the corresponding base models by +11.2, +10.1, and +11.6 points on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct,
  Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, respectively. Under matched training budgets, CoEvoKG further improves over competitive self play baselines and RL baselines for search agents by +2.6 to +3.7 macro average points across the three
  backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/lazzy1225/CoEvoKG.

</details>


### [76] [Diagnosing Search Behavior and Failure Modes in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01913)
*Qi Liu,Jiaxin Mao,Fengbin Zhu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 对长程搜索 agent 进行轨迹级失败模式诊断，区分检索缺口与利用缺口并提供停止准则方向，对 agent 可靠性评测有直接价值。

TL;DR: 本文通过轨迹级诊断研究深度搜索代理，发现搜索努力与答案质量弱相关，而证据质量（尤其累积召回率）与答案准确性更相关；失败可分解为检索缺口和利用缺口，并指出改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索代理在回答复杂问题时通过多次搜索收集证据，但搜索努力与最终答案质量之间的关系尚不明确，缺乏系统性诊断以理解搜索行为如何影响答案准确性。

Method: 采用轨迹级诊断，利用人工标注的文档级相关性判断，将代理行为分为证据检索和证据使用两个阶段，并分解失败为检索缺口和利用缺口；在固定检索模型和评估框架下，比较六个代理在BrowseComp-Plus上的表现，并在BrowseComp上通过开放网络搜索API验证。

Result: 搜索努力与答案质量仅弱对齐；答案准确性与累积检索召回率等证据质量指标相关性更强，而非搜索次数或上下文量；有用证据常出现在早期，但代理继续搜索产生长尾低收益步骤；冗余查询减少与更好性能相关。

Conclusion: 系统地表征了长时程搜索代理的行为和失败模式，指出构建更好的深度研究系统的实用方向：加强查询表述、改进证据选择与上下文管理，以及基于证据充分性的停止标准。

中文摘要：深度搜索代理通过迭代发出搜索查询来收集支持性证据，从而回答困难的信息寻求问题，但目前仍不清楚更大的搜索努力是否以及如何带来更好的答案。我们通过对长时程搜索代理的轨迹级诊断来研究这些问题。利用人工标注的文档级相关性判断，我们评估每个搜索步骤检索到的证据，并将代理行为分为两个阶段：代理检索到什么证据，以及它如何有效地使用该证据。这种区分进一步使我们能够将失败分解为检索缺口（即从未找到必要证据）和利用缺口（即检索到相关证据但未正确使用）。在保持检索模型和评估框架固定的情况下，我们比较了六个代理在BrowseComp-Plus上的表现，并通过开放网络搜索API在BrowseComp上进一步验证了我们的发现。在各种设置中，我们发现搜索努力和答案质量仅弱对齐。答案准确性与检索证据的质量（尤其是累积检索召回率）的相关性，高于与搜索次数或消耗的上下文量的相关性。有用的证据通常出现在轨迹早期，但代理倾向于继续搜索，产生低收益检索步骤的长尾。在查询层面，探索性重新表述仍然有用，但表现最好的代理发出的冗余查询要少得多。总体而言，通过系统地表征长时程搜索代理的搜索行为和失败模式，这项工作为构建更好的深度研究系统指出了实用方向，包括更强的查询表述、更有效的证据选择和上下文管理，以及基于是否已检索到足够支持性证据的停止标准。

Original abstract: Deep search agents answer difficult information-seeking questions by iteratively issuing search queries to gather supporting evidence, but it remains unclear whether and how greater search effort leads to better answers. We study these questions through a trajectory-level diagnosis of long-horizon search agents. Using human-annotated document-level relevance judgments, we evaluate the evidence retrieved at each search step and separate two stages of agent behavior: what evidence an agent retrieves and how effectively it uses that evidence. This distinction further allows us to decompose failures into retrieval gaps, where the necessary evidence is never found, and utilization gaps, where relevant evidence is retrieved but not used correctly. With the retrieval model and evaluation harness held fixed, we compare six agents on BrowseComp-Plus and further validate our findings on BrowseComp with an open-web search API. Across settings, we find that search effort and answer quality are only weakly aligned. Answer accuracy is better correlated with the quality of retrieved evidence, especially cumulative retrieval recall, than with the number of searches or the amount of context consumed. Useful evidence often appears early in the trajectory, yet agents tend to continue searching, producing a long tail of low-yield retrieval steps. At the query level, exploratory reformulations remain useful, but the best-performing agents issue far fewer redundant queries. Overall, by systematically characterizing the search behavior and failure modes of long-horizon search agents, this work points to practical directions for building better deep research systems, including stronger query formulation, more effective evidence selection and context management, and stopping criteria based on whether sufficient supporting evidence has been retrieved.

</details>


### [77] [Long-Horizon Autonomous Architecture Research with a Language-Model Agent: A Behavioural Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01995)
*Aon Safdar,Mohamed Saadeldin*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication. Copyright may be transferred without notice, after which this version may no longer be accessible

Selection reason: 单个 LLM agent 进行长程自主架构研究的实证案例，揭示 workflow 设计比 agent 能力更影响产出，对自主科研 agent 设计有直接启发。

TL;DR: 单个通用LLM作为唯一研究者，在约100个连续实验中自主改进Vision Transformer，发现工作流设计比模型能力更关键，并提出未来自主研究的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索单个通用大语言模型在长周期神经架构设计中扮演唯一研究者角色时的行为、产出和局限性。

Method: 设置一个具备实验管理、跟踪、文献访问和持久记忆的自主代理，分三阶段逐步扩展工具面和问题规模，进行约100个顺序实验，并分析其行为轨迹。

Result: 代理将弱基线改进为小基准上的高效模型和ImageNet-1K上可用的模型；发现生产力阶段结构、早期假设主导收益、贪婪偏好源于工作流，以及独立重发现/推翻设计选择。

Conclusion: 工作流设计比代理能力更影响研究效率，提出多样化搜索、预算化登月假设、显式分叉和机制感知重验证作为未来方向。

中文摘要：我们研究了当单个通用大语言模型作为唯一研究者处理一个长周期神经架构设计问题时会发生什么。该代理接收一个科学问题、初始假设和动机、计算预算以及研究工具（源代码和实验管理、实验跟踪、文献访问和持久记忆），然后在较长时间段内自主提出、实现、评估和记录实验。研究包括三个阶段，由人类声明的转换分隔，逐步扩展代理的工具面或问题规模。在大约100个顺序实验中，代理将一个非标准的Vision Transformer从弱基线提升到小基准上的更强、更高效的模型，以及在ImageNet-1K上可用但低于SOTA的模型，同时产生密集的行为轨迹。我们报告了四个发现。（i）生产力表现出清晰的阶段结构：早期快速收益、持续数十个假设的饱和墙和恢复，恢复由扩展动作面而非改变底层模型触发。（ii）单个早期假设对准确率提升贡献更大，后期改进呈长尾分布。（iii）对贪婪、增量假设的偏好主要是工作流导致的：提交或丢弃的评估规则与贪婪爬山同构；其余部分反映了在重大失败后的风险规避和对熟悉文献的锚定。（iv）代理独立重新发现了已有结果，并且在纯通道注意力的不熟悉机制中推翻了标准设计选择。我们得出结论，工作流设计在本研究中至少与代理能力同等重要，并提出了多样化搜索、有预算的登月假设、显式分叉和机制感知的重新验证作为未来自主研究的可测试方向。

Original abstract: We study what happens when a single general-purpose large language model acts as the sole researcher on a long-horizon neural architecture design problem. The agent receives a scientific question, an initial hypothesis and motivation, a compute budget, and research affordances (source and experiment management, experiment tracking, literature access, and persistent memory), then autonomously proposes, implements, evaluates, and records experiments over an extended period. The study comprises three phases, separated by human-declared transitions, that progressively expand the agent's tool surface or problem scale. Across approximately 100 sequential experiments, the agent improves a non-standard Vision Transformer from a weak baseline to a stronger, efficient model on small benchmarks and a usable but sub-SOTA model on ImageNet-1K, while producing a dense behavioural trace. We report four findings.(i)Productivity exhibits a clear phase structure: rapid early gains, a multi-dozen-hypothesis saturation wall, and recovery, with recovery triggered by expanding the action surface rather than changing the underlying model.(ii)A single early hypothesis contributes more to accuracy gain, with later improvements long-tailed.(iii)The preference for greedy, incremental hypotheses is largely workflow-induced: a commit-or-discard evaluation rule is isomorphic to greedy hill-climbing; the remainder reflects risk aversion after bold failures and anchoring on familiar literature. (iv)The agent independently rediscovers established results and, in the unfamiliar regime of pure channel attention, overturns a standard design choice. We conclude that workflow design was at least as influential as agent capability in this study and propose diversified search, budgeted moonshot hypotheses, explicit forks, and regime-aware re-validation as testable directions for future autonomous research.

</details>


### [78] [Evolving in the Agent Jungle via History-Informed Opponent Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02005)
*Zhaofeng Zhang,Linhan Xia,Rui Liu,Yihao Wang,Binrui Shen,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出基于历史对手快照配对比较的智能体选择性进化，避免动态多智能体环境中的盲目技能更新，可迁移到 agent 自我改进。

TL;DR: 提出OASE，一种在动态多智能体环境中基于对手历史快照进行配对比较、仅在收益增益超过阈值时采纳技能修订的方法，在两个决策场景中比基线达到更低均衡距离且接受更少修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能修订方法针对静态环境，而在多智能体环境中对手策略同时演化，导致更新基于过时参考。

Method: OASE在对手策略历史快照锚定的相同条件下对候选技能与现有技能进行配对比较，只有当估计收益增益超过接受阈值时才采纳候选技能。

Result: 在一级价格拍卖和私人成本古诺竞争中，OASE相比Reflexion风格基线在两种环境下均取得更低的最终均衡距离，并显著减少技能修订次数。

Conclusion: OASE用证据锚定的选择替代盲目更新，使智能体在对手持续演化时仍能稳定高效适应。

中文摘要：通过交互学习策略适应是迈向更通用、更自主的LLM智能体的关键一步。现有方法通常通过修订技能库来实现行为适应。然而，在多智能体环境中，对手可能同时更新其策略，导致环境本身持续演化。将针对静态环境设计的技能修订方法应用于此类设置，等同于针对过时的参考进行更新。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了OASE（对手感知的选择性演化），它在动态多智能体环境中识别并采纳真正有益的技能修订。具体而言，OASE在由对手策略的历史快照锚定的相同条件下，对候选技能与现有技能进行配对比较，并且仅当估计的收益增益超过接受阈值时才采纳该候选。我们在两个决策场景中评估了OASE：一级价格拍卖和私人成本古诺竞争。实验结果表明，与基于Reflexion的基线相比，OASE在两种环境中都实现了更低的最终均衡距离，同时接受了更少的技能修订，从而抑制了缺乏足够收益支持的策略变化。因此，OASE用基于证据的选择取代了盲目更新，使得智能体即使在对手持续演化的情况下也能稳定且高效地适应。

Original abstract: Learning to adapt strategies through interaction is a key step toward more general and autonomous LLM agents. Existing approaches typically achieve behavioral adaptation by revising skill libraries. However, in multi-agent environments, opponents may simultaneously update their strategies, causing the environment itself to evolve continuously. Applying skill-revision methods designed for static environments in such settings therefore amounts to updating against an obsolete reference. To address this challenge, we introduce OASE (Opponent-Aware Selective Evolution), which identifies and adopts genuinely beneficial skill revisions in dynamic multi-agent environments. Specifically, OASE conducts paired comparisons between a candidate skill and the incumbent under identical conditions anchored by historical snapshots of opponent strategies, and adopts the candidate only when its estimated payoff gain exceeds an acceptance threshold. We evaluate OASE in two decision-making scenarios: first-price auctions and private-cost Cournot competition. Experimental results show that, compared with a Reflexion-style baseline, OASE achieves a lower final equilibrium distance in both environments while accepting substantially fewer skill revisions, thereby suppressing strategy changes that lack sufficient payoff support. OASE therefore replaces blind updating with evidence-anchored selection, allowing agents to adapt stably and efficiently even as opponents continuously evolve.

</details>


### [79] [HALT: Verification-Aware Stopping for Retrieval-Augmented Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02009)
*Daeyoung Roh,Donghee Han*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 23 pages, 6 figures. Code: https://github.com/Noverse0/HALT

Selection reason: 面向检索增强搜索 agent 的验证感知停止策略，以证据覆盖而非置信度决定是否停止，可直接用于长程任务控制。

TL;DR: 提出HALT，一种基于证据覆盖的轻量级停止策略，用于检索增强搜索代理，在保持精确匹配的同时减少冗余检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强搜索代理在多跳问答中不断检索，但证据充足后继续检索只会增加成本、延迟和干扰，需要有效的停止机制。

Method: 将停止问题建模为证据覆盖而非生成器置信度，通过验证累积证据是否支持每个预期的跳数声明来决定是否停止，且不改变搜索代理本身。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准上，HALT减少了冗余搜索并大致保持精确匹配；生成声明时节省较小但仍保精度，金标声明时节省更大；开放语料库中覆盖不足时弃权。

Conclusion: 证据覆盖是一种实用的运行时控制信号，无需重新训练或修改宿主代理即可提升检索增强代理的效率。

中文摘要：检索增强的搜索代理通过反复发出搜索查询并积累证据来回答多跳问题。这产生了一个停止问题：在必要证据出现后，进一步检索通常会增加成本、延迟和干扰性上下文，而非有用信息。我们将停止问题框架化为证据覆盖而非生成器置信度，并引入HALT，一种轻量级的、注重验证的策略，它保持搜索代理不变。给定预期的跳数声明，HALT仅在累积证据支持每个所需声明时停止。在三个多跳QA基准上，HALT减少了冗余搜索，同时大致保持了精确匹配。我们区分了一个可部署设置，其中跳数声明由问题生成，以及一个使用黄金支持事实注释的诊断性上界：生成的声明带来的节省较小但仍保持精确匹配，而黄金声明则显示出当跳数目标清晰时可获得的更大节省。基线比较和消融实验表明，这种行为是由声明-证据对齐驱动的，而非通用充分性、固定停止位置或词汇重叠。开放语料库试点进一步表明，当覆盖无法可靠验证时，HALT会弃权。总体而言，证据覆盖为改进检索增强代理提供了一种实用的运行时控制信号，无需重新训练或修改宿主代理。

Original abstract: Retrieval-augmented search agents answer multi-hop questions by repeatedly issuing search queries and accumulating evidence. This creates a stopping problem: after the necessary evidence has appeared, further retrieval often adds cost, latency, and distracting context rather than useful information. We frame stopping as evidence coverage rather than generator confidence, and introduce HALT, a lightweight verification-aware policy that leaves the search agent unchanged. Given expected hop claims, HALT stops only when cumulative evidence supports each required claim. Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks, HALT reduces redundant search while largely preserving exact match. We separate a deployable setting, where hop claims are generated from the question, from a diagnostic upper bound that uses gold supporting-fact annotations: generated claims give smaller but still exact-match-preserving savings, while gold claims show the larger savings available when hop targets are clean. Baseline comparisons and ablations show that this behavior is driven by claim-evidence alignment rather than generic sufficiency, fixed stop positions, or lexical overlap. Open-corpus pilots further suggest that HALT abstains when coverage cannot be reliably verified. Overall, evidence coverage provides a practical runtime control signal for improving retrieval-augmented agents without retraining or modifying the host agent.

</details>


### [80] [Before Reasoning Fails: Pre-Evidence Procedural Failures in Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02011)
*Daeyoung Roh,Donghee Han*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 22 pages, 7 figures. Code: https://github.com/Noverse0/before-reasoning-fails

Selection reason: 将 agentic RAG 失败分解为证据前过程失败与证据后推理失败，并验证强制阅读不变式，对 agent 轨迹级评测有启发。

TL;DR: 本文研究RAG智能体在检索后未阅读证据就给出答案的失败模式，提出Read-Gate不变式，强制阅读可显著提升准确率，并指出证据收集应作为轨迹级控制问题独立评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体RAG系统可能检索到相关证据但未阅读就完成回答，导致推理前失败。现有评估主要关注最终答案正确性，忽视了轨迹中是否实际使用了检索证据。作者希望从轨迹程序性属性角度分解失败类型，并验证强制阅读是否有效。

Method: 利用12,000条成对轨迹（HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA、MuSiQue），将错误分解为证据前纪律失败和黄金阅读后失败。提出Read-Gate运行时不变式，要求智能体在搜索后、最终回答前必须阅读证据，并评估其影响。同时使用正则表达式和spaCy实体提取器验证失败类型的非冗余性，并诊断隐藏思考预算的影响。

Result: 两种失败类型基本不重叠（同时触发率11.2%-13.1%）。强制阅读使原本跳过阅读的轨迹LLM-Acc提升14.9-19.9个百分点，完整最小推理单元提升3.2-9.4个百分点。更大思考预算不增加证据检查。

Conclusion: 证据收集应作为轨迹级控制问题单独评估，与答案侧推理分离。Read-Gate有效改善证据使用，但思考预算本身不足以保证证据检查，需显式机制。

中文摘要：智能体检索增强生成（RAG）系统可能在测试基于证据的推理之前就失败：智能体可能检索到候选片段，但未检查它们就完成最终回答。我们将这种失败模式作为智能体轨迹的程序性属性进行研究，利用保存的工具调用轨迹、检索到的证据、阅读的段落和最终答案，将错误答案分解为证据前纪律失败和黄金阅读后失败。在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue上的12,000条成对轨迹中，这两种失败类型在很大程度上是不冗余的：在正则表达式和spaCy实体提取器下，两者同时触发的比率在[11.2%, 13.1%]之间。然后我们评估了Read-Gate，这是一种最小的运行时不变式，要求智能体在搜索之后、最终确定之前进行阅读。强制阅读在原本会跳过阅读的轨迹上将LLM-Acc提高了14.9-19.9个百分点，在完整的最小推理单元上提高了3.2-9.4个百分点。额外的诊断表明，更大的隐藏思考预算并不一定会增加证据检查。综合这些结果表明，证据收集应作为一个轨迹级别的控制问题来评估，与答案侧推理分开。

Original abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can fail before evidence-conditioned reasoning is tested: an agent may retrieve candidate snippets but finalize without inspecting them. We study this failure mode as a procedural property of the agent trajectory, decomposing wrong answers into pre-evidence discipline failures and post-gold-read failures using saved tool-call traces, retrieved evidence, read passages, and final answers. Across 12,000 paired trajectories on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue, the two failure types are largely non-redundant: the both-trigger rate is in [11.2%, 13.1%] across regex and spaCy entity extractors. We then evaluate Read-Gate, a minimal runtime invariant requiring an agent to read after search and before finalization. Forced reading improves LLM-Acc by 14.9-19.9 points on trajectories that would otherwise skip reading and by 3.2-9.4 points on full minimal-reasoning cells. Additional diagnostics show that larger hidden thinking budgets do not necessarily increase evidence inspection. Together, these results indicate that evidence-gathering should be evaluated as a trajectory-level control problem, separately from answer-side reasoning.

</details>


### [81] [HPFA: Hypergraph-Based Paired Failure Attribution for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02026)
*Runchuan Zhu,Hongbin Lai,Bowen Jiang,Junrui Zhang,Zhangheng LI,Ostap Kilbasovych,Junyuan Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用超图配对比较定位 LLM 推理/编程 agent 失败根因并训练轻量 attributor，提升反思准确率，与 agent 自我改进直接相关。

TL;DR: 提出HPFA框架，用超图与配对比较将推理失败归因到具体步骤，高效生成训练数据，提升LLM反思和推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反思依赖准确归因失败步骤，但现有方法要么反事实测试成本高扩展性差，要么把轨迹当扁平序列忽略逻辑依赖，模型缺乏归因能力。

Method: 用超图表示推理路径，通过比较失败路径与参考成功路径的超边定位根因；缩小搜索空间后合成归因数据，训练轻量级归因模型（SFT+RL）。

Result: 在数学推理和智能体编码任务上，HPFA显著提升归因准确率和效率，训练后的归因器持续提升测试时推理准确率，优于无图结构或无配对分析的基线。

Conclusion: 基于超图的配对归因能高效准确找出失败根因，并可用于训练归因器增强LLM推理，证明图结构和配对分析的有效性。

中文摘要：反思是LLM推理的一种强大机制，但其有效性取决于能否将失败准确归因到具体的推理步骤，而当前模型明显缺乏这种能力。现有的失败归因方法要么需要昂贵的逐步反事实测试，且随轨迹长度扩展性差；要么将推理轨迹视为扁平序列，忽略内在的非线性逻辑依赖。我们提出一种基于超图的配对失败归因（HPFA）框架，通过将目标失败推理路径的超边与参考成功路径进行比较来归因失败根因。通过缩小搜索空间，我们的方法高效定位根因，并支持可扩展地合成归因数据，以通过监督微调和强化学习训练轻量级归因模型。在数学推理和智能体编码任务上的实验表明，HPFA能够显著提高归因准确性和效率，且训练后的归因器在测试时持续提升推理准确性，优于缺乏图结构或配对分析的基线方法。

Original abstract: Reflection is a powerful mechanism for LLM reasoning, yet its effectiveness hinges on accurately attributing failures to specific reasoning steps, a capability that current models notably lack. Existing failure attribution methods either require expensive step-by-step counterfactual testing that scales poorly with trajectory length, or treat reasoning traces as flat sequences that ignore the inherent non-linear logical dependencies. We propose a hypergraph-based paired failure attribution (HPFA) framework that attributes the failure root cause by comparing the hyperedges of the targeted failure reasoning path against a reference successful path. By reducing the search space, our method efficiently localizes root causes and enables scalable synthesis of attribution data for training a lightweight attributor model via supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and agentic coding tasks demonstrate that HPFA can dramatically increase attribution accuracy and efficiency, and the trained attributor consistently improves reasoning accuracy at test time, outperforming baselines that lack graph structure or paired analysis.

</details>


### [82] [Instruction-Conditioned Exploration with Asymmetric Reinforcement Learning and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02087)
*Jim Dilkes,Vahid Yazdanpanah,Sebastian Stein*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Submitted to ACL Rolling Review (ARR) May 2026 cycle. OpenReview submission record at https://openreview.net/forum?id=PV945lekMa

Selection reason: 提出指令条件探索与非对称 RL/自蒸馏训练目标，扩展 LLM 后训练探索多样性，可迁移到 agent 训练。

TL;DR: 提出指令条件探索（ICE）和Asymmetric-RL/SD目标，通过多样化指令提示增强强化学习中的探索，并将探索行为迁移到测试策略，在数学推理上提升pass@1达5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典RL在LLM后训练中面临动作空间结构带来的探索挑战，需要利用预训练LLM的广泛知识来生成多样化经验，但现有方法缺乏刻意引导探索的机制。

Method: ICE在训练时用多种指令补充任务提示以增加行为覆盖；Asymmetric-RL/SD结合强化学习与自蒸馏，将探索策略中的行为迁移到无条件的测试策略。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B上，4K响应长度下数学推理保留集pass@1相对DAPO提升5.0%，8K上下文下提升依然存在。

Conclusion: ICE与Asymmetric-RL/SD能有效提升LLM在数学推理任务中的探索效率和最终性能，且对更长上下文具有鲁棒性。

中文摘要：使用强化学习（RL）对大型语言模型（LLM）进行后训练已成为提升模型能力的重要工具，但LLM的动作空间结构带来了不同于经典RL的挑战，并对诱导探索产生影响。需要新的方法来利用预训练LLM的广泛知识和灵活性，在训练时刻意生成多样化的经验。我们提出了指令条件探索（ICE），在训练期间用几种不同指令之一补充任务提示，从而增加尝试行为的覆盖范围。为了促进ICE，我们提出了Asymmetric-RL/SD，一种结合强化学习和自蒸馏的训练目标，将探索到的行为迁移到无条件的测试时策略。ICE结合Asymmetric-RL/SD目标，在数学推理任务上，将Qwen3-1.7B在4K响应长度下的保留集pass@1性能相对仅使用DAPO训练提升了5.0%，并且在更长的8K上下文下提升仍然持续。

Original abstract: Post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) with Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become an important tool for improving model capabilities, but the LLM action-space structure introduces challenges distinct from classical RL, with implications for inducing exploration. New methods are required that leverage the broad knowledge and flexibility of pre-trained LLMs to deliberately generate diverse experience at training time. We propose Instruction-Conditioned Exploration (ICE), which supplements task prompts during training with one of several distinct instructions, increasing the coverage of behaviours attempted. To facilitate ICE, we propose Asymmetric-RL/SD, a combined Reinforcement Learning and Self-Distillation training objective, to transfer explored behaviours to the unconditioned test-time policy. ICE with the Asymmetric-RL/SD objective improves Qwen3-1.7B held-out pass@1 performance at $4$K response length on mathematical reasoning tasks by $5.0\%$ relative to training with DAPO, with improvement persisting at a longer 8K context.

</details>


### [83] [Fetch-then-Explore: Decoupling Selection from Extraction over a Persistent Workspace for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02097)
*Qi Liu,Yiqun Chen,Zidan Chen,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao,Fengbin Zhu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 将页面选择与证据提取解耦并保持持久文件系统工作区的搜索 agent 设计，提升长程证据积累，对检索型 agent 架构有直接启发。

TL;DR: 提出Fetch-then-Explore方法，将页面选择与证据提取分离并在文件系统中持久化页面，从而提升搜索代理在开放式问答中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理的文档接口（visit-and-read和browsing）都将页面与打开时刻绑定，导致页面在被需要时需重新获取，且证据提取过早。

Method: Fetch-then-Explore在文件系统中为每个问题保存页面工作区，先选择页面，之后按需提取证据，允许重复提取和跨轨迹累积证据。

Result: 在BrowseComp和WideSearch上，相比基线和三个骨干网络上，该方法在BrowseComp准确率领先，WideSearch上通常达到或超过基线；行为分析显示其经常返回已离开的页面。

Conclusion: 通过分离选择与提取并持久化页面，能有效恢复首次遗漏的证据，提升搜索代理性能。

中文摘要：搜索代理现在可以回答需要数十次搜索才能解决的问题，然而这样的代理如何阅读页面却远不如它如何找到页面那样受到关注。几乎所有的代理都使用两种文档接口之一，而这两种接口都将页面与打开它的时刻绑定在一起。'访问即阅读'（Visit-and-read）在抓取时将对页面的阅读注入到消息历史中，在代理知道需要哪个事实之前就固定了该阅读。有状态的'浏览'（browsing）则按需从手头的页面中提取信息，但一次只持有一个页面，并且代理一打开另一个页面就释放它。无论哪种方式，一个在多个回合之后才被证明重要的页面都必须重新获取并渲染到上下文中。我们提出了'先获取后探索'（Fetch-then-Explore），它将页面选择与证据提取分开，并保留它所选择的内容：页面被记录在文件系统上每个问题的专用工作区中，而不是上下文窗口或临时会话中，证据则按需稍后从中提取。选择变得几乎免费，提取可以等到代理知道要寻找什么时再进行，并且随着其假设的细化而重复，页面也不会在代理继续前进时被释放，因此证据会在整个轨迹中累积。在固定的搜索的统一ReAct框架中，我们将Fetch-then-Explore与仅片段、访问即阅读和浏览基线在两个开放网络基准测试（BrowseComp和WideSearch）上、三个代理骨干网络上进行了比较。它在每个骨干网络上均领先于BrowseComp的准确率，并且在WideSearch上通常达到或超过基线，行为分析将增益归因于工作区的决定性动作：在离开页面后返回该页面，它比任何临时接口都更频繁地这样做，因此第一遍遗漏的证据仍然可以在以后恢复。

Original abstract: Search agents now answer questions that take dozens of searches to settle, yet how such an agent reads a page has drawn far less attention than how it finds one. Nearly all of them use one of two document interfaces, and both tie a page to the moment it is opened. \emph{Visit-and-read} injects a reading of the page into the message history at fetch time, fixing that reading before the agent knows which fact it will need. Stateful \emph{browsing} instead extracts on demand from the page in hand, but holds one page at a time and releases it as soon as the agent opens another. Either way, a page that turns out to matter many turns later has to be fetched and rendered into context all over again. We propose \textbf{Fetch-then-Explore}, which separates page selection from evidence extraction and keeps what it selects: pages are recorded in a per-question workspace on the filesystem rather than the context window or a transient session, and evidence is pulled from them on demand later. Selection becomes almost free, extraction can wait until the agent knows what to look for and be repeated as its hypothesis sharpens, and pages are not released when the agent moves on, so evidence accumulates across the trajectory. In a unified ReAct harness with fixed search, we compare Fetch-then-Explore against snippet-only, visit-and-read, and browsing baselines on two open-web benchmarks, BrowseComp and WideSearch, across three agent backbones. It leads BrowseComp accuracy at every backbone and generally matches or exceeds the baselines on WideSearch, and a behavioral analysis traces the gains to the workspace's defining move: returning to a page after leaving it, which it does far more than any transient interface, so evidence missed on a first pass can still be recovered later.

</details>


### [84] [MemArbiter: Decision-Time Memory Arbitration for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02113)
*Jiajun Dong,Yutao Hu,Fengrui Fan,Shihan Dou,Yueming Wu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 9 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables

Selection reason: 提出功能感知的记忆仲裁框架，按功能存储库控制记忆显著性，缓解长程 LLM agent 的 memory-action gap。

TL;DR: MemArbiter通过功能感知的记忆仲裁机制，在ALFWorld任务中将LLM代理的成功率提升20%以上，解决了记忆可访问但无法有效指导动作的“记忆-动作鸿沟”问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的记忆方法主要关注可访问性，但即使信息可访问，若记忆管理不当（形成、组织、优先级、呈现），仍无法有效指导当前决策，导致记忆-动作鸿沟。

Method: MemArbiter将交互历史分解为原子项，组织为五个功能记忆库，结合库级需求、项级相关性、焦点-环境表示和时间呈现门控，动态控制记忆显著性。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，MemArbiter在500和750令牌预算下分别达到82.8%和92.5%的成功率，比最强基线高20.9和25.4个百分点；改善了失败恢复，减少了失败动作和状态-动作重复。

Conclusion: 功能感知的记忆仲裁能有效缩小记忆-动作鸿沟，使可访问信息更好地引导动作，提升长程任务的代理性能。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）代理必须在长时间跨度的任务中保留并使用跨步骤信息以保持一致的行为。现有方法改善了记忆的可访问性，然而与动作相关的信息仍可能因其形成、组织、优先级排序或呈现方式不佳而无法指导当前决策。我们将这种访问后失败称为“记忆-动作鸿沟”。我们提出MemArbiter，一种功能感知的记忆仲裁框架，用于解决由记忆管理引起的这一鸿沟的组成部分。MemArbiter将交互历史分解为原子项，将其组织为五个功能记忆库，并结合库级需求、项级相关性、焦点-环境表示以及时间呈现门控，动态控制记忆显著性。我们在ALFWorld上将MemArbiter与扁平检索和扁平时间顺序方法在统一的每步记忆预算下进行评估。在使用开放权重动作生成模型时，MemArbiter在500和750令牌预算下分别达到82.8%和92.5%的成功率，比最强基线高出20.9和25.4个百分点。它还改善了失败后的恢复能力，并减少了失败动作重复和状态-动作重复。这些结果表明，功能感知的记忆仲裁能使可访问的信息更有效地指导动作。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents must retain and use cross-step information to act coherently in long-horizon tasks. Existing methods improve memory accessibility, yet action-relevant information may still fail to guide the current decision because it is poorly formed, organized, prioritized, or presented. We call this post-access failure the Memory-Action Gap. We propose MemArbiter, a function-aware memory arbitration framework that addresses the memory-management-induced component of this gap. MemArbiter decomposes interaction histories into atomic items, organizes them into five functional Memory Banks, and combines bank-level demand, item-level relevance, focal-ambient representations, and a temporal presentation gate to dynamically control memory salience. We evaluate MemArbiter on ALFWorld against Flat Retrieval and Flat Recency under unified per-step memory budgets. With an open-weight action-generation model, MemArbiter achieves success rates of 82.8% and 92.5% under 500- and 750-token budgets, outperforming the strongest baseline by 20.9 and 25.4 percentage points, respectively. It also improves post-failure recovery and reduces failed-action repetition and state-action recurrence. These results show that function-aware memory arbitration enables accessible information to guide actions more effectively.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Solution-Centric Search: Adaptive Inquiry and Knowledge Revision for Autonomous ML Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02143)
*Shaokang Fu,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Jiarong Zhao,Qiming Shi,Tianjun Pan,Haonan Li,Chengyu Wang,Jia Wu,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出以信息状态为中心的查询-修订循环用于自主 ML 工程 agent，在 MLE-Bench 取得领先，与 SemOpt 的自主实验范式高度相关。

TL;DR: 本文提出信息范式替代以解决方案为中心的搜索，并实例化为Iris系统，通过查询-修订循环动态维护信息状态来指导解决方案改进，在MLE-Bench上以64.9%的任意奖牌率刷新纪录，并展现跨领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体采用树、图或链等结构组织候选方案改进，搜索过程决定信息获取和管理方式，导致决策受限于固定的搜索结构，难以高效处理长时程、相互依赖的决策任务。因此需要一种以信息为中心的新范式，让系统对任务的理解动态演化并主导方案改进。

Method: Iris采用查询-修订循环，包含信息获取与信息管理两大机制。信息获取中，从当前信息状态生成局部行动计划，利用认知行动探索关键不确定性而无需改动已有方案；信息管理中，将实验观察综合为可修订的任务知识（带明确范围和状态的声明），随新证据更新，并按需从原始证据、结构化摘要或任务知识构建决策上下文。

Result: 在MLE-Bench上，Iris在12小时预算下达到64.9%的任意奖牌率，为所有对比系统最高；在框架工程和模型后训练共4个任务上展示了跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 信息范式能有效提升长期自主研究任务的性能，Iris通过动态信息状态管理实现了超越现有解决方案中心搜索的效果，并为跨领域任务提供了通用途径。

中文摘要：长期自主研究任务，如机器学习工程，要求系统在有限预算下做出相互依赖的决策。现有的基于LLM的智能体通常通过树、图或链结构来组织候选方案的改进，这意味着搜索过程决定了信息的获取和管理方式。我们将这种设计称为以解决方案为中心的搜索，并提出了信息范式，其中不断演化的信息状态代表系统对任务的理解，并指导解决方案的改进。我们将这一范式实例化为Iris，一个查询-修订循环。在信息获取方面，Iris根据当前信息状态生成局部行动计划，并使用认知行动来探测决策关键的不确定点，而不修改保留的解决方案。在信息管理方面，Iris将跨实验的观察综合为任务知识，这些知识由可修订的声明组成，并具有明确的适用范围和状态。它会随着新证据的出现更新这些知识，并根据所需的详细程度，从原始证据、结构化摘要或任务知识构建每个决策上下文。在MLE-Bench上，Iris在12小时预算下达到了64.9%的任意奖牌率，这是所有比较系统中最高的。在跨四个任务（涵盖框架工程和模型后训练）中，Iris也展示了跨领域的泛化能力。
