Original abstract: Long-horizon autonomous research tasks such as machine learning engineering require systems to make interdependent decisions under a limited budget. Existing LLM-based agents typically organize candidate-solution improvement through tree, graph, or chain structures, meaning that the search process determines how information is acquired and managed. We call this design solution-centric search and propose instead the information paradigm, in which an evolving information state represents the system's understanding of the task and guides solution improvement. We instantiate this paradigm in Iris, an inquiry-revision loop. For information acquisition, Iris generates local action plans from the current information state and uses epistemic actions to probe decision-critical unknowns without modifying the retained solution. For information management, Iris synthesizes observations across experiments into task knowledge composed of revisable claims with explicit scope and status. It updates this knowledge as new evidence arrives and constructs each decision context from raw evidence, structured summaries, or task knowledge at the required level of detail. On MLE-Bench, Iris attains a 64.9% any-medal rate under a 12-hour budget, the highest among compared systems. Across four tasks spanning harness engineering and model post-training, Iris also demonstrates cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [86] [Beyond the Mean: Multi-Moment Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02149)
*Yijun Zhang,Yule Xie,Jiaxin Ding,Xin Ding,Fan Xu,Haoxiang Zhang,Luoyi Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出多矩策略优化统一视角优化 LLM 推理，作为 RL 训练机制可迁移到 agent 策略提升。

TL;DR: 提出一种基于矩的视角，通过联合最小化失败概率分布的多个矩来优化LLM推理策略，并验证其在数学推理任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理策略优化方法仅优化失败概率分布的单阶矩（如期望），忽略了分布结构，导致优化不充分。因此，需要一种能够刻画多阶矩的框架，以更全面地降低失败概率并提升推理性能。

Method: 提出MMPO（多矩策略优化），将问题失败概率视为随机变量，并联合最小化其多个矩；同时发展了一个通用的矩变换框架，可系统地诱导不同矩分布，统一多种策略优化目标，并赋予MMPO以最小化期望截断成功时间的运筹学解释。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准和多种规模模型上，MMPO相比强基线取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于矩的视角为LLM推理策略优化提供了新见解，MMPO及其矩变换框架有望推动该领域设计更有效的优化目标。

中文摘要：强化学习已成为提升大型语言模型推理能力的核心范式。现有方法通常旨在降低跨问题引发的失败概率。在本文中，我们引入了一种基于矩的视角来对待LLM推理的策略优化，将随机采样问题的失败概率视为随机变量，并通过其矩来刻画优化目标。在此视角下，许多现有方法仅优化失败概率分布的单阶矩，而对其更广泛的分布结构缺乏刻画。我们提出了多矩策略优化（MMPO），一种新颖的策略优化框架，联合最小化失败概率分布的多个矩。MMPO具有直接的运筹学解释，即最小化获得首个成功响应所需的期望截断时间。在MMPO之外，我们进一步开发了一个通用的矩变换框架，能够系统地诱导不同的矩分布，并为更广泛的策略优化目标家族提供统一视角。在五个数学推理基准和不同规模模型上的实验表明，MMPO始终优于强基线。我们希望这种基于矩的视角能为LLM推理策略优化目标的设计提供新的见解。

Original abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a central paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Existing methods generally aim to reduce the failure probabilities induced across problems. In this paper, we introduce a moment-based perspective on policy optimization for LLM reasoning by treating the failure probability of a randomly sampled problem as a random variable and characterizing optimization objectives through its moments. Under this perspective, many existing methods optimize only a single moment of the failure-probability distribution, leaving its broader distributional structure largely uncharacterized. We propose \textbf{M}ulti-\textbf{M}oment \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (MMPO), a novel policy optimization framework that jointly minimizes multiple moments of the failure-probability distribution. MMPO admits a direct operational interpretation as minimizing the expected truncated time required to obtain the first successful response. Beyond MMPO, we further develop a general moment-transformation framework that systematically induces different moment profiles and provides a unified view of a broader family of policy optimization objectives. Experiments across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and models of different scales demonstrate that MMPO consistently outperforms strong baselines. We hope this moment-based perspective offers new insights into the design of policy optimization objectives for LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [87] [From Simple QA to Deep Research: A Verifiable Benchmark Constructed through Iterative Task Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02163)
*Can Wang,Haoran Chen,Haowen Gao,Hao Ding,Zhaoyang Liu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 6 figures. Includes supplementary material. Code and data are publicly available

Selection reason: 用迭代任务演化自动构建可验证的 deep research benchmark，对 agent 长程评测与验证设计有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出一个包含500个深度研究任务的可验证基准，利用迭代流水线自动构建，并以DAG形式支持细粒度、稳定且与人类对齐的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究基准依赖专家编写或人工材料，全自动构建难以保证一致且可追溯的验证，缺乏可靠且可自动扩展的评估方式。

Method: 设计迭代的Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger流水线，将简单问题逐步转化为深度研究任务；每个任务表示为原子步骤和检查点的有向无环图（DAG），并伴随三种查询形式以覆盖互补能力。

Result: 实验表明该基准能清晰区分不同模型和查询类型，基于事实的逐点评分标准提供细粒度、与人类对齐且稳定的评估效果。

Conclusion: 公开数据、实现和结果，为深度研究评估提供可靠且可验证的基准，推动自动构建与评估的实用性。

中文摘要：深度研究基准需要专家级任务和基于任务特定知识的可靠评估。现有基准严重依赖专家编写或预先存在的人工编写材料，而全自动构建难以确保一致且可追溯的验证。为了解决这一差距，我们引入了一个可验证的基准，包含500个深度研究任务，涵盖31个主题和10个主要类别，并设计了三种查询形式，以探索深度研究所需的互补能力。该基准通过迭代的Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger流水线自动构建，逐步将简单问题转化为深度研究任务。每个任务表示为原子步骤和关联检查点的有向无环图（DAG），使查询、DAG和评分标准能够以受控方式共同演化。实验表明，该基准能清晰区分模型和查询类型，同时其基于事实的逐点评分标准能够实现细粒度、与人类对齐且稳定的评估。我们的数据、实现和结果公开可用。

Original abstract: Deep research benchmarks require expert-level tasks and reliable evaluation grounded in task-specific knowledge. Existing benchmarks rely heavily on expert authoring or pre-existing human-authored materials, while fully automatic construction struggles to ensure consistent and traceable verification. To address this gap, we introduce a verifiable benchmark of 500 deep research tasks spanning 31 topics and 10 major categories, with three query forms designed to probe complementary capabilities required for deep research. The benchmark is constructed automatically using an iterative Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger pipeline that progressively transforms simple questions into deep research tasks. Each task is represented as a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of atomic steps and associated checkpoints, enabling the query, DAG, and rubrics to evolve together in a controlled manner. Experiments demonstrate that the benchmark clearly discriminates among models and query types, while its fact-grounded pointwise rubrics enable fine-grained, human-aligned, and stable evaluation. Our data, implementation, and results are publicly available.

</details>


### [88] [From Profiling to Synthesis: Benchmarking Implicit Behavioral Alignment in Personalized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02171)
*Jiajia Song,Bobo Li,Haiwen Yi,Zibo Ji,Meishan Zhang,Hao Fei,Min Zhang,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出隐式行为对齐的个性化 agent benchmark 与框架，涉及长程执行、检索与轨迹级对齐，可迁移到 coding agent。

TL;DR: 提出IBA-Bench基准和IBA-Agent框架，用于评估和改进LLM代理的隐式行为对齐，解决知识到行动鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准依赖静态偏好快照或固定交互日志，无法捕捉演化偏好和偏好条件任务执行，存在知识到行动鸿沟。

Method: 构建基于纵向交互历史的IBA-Bench基准，并提出IBA-Agent框架，通过广泛检索和轨迹级对齐协调冲突优先级。

Result: 实验表明，现有LLM代理在IBA-Bench上表现不佳，而IBA-Agent在九个应用领域显著提升了行为对齐。

Conclusion: 有效个性化仍是LLM代理的重大挑战，IBA-Agent为复杂场景下的行为对齐提供了有效改进方案。

中文摘要：大型语言模型已经使得越来越强大的自主代理成为可能，然而个性化对于使这类代理在实际中实用仍然至关重要。最近的基准测试已经开始评估代理中的个性化，但它们主要依赖于静态的偏好快照、固定的交互日志或基于预定义用户档案的问答。这种设计无法捕捉用户偏好演化的复杂性，并且忽略了偏好条件下的任务执行——我们将这一差异称为“知识到行动鸿沟”。为了解决这一挑战，我们引入了IBA-Bench，一个用于隐式行为对齐的基准测试，它由包含噪声、隐式线索和时间不一致性的纵向交互历史构建而成。与先前工作不同，IBA-Bench评估代理是否能在满足从历史交互中推断出的隐式用户约束的同时执行任务。我们进一步提出了IBA-Agent，一个通过广泛检索和轨迹级对齐来协调冲突优先级的代理框架。在IBA-Bench上的实验结果表明，有效的个性化对于最先进的LLM代理来说仍然是一个重大挑战，而所提出的IBA-Agent在九个应用领域的复杂场景中显著提高了行为对齐。

Original abstract: Large Language Models have enabled increasingly capable autonomous agents, yet personalization remains critical for making such agents practically useful. Recent benchmarks have begun evaluating personalization in agents, but they largely rely on static preference snapshots, fixed interaction logs, or question answering over predefined user profiles. Such designs fail to capture the complexity of evolving user preferences and neglect preference-conditioned task execution-a discrepancy we term as the knowledge-to-action gap. To address this challenge, we introduce IBA-Bench, a benchmark for implicit behavioral alignment constructed from longitudinal interaction histories that contain noise, implicit cues, and temporal inconsistencies. Unlike prior work, IBA-Bench evaluates whether an agent can execute tasks while satisfying implicit user constraints inferred from historical interactions. We further propose IBA-Agent, an agent framework that reconciles conflicting priorities through broad retrieval and trajectory-level alignment. Experiment results on IBA-Bench show that effective personalization remains a significant challenge for state-of-the-art LLM agents, and the proposed IBA-Agent substantially improves behavioral alignment in complex scenarios across nine application domains.

</details>


### [89] [MEGRAG: Multi-Granular Evidence Graphs for Answer-Aware Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02195)
*Weidong Bao,Yingying Sun,Jun Yang,Yilin Wang,Zili Wei,Yubin Bao,Fangling Leng,Minghe Yu,Tiancheng Zhang,Ge Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 9 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables

Selection reason: 多粒度证据图的迭代 RAG 方法，可作为 agent 的检索与多跳推理组件。

TL;DR: MEGRAG 提出一种答案感知的多粒度证据图框架，通过路径结构组织证据并动态判断查询是否解决，从而改进多跳 RAG 的检索与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代RAG方法存在两个局限：一是单粒度证据难以平衡信息密度与噪声；二是仅在所有中间证据聚合后才回答原始问题，导致冗余和错误累积。

Method: MEGRAG 离线构建跨粒度索引（段落-句子-三元组），在线检索时选择对齐证据，从紧凑三元组开始逐步扩展到句子或段落，并利用中间答案决定是否继续检索或终止。

Result: 在多种RAG基线上取得一致且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: MEGRAG 通过多粒度证据图和答案感知的迭代决策，有效缓解了现有方法的缺陷，提升了多跳问答的准确性。

中文摘要：多跳问答是检索增强生成（RAG）中的一个基本挑战，因为推导答案需要整合分散的证据。迭代RAG（iRAG）被广泛用于应对这一挑战，但现有方法存在两个局限性。首先，大多数方法仍以单粒度证据支持每个推理步骤，难以在信息密度和上下文噪声之间取得平衡。其次，现有方法通常仅在聚合了中间步骤检索到的证据之后才回答原始问题，因此冗余证据和中间检索错误可能会累积并降低最终答案的质量。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了MEGRAG，一种答案感知框架，将多跳推理表示为路径结构的多粒度证据图。离线阶段，MEGRAG通过跨粒度索引将段落与其句子和提取的三元组链接起来。在线阶段，它为当前查询检索段落并选择对齐的证据，从紧凑的三元组开始，根据需要添加句子或段落上下文。MEGRAG利用得到的中间答案和先前的推理来决定初始查询是否已被解决。如果未解决，它识别缺失的信息并制定有针对性的下一个查询；否则，它停止检索并返回答案。大量实验表明，MEGRAG在多种RAG基线上取得了一致的性能提升。

Original abstract: Multi-hop question answering is a fundamental challenge in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), because deriving an answer requires integrating dispersed evidence. Iterative RAG (iRAG) is widely used for this challenge, but existing methods have two limitations. First, most methods still support each reasoning step with single-granularity evidence, making it difficult to balance information density and contextual noise. Second, existing methods often answer the original question only after aggregating evidence retrieved across intermediate steps, so redundant evidence and intermediate retrieval errors may accumulate and degrade the final answer. To address these limitations, we propose MEGRAG, an answer-aware framework that represents multi-hop reasoning as a path-structured multi-granular evidence graph. Offline, MEGRAG links passages to their sentences and extracted triples through a cross-granularity index. Online, it retrieves passages for the current query and selects aligned evidence, starting with compact triples and adding sentence or passage context as needed. MEGRAG uses the resulting intermediate answer and prior reasoning to decide whether the Initial Query has been resolved. If not, it identifies the missing information and formulates a focused next query; otherwise, it stops retrieval and returns the answer. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent gains over a diverse set of RAG baselines.

</details>


### [90] [PosterMELD: Multi-Agent Paper-to-Poster Generation for Controllable Design Diversity with Editable Print-Ready Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02218)
*Haojie Hu,Chenhao Dang,Yaojia Liu,Hengrui Kang,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 9 pages, 5 figures, and 4 tables. Code and resources are available at https://github.com/Shannon4Science/PosterMELD

Selection reason: 多 agent 文档生成流程含 VLM 审查、确定性门控与修复，其生成-验证-修复模式可借鉴于代码 agent。

TL;DR: PosterMELD 是一个模板条件的多智能体管道，通过容量感知槽位、确定性门控和 VLM 审查实现高打印就绪率（81.3% PRR）且成本极低（每请求 0.38 美元），同时保留原生可编辑性和显式设计控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学海报生成系统存在不足：直接图像生成不可编辑，编码智能体工作流成本高，且现有评估仅对完整输出评分而忽略请求级失败。

Method: PosterMELD 采用模板条件的多智能体管道，包括容量感知槽位预先指导写作、确定性门控与 VLM 审查失败路由至有界修复，并输出 PPTX 和 PNG 工件，支持显式设计控制。

Result: 在 621 篇论文上，PosterMELD 达到 81.3% 的 PRR，为 P2P 的 3.4 倍、PosterGen 的 5.2 倍；条件性 CHE 分数最高；平均成本 0.38 美元，仅为 Codex+Skill 的 3.5%。

Conclusion: PosterMELD 在保持原生可编辑性和显式设计控制的同时，以较低成本显著提升打印就绪率，为科学海报生成提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

中文摘要：科学海报构建将长篇多模态论文压缩为可读、可编辑的画布。现有系统仅对完整输出进行评分，从而隐藏了请求级失败；直接图像生成不可逐元素编辑，而编码智能体工作流成本高昂。PosterMELD 是一个模板条件的多智能体流水线：容量感知的槽位在渲染前引导写作，确定性门控加上视觉语言模型（VLM）审查将失败路由至有界修复。每个被接受的请求导出可编辑的 PowerPoint（PPTX）和便携式网络图形（PNG）工件；显式设计控制产生同论文变体。在 621 篇论文中，打印就绪率（PRR）统计通过几何、可读性、资产完整性和明显事实错误检查的请求，本地可编辑性单独报告。一个冻结的 VLM 为打印就绪输出分配条件性工艺-和谐-表现力（CHE）评分。PosterMELD 达到 81.3% 的 PRR，是 P2P 的 3.4 倍、PosterGen 的 5.2 倍，并在具有多个打印就绪输出的生成方法中取得最高的条件性 CHE。本地可编辑性和显式设计控制以平均每个请求 0.38 美元的成本保留，仅为 Codex+Skill 的 3.5%。代码和资源可在 https://github.com/Shannon4Science/PosterMELD 获取。

Original abstract: Scientific poster construction compresses a long multimodal paper into a readable, editable canvas. Existing systems hide request-level failures by scoring only completed outputs; direct image generation is not element-editable, while coding-agent workflows are costly. PosterMELD is a template-conditioned multi-agent pipeline: capacity-aware slots guide writing before rendering, and deterministic gates plus vision-language model (VLM) review route failures to bounded repair. Each accepted request exports editable PowerPoint (PPTX) and Portable Network Graphics (PNG) artifacts; explicit design controls yield same-paper variants. Across 621 papers, Print-Ready Rate (PRR) counts requests passing geometric, readability, asset-integrity, and obvious-factual-error checks, with native editability reported separately. A frozen VLM assigns conditional Craftsmanship-Harmony-Expressiveness (CHE) scores to print-ready outputs. PosterMELD attains 81.3% PRR, 3.4 times P2P's rate and 5.2 times PosterGen's, and the highest conditional CHE among generated methods with multiple print-ready outputs. Native editability and explicit design controls are retained at a mean cost of USD 0.38 per request, 3.5% of Codex+Skill's. Code and resources are available at https://github.com/Shannon4Science/PosterMELD.

</details>


### [91] [Self-Certification of Representation Adequacy: Sequential Certification at Minimum Task Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02267)
*Zijie Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 108 pages, 3 figures. Full proofs and appendices included. Independent researcher

Selection reason: 针对 agent 表示充分性的自认证理论，为长程 agent 的可靠性验证提供形式化思路。

TL;DR: 本文提出四层自我认证理论，确保智能体的历史压缩表示不会因混同不同最优行动而产生不可检测的损失；通过静态验证、最优停止与边界分析，给出可渐近最优的认证策略，并指明表示修订的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩历史表示可能导致表示混同，使任何可测规则都产生不可约损失，且智能体无法从自身记录中察觉，因此需要自我认证表示充分性的理论。

Method: 建立四层理论：静态层用贝叶斯风险分组恒等式定义充分性并定价外部验证；序列层用覆盖线性规划定义认证复杂度常数，设计认证跟踪-停止策略；边界层提供核切换示例并指出开放定理。

Result: 定义了逐环境认证复杂度常数，证明了所有δ-正确策略的信息-任务损失下界，给出的策略渐近匹配下界；并提供核切换实例，明确固定核保证不适用于表示修订。

Conclusion: 该理论为表示充分性的自我认证提供了严谨框架，但固定核保证不能扩展到表示修订；未来需解决策略切换或表示修复的开放定理。

中文摘要：作用于其历史压缩表示的智能体面临一个结构性风险：如果该表示将具有不同最优行动的历史混同（aliases），那么任何相对于该表示可测的规则都无法避免不可约的每轮损失，而且智能体可能无法从其自身的交互记录中检测到这一点。本文发展了一个关于表示充分性自我认证的四层理论。静态层通过贝叶斯风险分组恒等式定义决策理论充分性，并通过精确的总变差阈值为一次性外部验证定价。序列层将认证视为以任务损失为度量单位的最优停止问题：我们通过一个覆盖线性规划定义逐环境的认证复杂度常数，为每个δ-正确策略证明信息-任务损失下界，并给出一种认证跟踪-停止策略，其代价渐近匹配该下界。最后一个边界层给出了一个显式的核切换示例，并识别出覆盖策略切换或表示修复所需解决的开放定理；它并未声称固定核的保证可以推广到表示修订。两个主要定理的证明完整地列于附录中。

Original abstract: Agents that act on a compressed representation of their history face a structural risk: if the representation aliases histories with different optimal actions, no rule measurable with respect to the representation can avoid an irreducible per-round loss, and the agent may be unable to detect this from its own transcript. This paper develops a four-layer theory of self-certification of representation adequacy. The static layer defines decision-theoretic adequacy through a Bayes-risk grouping identity and prices a one-shot external verification by an exact total-variation threshold. The sequential layer poses certification as an optimal-stopping problem in the currency of task loss: we define an environment-wise certification complexity constant through a covering linear program, prove an information-task-loss lower bound for every delta-correct strategy, and give a Certification Track-and-Stop policy whose cost matches the bound asymptotically. A final boundary layer gives an explicit kernel-switching example and identifies the open theorem needed to cover policy switching or representation repair; it does not claim that the fixed-kernel guarantees extend to representation revision. The proofs of the two main theorems are given in full in the appendices.

</details>


### [92] [Harness-R1: Learning to Edit Executable Runtime Harnesses from Agent Failure Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02276)
*Shuai Shao,Kangning Zhang,Qingyao Li,Shijian Wang,Hao Wang,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Yi Guo,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 直接从失败轨迹学习编辑可执行 agent harness，用 RL 优化 agent 运行时框架，与代码 agent 高度相关。

TL;DR: Harness-R1通过在线强化学习训练一个框架工程师，对智能体的运行时进行失败条件编辑，从而提升任务成功率，并支持与目标智能体协同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在部署中积累轨迹但行为固定，未充分利用轨迹来改进智能体框架（如上下文构建、工具协调、错误恢复）。需要一种方法让框架本身能基于失败经验进行自适应编辑，以提升任务成功率。

Method: 后训练一个专用框架工程师（9B模型），使用在线强化学习（GRPO）优化对目标智能体运行时补丁的编辑，以实际任务成功率为奖励；冷启动通过监督微调初始化编辑策略，训练时冻结目标智能体，仅更新工程师。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld和DBBench上，Harness-R1将基础Qwen3.5-9B成功率从44.3%提升至53.6%（+9.3点）；在目标智能体微调后，目标特定工程师将平均成功率从59.2%提升至64.2%（+5.0点）。

Conclusion: Harness-R1验证了失败条件编辑作为可学习能力的有效性，且收益在目标微调前后均成立，表明框架工程师与目标智能体可协同进化以持续提升性能。

中文摘要：围绕大语言模型构建的智能体在部署过程中会不断积累交互轨迹，但其行为通常保持固定。除了更新模型权重外，这些轨迹还可以改进构建上下文、协调工具、验证行动以及从执行中恢复的智能体框架（harness）。我们引入了Harness-R1，据我们所知，这是第一种将针对现有可执行运行时的、以失败为条件且覆盖整个生命周期的编辑作为一种习得能力的方法。它通过在线强化学习对专用的框架工程师（harness engineer）进行后训练，使其编辑针对实现的任务成功进行优化，而非由固定编辑器提出。一个独立的9B工程师将目标智能体的失败批次转换为经过验证的可执行补丁；冻结目标的新同批次重跑提供结果奖励，因此训练仅更新工程师。冷启动监督微调初始化该编辑策略，随后使用组相对策略优化进行在线训练。在WebShop、ALFWorld和DBBench上，Harness-R1将vanilla Qwen3.5-9B的成功率从44.3%提升至53.6%（+9.3个百分点）。在直接对目标智能体进行微调后，目标特定的工程师将平均成功率从59.2%进一步提升至64.2%（+5.0个百分点）；由于这些收益在目标微调前后均保持一致，Harness-R1指向框架工程师与目标智能体的协同进化。

Original abstract: Agents built around large language models continually accumulate interaction trajectories during deployment, yet their behavior typically remains fixed. Beyond updating model weights, these trajectories can improve the agent harness that constructs context, mediates tools, validates actions, and recovers execution. We introduce Harness-R1, the first method, to our knowledge, that makes failure-conditioned, lifecycle-wide editing of an existing executable runtime a learned capability. It post-trains a dedicated harness engineer with online reinforcement learning so that its edits are optimized for the realized task success they produce, rather than proposed by a fixed editor. A separate 9B engineer converts batches of target-agent failures into validated executable patches; fresh same-batch reruns of the frozen target provide outcome rewards, so training updates only the engineer. Cold-start supervised fine-tuning initializes this editing policy, which is then trained online with group-relative policy optimization. Across WebShop, ALFWorld, and DBBench, Harness-R1 raises vanilla Qwen3.5-9B success from 44.3% to 53.6% (+9.3 percentage points). After direct target-agent fine-tuning, a target-specific engineer raises the average further from 59.2% to 64.2% (+5.0 points); because these gains hold both before and after fine-tuning the target, Harness-R1 points toward co-evolving the harness engineer and the target agent.

</details>


### [93] [SKT: Skill-Use Training at Scale via Verified Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02287)
*Zelin Tan,Yiqun Zhang,Hao Li,Zhiyao Cui,Hejia Geng,Shao Zhang,Hangfan Zhang,Yang Chen,Xiaosong Wang,Lilong Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Shuyue Hu,Chen Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 24 pages,8 figures, Version 1

Selection reason: 通过验证数据合成训练 agent 技能使用，含规则/agent 验证与反馈修复，适用于技能库型 coding agent。

TL;DR: 提出了SKT数据合成流水线，从智能体技能生成验证过的任务与轨迹，并构建SkillEval基准，证明监督微调能持续提升技能使用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅提供技能不足以让模型有效识别、应用和协调技能，需要改进技能使用能力，因此需要一种可扩展的方法来生成高质量的训练数据。

Method: SKT流水线：选择单技能和多技能配置，通过规则和智能体验证结合反馈修复合成任务，过滤出充分使用所有必要技能的成功轨迹；基于同一流程构建SkillEval基准。

Result: 在多种模型、基准和智能体框架上，使用SKT生成的轨迹进行监督微调一致提升技能使用性能；验证消融、跨框架评估和扩展实验表明收益依赖高质量监督、可跨接口泛化且随技能覆盖增加而提升。

Conclusion: 验证数据合成是提升技能使用能力的有效且可扩展的方法。

中文摘要：智能体技能已成为赋予语言模型智能体可复用程序性知识的重要机制。然而，仅提供技能并不能保证当前模型能够有效识别、应用和协调它们。为了提升技能使用能力，我们引入了SKT，一种经过验证的数据合成流水线，可从大量智能体技能中构建基于技能的任务和可执行轨迹。SKT选择合适的单技能和多技能配置，通过基于规则和基于智能体的验证以及反馈引导修复来合成任务，并仅保留那些充分使用每个所需技能的成功轨迹。利用2000个公开技能，SKT生成了4000个任务包和27164条经过验证的轨迹。基于同一流水线和不相交的测试池，我们进一步构建了SkillEval，一个用于评估技能使用的留出可执行基准。跨多种模型、基准和智能体框架的实验表明，在SKT生成的轨迹上进行监督微调能持续提升技能使用性能。验证消融、跨框架评估和扩展实验进一步证明，这些收益依赖于高质量监督，其效果超越了单一智能体接口，并随着技能覆盖范围的扩大而增加。总之，这些结果确立了经过验证的数据合成是一种有效且可扩展的技能使用训练方法。

Original abstract: Agent skills have become an important mechanism for equipping language-model agents with reusable procedural knowledge. However, providing skills alone does not guarantee that current models can effectively identify, apply, and coordinate them. To improve skill-use capabilities, we introduce SKT, a verified data synthesis pipeline that constructs skill-grounded tasks and executable trajectories from large collections of agent skills. SKT selects suitable single-skill and multi-skill configurations, synthesizes tasks through rule-based and agent-based verification with feedback-guided repair, and retains only successful trajectories that substantially use every required skill. Using 2,000 public skills, SKT produces 4,000 task packages and 27,164 verified trajectories. Based on the same pipeline and a disjoint test pool, we further construct SkillEval, a held-out executable benchmark for evaluating skill use. Experiments across diverse models, benchmarks, and agent harnesses show that supervised fine-tuning on SKT-generated trajectories consistently improves skill-use performance. Verification ablations, cross-harness evaluation, and scaling experiments further demonstrate that these gains depend on high-quality supervision, extend beyond a single agent interface, and increase with broader skill coverage. Together, these results establish verified data synthesis as an effective and scalable approach for skill-use training.

</details>


### [94] [Shared Prefixes, Better Credit: Adaptive Routing for Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02291)
*Yiqing Liu,Zihao Wang,Hantao Yao,Wu Liu,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 共享前缀信用分配的自适应多 agent 路由，提升推理效率与准确率，可迁移到多 agent 协作场景。

TL;DR: 提出TreeCredit共享前缀信用分配框架，通过细粒度状态-操作符信用训练轻量级路由器，在六个基准上以更低推理成本实现更好准确性-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应多智能体推理方法依赖查询级或轨迹级粗粒度监督，难以估计多步协作中单个操作符的状态条件效用。

Method: TreeCredit构建共享前缀协作树，基于终端正确性和累计额外成本为状态-操作符对分配后缀信用，转换为局部偏好训练成对状态路由器，动态选择下一操作符。

Result: 在六个推理基准上，TreeCredit适度提升准确性并大幅降低推理成本，优于代表性多智能体推理方法的准确性-成本权衡。

Conclusion: TreeCredit验证了细粒度状态局部信用分配的有效性，为高效自适应多智能体推理提供了更好的权衡方案。

中文摘要：多智能体推理（MAR）通过迭代的解决方案交换与细化来提高推理可靠性。现有的自适应多智能体推理方法通常从查询级标签或轨迹级回报中学习路由决策，但这种粗粒度的监督无法准确估计多步协作中单个操作符的状态条件效用。我们提出了TreeCredit，一种用于高效自适应多智能体推理的共享前缀信用分配框架。其核心见解是通过状态匹配的下游比较来估计操作符效用，而不是直接将轨迹级结果归因于先前的决策。TreeCredit通过从相同的中间状态扩展候选操作符来构建共享前缀协作树，并根据其完整后续的终端正确性和累计额外成本，为每个状态-操作符对分配一个以正确性优先的后缀信用。这些结构化信用被转换为状态局部的操作符偏好，用于训练一个轻量级的成对状态路由器，该路由器在推理过程中动态选择下一个可允许的操作符。在六个推理基准上的实验表明，TreeCredit在显著降低推理成本的同时适度提高了准确性，比代表性多智能体推理方法实现了更好的准确性-成本权衡。

Original abstract: Multi-agent reasoning (MAR) improves reasoning reliability through iterative solution exchange and refinement. Existing adaptive MAR methods typically learn routing decisions from query-level labels or trajectory-level returns, but such coarse supervision cannot accurately estimate the state-conditioned utility of individual operators in multi-step collaboration. We propose TreeCredit, a shared-prefix credit assignment framework for efficient adaptive MAR. Its core insight is to estimate operator utility through state-matched downstream comparisons, rather than directly attributing trajectory-level outcomes to preceding decisions. TreeCredit constructs shared-prefix collaboration trees by expanding candidate operators from the same intermediate state and assigns each state--operator pair a correctness-prioritized suffix credit based on the terminal correctness and cumulative additional cost of its complete continuation. These structured credits are converted into state-local operator preferences to train a lightweight pairwise state router, which dynamically selects the next admissible operator during inference. Experiments on six reasoning benchmarks show that TreeCredit modestly improves accuracy while substantially reducing inference cost, achieving a better accuracy--cost trade-off than representative MAR methods.

</details>


### [95] [MechGeo: Autoformalizing and Proving Euclidean Geometry in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02295)
*Hao Shen,Junyu Guo,Tian Cui,Yuxuan Xiao,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 43 pages

Selection reason: LLM 自动形式化与定理证明的 agent 框架，含语句修复、反例诊断和内核验证，对代码验证 agent 有启发。

TL;DR: MechGeo 是一个 Mathlib 原生智能体框架，将欧几里得几何非正式问题自动形式化为 Lean 4 并构造内核验证的证明或反例；在 IMO 几何问题上大幅提升自动化证明能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧几里得几何的自动推理面临两个关键挑战：将非正式问题忠实形式化为可验证的陈述，以及构造经过内核检查的证明；现有方法在形式化质量和证明可靠性上难以兼顾。MechGeo 旨在通过反例引导诊断与认证符号计算，实现可信的形式几何。

Method: MechGeo 包含两个核心组件：GeoFormalizer 使用 GeoIR 表示非正式问题，确定性地翻译为 Lean 4，并通过结构诊断和语义评估迭代修复候选陈述；GeoProver 构造几何证明计划、推导中间引理，并利用 Lean 中验证的库选择性地代数化合适的子目标。Singular 或 SymPy 可生成代数证书，但所有证明和反例最终均由 Lean 内核检查。

Result: 在七个 LLM 后端上，自动形式化显著改进，尤其是直接翻译性能较弱的模型。在 43 个历史 IMO 几何问题中，GeoFormalizer 生成的形式陈述被 GeoProver 证明 29 个；其余 14 个生成 Lean 验证的反例，并在专家修正后全部证明。连同 IMO 2026 问题 2，形成已知最大的自动化、内核检查的 IMO 几何 Lean 证明集合。在 LEAP 的 Lean-IMO-Bench 的 14 个几何陈述中，首次证明 12 个，正式反驳 2 个，并证明两个修复后的陈述。

Conclusion: 反例引导诊断、几何推理与认证符号计算相结合，为可信形式几何提供了实用基础。

中文摘要：我们提出 MechGeo，一个 Mathlib 原生的智能体框架，联合解决欧几里得几何的忠实自动形式化与认证证明构造。在该框架中，GeoFormalizer 用 GeoIR 表示非正式问题，确定性地将其翻译为 Lean 4，并使用结构诊断和语义评估迭代修复候选陈述。GeoProver 构造几何证明计划，推导中间引理，并通过在 Lean 中验证的库选择性地将合适的子目标代数化。Singular 或 SymPy 可生成代数证书，但所有生成的证明和反例均由 Lean 的内核检查。跨七个 LLM 后端的实验显示自动形式化的显著改进，尤其是对于直接翻译性能较弱的模型。在 43 个历史 IMO 几何问题上，GeoFormalizer 生成的形式陈述中 GeoProver 证明了 29 个；对于其余 14 个，它构造了经 Lean 验证的反例，并在专家修正后证明了所有修复的陈述。连同 IMO 2026 问题 2，这据我们所知构成了最大的已报道的、针对 IMO 几何问题的自动化且经内核检查的 Lean 证明集合。在 LEAP 的 Lean-IMO-Bench 的 14 个几何陈述中，MechGeo 首次证明其中 12 个，正式反驳其余 2 个，并证明两个修复后的陈述。这些结果确立了反例引导的诊断、几何推理和认证符号计算作为可信形式几何的实用基础。

Original abstract: We present MechGeo, a Mathlib native agentic framework that jointly addresses faithful autoformalization and certified proof construction for Euclidean geometry. In this framework, GeoFormalizer represents informal problems in GeoIR, deterministically translates them into Lean 4, and iteratively repairs candidate statements using structural diagnostics and semantic evaluation. GeoProver constructs geometric proof plans, derives intermediate lemmas, and selectively algebraizes suitable subgoals through a library verified in Lean. Singular or SymPy may generate algebraic certificates, but all resulting proofs and counterexamples are checked by Lean's kernel. Experiments across seven LLM backbones show substantial improvements in autoformalization, particularly for models with weaker direct translation performance. On 43 historical IMO geometry problems, GeoFormalizer generates formal statements that GeoProver proves in 29 cases; for the remaining 14, it constructs counterexamples verified in Lean and proves all repaired statements after expert correction. Together with IMO 2026 Problem 2, this yields, to the best of our knowledge, the largest reported collection of automated, kernel-checked Lean proofs for IMO geometry problems. On the 14 geometry statements in LEAP's Lean-IMO-Bench, MechGeo proves 12 for the first time, formally refutes the remaining two, and proves both repaired statements. These results establish counterexample guided diagnosis, geometric reasoning, and certified symbolic computation as a practical foundation for trustworthy formal geometry.

</details>


### [96] [KC-Agent: A Dual-Process Cognitive Architecture for Efficient ML Model Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02351)
*Gusseppe Bravo-Rocca,Jordi Guitart,Ajay Dholakia,David Ellison,Puneet Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at IEEE COMPSAC 2026

Selection reason: 双过程认知架构用于自动化 ML 模型改进，含记忆、原子修改与回滚，可迁移到代码修复 agent。

TL;DR: KC-Agent是一种双过程认知架构，结合系统1的快速模式识别和系统2的增量更新，通过结构化记忆和原子变更机制实现自动化ML模型改进，在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能并显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据漂移导致生产环境中的ML模型性能下降，需要持续更新，但现有方法在效率和可靠性上存在不足。

Method: 提出KC-Agent，包含系统1（快速模式识别）和系统2（增量更新），使用结构化记忆系统复用成功解决方案，并采用原子变更和回滚机制确保可靠更新。

Result: 在5个数据集上达到76.8%准确率，执行时间13.2秒，优于CodeAct、ToT、ReAct、Reflexion等基线，知识整合带来91%加速，LLM共识评估智能得分8.33/10。

Conclusion: KC-Agent证明了认知启发的自动化ML改进系统在应对复杂数据漂移场景中的理论有效性和实际可行性。

中文摘要：数据漂移对生产环境中的机器学习系统构成了重大挑战，要求持续更新模型以保持性能。我们提出了KC-Agent，一种用于自动化机器学习模型改进的双过程认知架构，将快速模式识别（系统1）与深思熟虑的增量更新（系统2）相结合。我们的方法实现了结构化记忆系统，使系统1能够利用系统2先前发现的成功解决方案，在不进行昂贵重新计算的情况下实现高效的基于模式的响应。KC-Agent融入了原子变更原则和回滚能力，以确保在生产环境中进行可靠、可验证的更新。我们在五个数据集上评估了我们的方法，包括具有真实时间退化现象的真实世界NASA涡扇数据，以及具有受控漂移场景的合成数据集。KC-Agent实现了最先进的性能（76.8%准确率），同时保持了最优效率（13.2秒执行时间），优于已有的认知架构：CodeAct（+2.4%）、思维树（+3.6%）、ReAct（+8.0%）和Reflexion（+8.9%）。由一组最先进的LLM进行共识评估，确认其具有卓越的战略效能（智能得分为8.33/10），显著优于基线智能体。知识整合机制相比慢速变体实现了91%的加速，同时保持了更高的准确率。我们的方法展示了认知启发的自动化机器学习改进系统在处理复杂真实世界数据漂移场景方面的理论基础和实际可行性。

Original abstract: Data drift poses significant challenges for machine learning systems in production, requiring continuous model updates to maintain performance. We present KC-Agent, a dual-process cognitive architecture for automated ML model improvement that combines fast pattern recognition (System 1) with deliberate incremental updates (System 2). Our approach implements structured memory systems enabling System 1 to leverage successful solutions previously discovered by System 2, achieving efficient pattern-based responses without costly re-computation. KC-Agent incorporates atomic change principles and rollback capabilities to ensure reliable, verifiable updates in production environments. We evaluate our method on five datasets including real-world NASA turbofan data with authentic temporal degradation and synthetic datasets with controlled drift scenarios. KC-Agent achieves state-of-the-art performance (76.8% accuracy) while maintaining optimal efficiency (13.2s execution time), outperforming established cognitive architectures: CodeAct (+2.4%), Tree of Thoughts (+3.6%), ReAct (+8.0%), and Reflexion (+8.9%). Consensus evaluation by a panel of state-of-the-art LLMs confirms superior strategic efficacy (8.33/10 Smartness score), significantly outperforming baseline agents. The knowledge consolidation mechanism delivers 91% speedup over the slow variant while maintaining higher accuracy. Our approach demonstrates both theoretical foundations and practical viability for cognitive-inspired automated ML improvement systems capable of handling complex real-world data drift scenarios.

</details>


### [97] [SkillTrace: Traversing a Query-Skill Graph for Composable LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02356)
*Yue Yao,Shengyuan Wang,Xin Chen,Minke Zhang,Jia He,Bingjun Luo,Tom Gedeon*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 通过查询-技能图遍历实现技能组合检索，提升 agent 从技能库选择与组合能力。

TL;DR: SkillTrace通过构建三层图（组合关系、相似性、依赖关系）来检索完整可执行的技能组合，在SkillsBench和ALFWorld上取得最优结果，并跨模型保持一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能检索只关注单个技能的相似性，忽略了技能之间需要组合成完整可执行序列的依赖关系，导致复杂任务中可能缺少关键技能或组合不可行。

Method: SkillTrace将用户查询组织成语义层次结构，匹配技能查询与候选者，并在技能依赖关系上进行传播，通过三层图（组合关系、相似性、依赖关系）实现完整的技能组合识别。

Result: 在SkillsBench上达到53.17%的成功率，在ALFWorld上达到91.43%，均超过现有最先进方法；在不同骨干语言模型上表现一致提升。

Conclusion: 基于图的技能检索能够有效捕捉技能组合的结构性依赖，其通用性和鲁棒性表明这是一种有前景的智能体技能检索范式。

中文摘要：大型语言模型智能体越来越多地通过从技能库中组合可复用的技能来解决复杂任务。为了解决这个问题，关键挑战不仅在于检索单独相关的技能，还在于识别完整且可执行的技能组合。在本文中，我们认为这个问题可以通过一个三层图来解决：技能查询之间的组合关系、查询与技能库中候选者之间的相似性，以及所选候选者之间的依赖关系。我们引入了SkillTrace，它将用户查询组织成语义层次结构，匹配技能查询和候选者，并在技能依赖关系上进行传播。在SkillsBench和ALFWorld上的实验表明，SkillTrace达到了最先进的性能，在SkillsBench上成功率为53.17%，在ALFWorld上为91.43%。SkillTrace还在不同的骨干语言模型上提供了一致的改进，展示了基于图的技能检索的通用性和鲁棒性。

Original abstract: Large language model agents increasingly solve complex tasks by composing reusable skills from a library. To address this, the key challenge is not merely to retrieve individually relevant skills, but to identify a complete and executable skill composition. In this paper, we argue that this problem can be solved in a graph with three levels: compositional relations among skill queries, similarity between queries and candidates in the skill library, and the dependencies among the selected candidates. We introduce SkillTrace, which organizes the user query into a semantic hierarchy, matches skill queries and candidates, and propagates over the skill dependencies. Experiments on SkillsBench and ALFWorld demonstrate that SkillTrace achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching a success rate of 53.17% on SkillsBench and 91.43% on ALFWorld. SkillTrace also delivers consistent improvements across different backbone language models, demonstrating the generality and robustness of graph-based skill retrieval.

</details>


### [98] [Cooperative Coevolution for Resource-Constrained Agentic LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02391)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Shengcai Liu,Jiahao Wu,Ning Lu,Hui Ouyang,Shaofeng Zhang,Haoze Lv,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages,9 figures, submit to AAAI 2027

Selection reason: 协同进化策略实现记忆高效的 agentic LLM 后训练，效果接近梯度 RL 且资源需求低，可迁移到 coding agent 训练。

TL;DR: CoPES提出一种合作子空间进化策略，在低资源GPU环境下对工具型LLM智能体进行高效后训练，以较低内存开销达到接近GRPO的性能，并在多个基准上优于标准ES和LoRA-GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的后训练对内存需求高，而标准ES虽然节省内存但GPU时长过长；资源受限场景下需要更高效的优化方法。

Method: CoPES将全参数空间分解为多个低维子空间，并采用合作协同进化策略在这些子空间上协同搜索，从而在不牺牲性能的前提下降低训练时间和内存需求。

Result: 在数学任务上，Qwen3.5-4B后训练后，CoPES在相同GPU时长预算下恢复了GRPO的验证准确率提升的92%（标准ES为67%），理论GPU内存需求不足GRPO的1/8；在五个基准的pass@k上均优于标准ES和LoRA-GRPO，并在问答任务上也表现更优。

Conclusion: CoPES在资源受限的智能体LLM后训练中实现了内存效率与训练时间更优的权衡，为进化策略在LLM后训练中的应用提供了可行方案。

中文摘要：使用工具的大语言模型（LLM）智能体会产生长序列、多轮次的轨迹，这使得基于梯度的后训练对内存的需求很高。进化策略（ES）能够在没有反向传播的情况下实现内存高效的全参数后训练，并最终可以达到基于梯度的强化学习（RL）的性能水平。然而，资源受限的场景通常只提供少量GPU，因此ES的高GPU时长需求会转化为难以接受的长时间训练。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了合作参数子空间进化策略（CoPES），这是一种合作协同进化方法，它将完整参数空间分解为低维子空间，并在这些子空间上协同搜索以提高优化效率。我们针对数学任务对一个Qwen3.5-4B工具使用智能体进行后训练，并在五个难度不同的基准上对其进行了评估。在全参数GRPO最佳验证检查点的GPU时长预算下，CoPES恢复了GRPO验证准确率提升的92%，而标准ES为67%，同时其理论GPU内存需求不到全参数GRPO的八分之一。在五个基准上的所有评估pass@k指标中，它始终优于标准ES和基于LoRA的GRPO。额外的实验进一步展示了CoPES在问答任务上的优势。这些结果表明，在资源受限条件下，CoPES改善了智能体LLM后训练在内存需求和训练时间之间的权衡。代码已在https://github.com/MetaronWang/CoPES开源。

Original abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents produce long, multi-turn trajectories, making gradient-based post-training memory-intensive. Evolution strategies (ES) enable memory-efficient full-parameter post-training without backpropagation and can eventually match the performance of gradient-based reinforcement learning (RL). However, resource-constrained settings typically offer only a few GPUs, so the high GPU-hour requirements of ES translate into prohibitively long training times. To address this, we introduce Cooperative Parameter-subspace Evolution Strategy (CoPES), a cooperative coevolutionary method that decomposes the full parameter space into lower-dimensional subspaces and searches over them cooperatively to improve optimization efficiency. We post-train a Qwen3.5-4B tool-using agent for the math task and evaluate it on five benchmarks of varying difficulty. Under the GPU-hour budget of full-parameter GRPO's best validation checkpoint, CoPES recovers 92% of GRPO's validation-accuracy gain, versus 67% for standard ES, while its theoretical GPU memory requirement is less than one-eighth that of full-parameter GRPO. It consistently outperforms standard ES and LoRA-based GRPO on all evaluated pass@k metrics across the five benchmarks. Additional experiments further show the advantage of CoPES on the question-answering task. These results demonstrate an improved trade-off between memory requirements and training time for agentic LLM post-training under resource constraints. The code is open-sourced in https://github.com/MetaronWang/CoPES

</details>


### [99] [MonitrLLM: A Community-Centered Evaluation Infrastructure for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02409)
*Victor Ojewale,Ro Encarnación,Suresh Venkatasubramanian,Danaé Metaxa*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted to AIES 2026

Selection reason: 提出将完整对话轨迹与用户任务结果关联的评测基础设施，对 agent 类系统评测有借鉴，但更偏向人类反馈而非自动化 agent 方法。

TL;DR: 提出MonitrLLM开源基础设施，连接对话轨迹与用户报告结果，试点发现高满意度下仍有高失败率，多轮对话失败率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估方法分别关注任务表现、自然对话或用户满意度，但缺乏将交互轨迹与用户定义结果联系起来的基础设施。

Method: 构建MonitrLLM平台，收集完整对话记录、用户任务意图和结果评估；开展26名大学生使用ChatGPT的两周试点，收集206份评估报告。

Result: 用户平均满意度4.19/5，但目标任务失败率23.1%；多轮对话失败率是单轮对话的2.5倍。

Conclusion: 将直接用户反馈与观察数据结合可提升LLM评估的稳健性，MonitrLLM为社区中心评估提供了可行基础设施。

中文摘要：基准套件在受控任务上评估模型能力；大规模对话语料库捕获自然使用情况而不包含用户反馈；界面内反馈机制记录满意度而不包含任务目的。它们共同在LLM评估中留下了一个关键空白：现有基础设施没有常规地将交互轨迹与用户定义的结果联系起来。我们介绍了MonitrLLM，一个用于社区中心LLM评估的开源基础设施，它将完整的对话记录与用户报告的任务意图和结果评估联系起来，将所有三者视为主要评估信号而非可选元数据。为了展示这种方法的价值，我们进行了一项为期两周的可行性试点研究，涉及26名使用ChatGPT的大学生，收集了206份带有完整对话记录的评估报告。我们的试点结果证明了将对话轨迹与用户报告结果联系起来的价值。例如，尽管参与者对其LLM交互报告了较高的平均满意度（4.19/5），但他们在目标任务上的失败率也高达23.1%。我们还发现，多轮对话的失败率是单轮对话的2.5倍，这一模式将扩展交互重新定义为困难信号而非参与信号。我们最后讨论了将直接用户反馈与观察数据相结合用于稳健LLM评估的价值，以及实现这一目标的基础设施的可能性。

Original abstract: Benchmark suites assess model capability on controlled tasks; large-scale conversation corpora capture naturalistic use without user feedback; and in-interface feedback mechanisms record satisfaction without task purpose. Together, they leave a critical gap in LLM evaluation: no existing infrastructure routinely links interaction trajectories to user-defined outcomes. We introduce MonitrLLM, open-source infrastructure for community-centered LLM evaluations that links full conversation transcripts to user-reported task intent and outcome assessments, treating all three as primary evaluative signals rather than optional metadata.
  To demonstrate the value of this approach, we conducted a two-week feasibility pilot with 26 college students using ChatGPT, collecting 206 evaluation reports with full conversation transcripts. The findings from our pilot demonstrate the value of connecting conversation trajectories with user-reported outcomes. For instance, despite reporting high average satisfaction (4.19/5) with their LLM interactions, participants also experience a substantial 23.1% failure rate on their goal tasks. We also find that multi-turn conversations are reported as failing at 2.5 times the rate of single-turn exchanges, a pattern that reframes extended interaction as a signal of difficulty rather than engagement. We conclude by discussing the value of incorporating direct user feedback with observational data for robust LLM evaluations, and the possibilities for infrastructure that enables this goal.

</details>


### [100] [Agentic Commerce World: An Auditable and Verifiable Environment for Vibe Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02441)
*Shicheng Fan,Mingdai Yang,Duohao Wang,Canyu Chen,Yongfeng Zhang,Hua Wei,Manling Li,Julian McAuley,Kun Zhang,Philip S. Yu,Kejing Yu,Zhiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 面向多 agent 商务交互的可审计、可验证评测环境，其过程级验证和可复现设计对 agent 评测有迁移价值，但领域在商务而非软件工程。

TL;DR: 提出ACWorld，一个用于评估自然语言驱动的买方/商户代理在共享市场中交互的环境和基准，包含VCP协议、200+60任务，测试10模型，并强调过程级证据的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有vibe commerce缺乏在真实市场环境中评估独立买方和商户代理交互的框架，最终状态评估不足，需要可审计、可复现且能捕捉过程信号的评估环境。

Method: 构建Agentic Commerce World (ACWorld)，定义Vibe Commerce Protocol (VCP)验证代理动作并记录交互；设计200任务能力覆盖轨道和60任务大型目录轨道（搜索785,022个列表），用十个模型进行评估。

Result: 十个模型在两个轨道上平均得分范围分别为65.9%-85.6%和56.1%-91.4%；分析显示最终状态会遗漏错误，不完整轨迹仍有信号，大型目录任务存在阶段性瓶颈。

Conclusion: ACWorld提供可审计、可复现的代理商务评估，过程级证据对准确评估必不可少，且能揭示大型目录场景中的瓶颈。

中文摘要：在vibe coding中，人们用自然语言描述软件并将实现委托给AI代理。类比地，vibe commerce允许人们用自然语言表达购买或销售目标，并将相应任务委托给代理。然而，商务需要独立控制的买方和商户代理在共享市场中交互，同时保持其私人目标和不同权威。我们引入Agentic Commerce World（ACWorld），一个用于在持续交易中评估此类代理的环境。通过其Vibe Commerce Protocol（VCP），ACWorld在更新共享交易状态之前验证代理动作，并记录产生的交互，使代理行为可审计且评估可复现。ACWorld基准包含一个200任务的能力覆盖轨道和一个60任务的大型目录轨道，后者搜索785,022个可交易列表。在十个模型上，平均得分范围分别为65.9%至85.6%和56.1%至91.4%。我们的分析表明，过程级证据是必要的：仅最终状态可能遗漏评估错误，不完整轨迹仍保留有用的过程信号，大型目录任务暴露各阶段的瓶颈。

Original abstract: In vibe coding, people describe software in natural language and delegate implementation to AI agents. By analogy, vibe commerce allows people to express buying or selling goals in natural language and delegate the corresponding tasks to agents. Commerce, however, requires independently controlled Buyer and Merchant agents to interact in a shared market while preserving their private objectives and distinct authority. We introduce Agentic Commerce World (ACWorld), an environment for evaluating such agents across ongoing transactions. Through its Vibe Commerce Protocol (VCP), ACWorld validates agent actions before updating shared transaction state and records the resulting interactions, making agent behavior auditable and evaluation reproducible. The ACWorld Benchmark contains a 200-task capability-coverage track and a 60-task large-catalog track that searches 785,022 transactable listings. Across ten models, mean scores range from 65.9% to 85.6% and from 56.1% to 91.4%, respectively. Our analysis shows that process-level evidence is necessary: final state alone can miss evaluated errors, incomplete trajectories still retain useful process signals, and large-catalog tasks expose bottlenecks across stages.

</details>


### [101] [Right Answer, Wrong Method: Shortcut Hacking Misleads the Evaluation of LLM Reasoning on Frontier Science Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02442)
*Xuan Ren,Weiqi Zhai,Tianle Pu,Yihua Zhu,Yihua Zhu,Hu Wei,Bing Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: working in progress

Selection reason: 揭示只看最终答案会高估 LLM 推理能力，对 agent 结果验证和可靠 benchmark 设计有警示意义，但不是 agent 方法本身。

TL;DR: 研究发现大模型在科学推理中常通过捷径获取正确答案（Solution Hacking），且随难度增加，导致仅用答案准确率评估会高估推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅以最终答案准确率评估LLM，但正确答案未必反映真正的推理能力，因此需要识别并分析这种“方案黑客”现象。

Method: 系统分析了不同难度和领域中的方案黑客现象，并开发了自动评判器和测试时指令等反黑客策略。

Result: 方案黑客率随难度增加（从2.2%到37.4%），前沿模型中被判为正确的答案中有8.2%-44.1%为黑客方案；抑制捷径行为显著降低报告准确率，但对正确且非黑客准确率影响较小。

Conclusion: 答案仅评估会高估前沿LLM的科学推理能力，需要更严格的反黑客评估方法。

中文摘要：科学推理基准通常使用最终答案准确率来评估大型语言模型（LLMs）。然而，正确的答案并不一定证明模型具备问题所针对的推理能力。我们发现了“解决方案黑客”（Solution Hacking）这一失败模式，即LLM通过无效捷径（如数值搜索、枚举、猜测或先验证答案）达到正确答案，而没有提供有效的任务定向推导。我们系统分析了这一现象在不同难度级别、科学领域和前沿模型中的表现。解决方案黑客行为随基准难度急剧增加，从常见问题的2.2%上升到奥林匹克级别问题的28.3%以及HLE的37.4%。此外，在前沿模型中被认为正确的答案中，有8.2%-44.1%被识别为黑客解决方案。我们进一步开发了受专家启发的反黑客策略，包括自动评判器和测试时指令。结果表明，抑制捷径行为能显著降低报告的准确率，而对正确且非黑客的准确率影响较小。这些发现表明，仅基于答案的评估可能高估前沿LLMs的科学推理能力。

Original abstract: Scientific reasoning benchmarks typically evaluate large language models (LLMs) using final-answer accuracy. However, a correct answer does not necessarily demonstrate the reasoning capability targeted by the problem. We identify Solution Hacking, a failure mode in which an LLM reaches the correct answer through invalid shortcuts, such as numerical search, enumeration, guessing, or answer-first verification, without providing a valid task-targeted derivation. We systematically analyze this phenomenon across difficulty levels, scientific domains, and frontier models. Solution hacking increases sharply with benchmark difficulty, from 2.2\% on common problems to 28.3\% on Olympiad-level problems and 37.4\% on HLE. Moreover, 8.2\%-44.1\% of answers credited as correct across frontier models are identified as hacked solutions. We further develop expert-inspired anti-hacking strategies, including an automatic judge and a test-time instruction. The results show that suppressing shortcut behavior substantially reduces reported accuracy while having a smaller effect on correct and non-hacked accuracy. These findings reveal that answer-only evaluation can overestimate the scientific reasoning capabilities of frontier LLMs.

</details>


### [102] [ParEvalLayer: When Partial LLM-Agent Evaluations Support a Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02444)
*Wei-Jung Huang,Bonan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the 2026 ACM International Conference on AI-ML Systems (AIMLSystems)

Selection reason: 直接研究部分 agent 评测结果能否支撑比较结论，给出决策层和提前停止判据，可迁移到代码优化 agent 的 benchmark 设计。

TL;DR: ParEvalLayer是一个决策层，通过重放公共基准数据评估，表明仅凭部分分数不足以支持结论，需要结合决策规则和未解决比较的数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体评估中，部分分数常在完整基准运行前就产生，但无法保证部分结果与完整评估结论一致；早期任务可能遗漏关键部分、顺序偏差或规则偏向简单样本，导致虚假的准确性，因此需要一种机制来明确判断部分结果能否支持决策。

Method: 提出ParEvalLayer决策层，读取两个智能体系统的配对结果和预选的比较策略，对每次部分运行给出“更优”、“未更优”、“需更多证据”或“弃权”的状态；通过重放已完成的公共基准数据，模拟提前停止并仅用已观测结果应用策略，再与完整数据判断进行比对验证。

Result: 在主要比较规则下，三个公共基准在仅观测15%至25%任务结果时就得出与完整评估相同的决策；其他基准需要更多任务结果，显示不同基准的所需数据量存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 部分分数单独不足以作为报告依据，必须同时说明所采用的决策规则以及尚未有决策的比较数量，以确保评估结论的可靠性和可比性。

中文摘要：LLM智能体评估通常会在完整基准测试运行完成之前很久就产生任务结果。部分分数虽然诱人，但它并不能显示已观测到的任务是否支持与完整评估相同的结论。早期任务可能遗漏基准测试的重要部分，先运行较便宜的任务可能会扭曲观测样本，而一个只对简单配对做出决策的规则可能看似准确，却使许多比较悬而未决。我们引入了ParEvalLayer，这是一个决策层，它读取两个智能体系统的配对结果以及预先选择的比较策略。对于每次部分运行，它记录被测智能体系统是否达到所需幅度的更优、未达到该幅度的更优、需要更多证据，或应当弃权。我们通过重放已完成的公共基准数据来评估ParEvalLayer，模拟每次评估更早停止。在每一个时间点，ParEvalLayer仅使用迄今观测到的结果来应用策略；如果它达到两种比较判断之一，我们检查该判断是否与同一系统配对的完整数据相匹配。采用主要比较规则时，三个公共基准在仅观测15%到25%的任务结果后就达到了与完整评估相同的决策。其他基准需要更多任务结果。这种差异说明了为什么仅凭部分分数是不够的：报告还应说明决策规则以及有多少比较仍未做出决策。

Original abstract: LLM-agent evaluations often produce task outcomes long before the full benchmark run is complete. A partial score is tempting to report, but it does not show whether the observed tasks support the same conclusion as the completed evaluation. Early tasks can omit important parts of a benchmark, running cheaper tasks first can distort the observed sample, and a rule that decides only easy pairs can appear accurate while leaving many comparisons unresolved. We introduce ParEvalLayer, a decision layer that reads paired outcomes for two agent systems and a comparison policy chosen in advance. For each partial run, it records whether the tested agent system is better by the required amount, is not better by that amount, needs more evidence, or should abstain. We evaluate ParEvalLayer by replaying completed public benchmark data as if each evaluation had stopped earlier. At each point, ParEvalLayer applies the policy using only the outcomes observed so far; if it reaches one of the two comparison judgments, we check whether that judgment matches the completed data for the same system pair. With the main comparison rule, three of the public benchmarks reach the same decision as the completed evaluation after observing only 15% to 25% of task outcomes. Other benchmarks require more task outcomes. This variation shows why a partial score alone is not enough: reports should also state the decision rule and how many comparisons remain without a decision.

</details>


### [103] [Long-term Measurements: Towards a Longitudinal Understanding of Human-AI Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02491)
*Nicole Mitchell,Dhruv Agarwal,Maty Bohacek,Remi Denton,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出长期纵向测量人类-AI 交互的评估视角，对 agent 可靠性与长程任务评测有启发，但尚未给出可直接迁移的方法机制。

TL;DR: 本文提出NLP应从短期评估转向长期行为测量，借鉴社会科学方法，以理解和减轻语言模型对用户的长期风险，并促进在线检测和正向对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的‘人性化’和快速普及可能引入认知、发展和社会情感方面的长期风险，而现有静态短期评估无法捕捉这些风险，因此需要新的长期测量方法。

Method: 借鉴社会科学中的纵向测量方法，将其与NLP计算方法结合，用于监测人类行为随模型互动的变化，并纳入对齐框架。

Result: 提出了一个框架，能够支持在线检测问题行为，并引导模型发展产生积极结果。

Conclusion: 建模人类行为变化作为模型互动的函数，有助于在NLP对齐中缓解长期风险，并促进以积极方式发展模型。

中文摘要：语言模型凭借其“人性化”和快速融入用户日常生活的特点，已成为一种非常新型的技术。这种特征组合可能引入纵向风险——人类在认知、发展和社会情感方面的变化——这些风险在短期互动中可能不会显现，但可能对用户产生持久的长期影响。这构成了NLP领域一项关键的新使命：从静态、短期的文本生成评估转向对行为变化的长期测量，以达成对人机互动的历时性理解。在这项工作中，我们借鉴社会科学领域中用于理解纵向数据中新兴现象的关键测量方法。我们讨论了如何将NLP领域的计算方法与这些测量相结合，不仅是为了理解人机互动的长期安全风险，而且是为了引导模型开发朝着对用户产生积极而非消极结果的方向发展。这种以模型互动为函数来模拟人类行为转变的能力，可以促进在线而非事后的有问题行为检测，并且应在对齐框架中加以利用，以减轻用户的长期风险。

Original abstract: Language models have taken on the role of a very new type of technology, by virtue of their "human-ness" and rapid integration into users' daily lives. This combination of features can introduce longitudinal risks---cognitive, developmental and socio-affective changes in humans---that might not surface in short-term interactions, but can have lasting long-term effects on users. This forms the basis of a critical new mission for NLP: to pivot from static, short-term evaluations of text generations to long-term measurements of behavioral changes, towards a diachronic understanding of human-model interactions. In this work, we draw from measurements used in social science fields that are crucial to understand emergent phenomena in longitudinal data. We discuss how computational methods in the field of NLP need to be combined with such measurements, not only to understand long-term safety risks of human-model interactions, but to help steer model development towards positive rather than negative outcomes for users. This ability to model human behavioral shifts as a function of model interactions can facilitate online rather than post-hoc detection of problematic behaviors, and should be leveraged in alignment frameworks to mitigate long-term risks in users.

</details>


### [104] [Abduction Without a Body? Representational Grounding and the Abduction Loop for Scientific Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02505)
*Michael Farmer*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 20 pages, 4 figures. DAB-30 execution reported in companion paper

Selection reason: 提出科学假设生成 agent 的 Abduction Loop 架构，包含表示生成、跨域检索、对抗验证和弃权机制，对通用 agent 工作流设计有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 本文提出科学溯因中的同一性溯因不需要在线具身性，而是通过表征转换和约定空间实现，并给出了一个架构、一个实例见证和DAB-30基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对AI和科学哲学中认为真正的假设生成必须依赖持续感觉运动具身性的观点，本文论证在线具身性并非所有溯因行为都必需，尤其对于同一性溯因；同时解决跨领域数学工作检索中缺乏共享词汇的困难。

Method: 提出了溯因循环架构，包括表征生成、基序提取、约定空间规范化、跨域检索、同一性假设生成和对抗性验证，并以弃权为默认；通过一个多模态模型从图形中生成并验证跨域等价假设的案例来阐明机制。

Result: 以引力记忆输运模型与球谐Kaiser-Squires质量映射复形等价的假设为例，展示了架构的可行性，并提出了可证伪的DAB-30基准；贡献在于机制提议、架构设计和测试程序。

Conclusion: 在线具身性并非同一性溯因的必要条件，通过表征转换利用约定空间可以实现跨域科学发现，但需要进一步的可证伪评估来验证其一般能力。

中文摘要：科学溯因能否在缺乏连续的感觉运动具身性的情况下发生？近来人工智能和科学哲学中的论证认为，真正的假设生成需要一个持续与物理世界耦合的智能体。我们维护一个更狭窄的主张：在线具身性并非每一次溯因科学行为所必需。我们关注的是同一性溯因：即在显式对应关系下，推断两个独立发展的结构是同一个对象，这种推断通过表征基础而非身体互动达成。一个智能体可以通过转化为暴露潜在不变量的表征来获得新的推断能力，而不是通过物理互动。科学图形是一种实用的基底，因为它们体现了独立演化的约定，这些约定在学科间部分地规范了对称性、拓扑性和算子结构——我们将这一性质发展为约定空间，它解决了一个困难的检索问题：当两个领域没有可区分的词汇时，如何找到数学上相关的工作。我们将该机制运作化为一种架构，即溯因循环：表征生成、基序提取、约定空间规范化、跨域检索、同一性假设生成和对抗性验证，并以弃权作为设计上的默认行为。一个记录在案的事件中，一个多模态模型在给定一张引力记忆输运模型图后，生成并验证了其中心微分复形等价于弱引力透镜宇宙学中的球谐Kaiser-Squires质量映射复形这一假设，这一事件作为激励性的可能性见证，从中抽象出该架构，而非作为一般能力的证据。最后，我们提出一个可证伪的评估程序，即DAB-30基准。我们的贡献是一个机制性提议、一个架构和一个测试程序。

Original abstract: Can scientific abduction occur without continuous sensorimotor embodiment? Recent arguments in AI and philosophy of science hold that genuine hypothesis generation requires an agent continuously coupled to the physical world. We defend a narrower claim: online embodiment is not necessary for every abductive scientific act. Our focus is identity abduction: the inference that two independently developed structures are one object under an explicit correspondence, reached through representational grounding rather than bodily interaction. An agent may acquire new inferential affordances not through physical interaction but through transformations into representations that expose latent invariants. Scientific diagrams are a practical substrate because they embody independently evolved conventions that partially canonicalize symmetry, topology, and operator structure across disciplines - a property we develop as convention space, which answers a hard retrieval problem: finding mathematically related work when two fields share no discriminating vocabulary. We operationalize the mechanism as an architecture, the Abduction Loop: representation generation, motif extraction, convention-space canonicalization, cross-domain retrieval, identity-hypothesis generation, and adversarial verification, with abstention as the designed default. A documented episode, in which a multimodal model given a figure of a gravitational-memory transport model generated and then verified the hypothesis that its central differential complex is equivalent to the spherical Kaiser-Squires mass-mapping complex of weak-lensing cosmology, serves as a motivating possibility witness from which the architecture is abstracted, not as evidence of general capability. We close with a falsifiable evaluation program, the DAB-30 benchmark. The contribution is a mechanistic proposal, an architecture, and a test program.

</details>


### [105] [Magnet: Detecting Cross-Session AI Misuse Through Capability Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02518)
*Natalie Isak,Matthew Dressman*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 针对跨会话 agent 能力累积的滥用检测方法，与 agent 安全、长程多会话任务可靠性直接相关。

TL;DR: 提出跨会话目标分解作为规避AI滥用检测的技术，并设计Magnet方法通过跨会话聚合证据来检测此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI滥用检测仅关注单会话或单轮攻击，无法应对攻击者将有害目标分解为多个无害子目标并在隔离会话中执行，从而逃避检测的情况。

Method: Magnet方法：在用户ID级别聚合跨会话能力证据，形成紧凑证据包，供检测器判断，而非逐会话检查。

Result: 实验表明跨会话分解可引出比单会话或多轮攻击更强的有害能力，且Magnet能高效稳健地检测。

Conclusion: 跨会话攻击是实际威胁，Magnet通过跨会话证据聚合能有效检测，弥补现有框架的空白。

中文摘要：最有能力的AI部署不是单一模型，而是由专门智能体组成的集成系统，它们通过委派和协调来行动。这种架构解锁了强大的新能力，同时也引入了现有监控、检测和缓解框架所未设计应对的风险。大多数最先进的AI滥用检测文献专注于单轮或多轮（单会话）威胁模型。这留下了一个关键缺口：攻击者可以将有害目标分解为看似无害的单元，并在孤立的智能体会话中执行每个单元。智能体在对话之间是无状态的，但攻击者不是。这种不对称性允许跨会话轨迹有效逃避检测。我们的贡献有两方面。首先，我们展示了跨会话目标分解作为一种规避技术，表明它可能比等效的单会话或多轮攻击引出更有害的能力。这里的能力是指目标某一步产生的产物，通过交互产生的内容（模型响应和工具调用结果）来证明，并且可以与在其他地方积累的能力组合成有害整体。其次，我们提出了Magnet：一种高效且稳健的检测方法，它对跨时间、跨智能体对话积累的相关能力进行建模，并在更高级别的相关器（在这种情况下是用户ID）处聚合，而不是在每个会话的状态处聚合。主要挑战是组装Magnet推理所依据的证据包。有罪证据可能是散布在良性会话大海中的针，单独无害，只有收集起来才危险。Magnet并非逐根搜索干草（即逐会话检查），而是名副其实：它从干草中吸出相关针，跨会话、跨时间，形成紧凑的证据包，供检测器采取行动。

Original abstract: The most capable AI deployments are not single models but ensembles of specialized agents that delegate and act in coordination. This architecture unlocks powerful new capabilities, and it also introduces risks that existing frameworks for monitoring, detection, and mitigation were not designed to address. Most state-of-the-art AI abuse detection literature focuses on single-turn or multi-turn (single-session) threat models. This leaves a critical gap: an attacker can decompose a harmful goal into innocuous-looking units and execute each in isolated agentic sessions. The agent is stateless between conversations, but the attacker is not. This asymmetry allows for cross-session trajectories that are effective at evading detection. Our contributions are twofold. First, we demonstrate cross-session goal decomposition as an evasion technique, showing it may elicit more harmful capability than equivalent single-session or multi-turn attacks. By capability we mean an artifact produced at one step of an objective, evidenced by what an interaction produced (model responses and tool-call results), and composable with capabilities accrued elsewhere into a harmful whole. Second, we propose Magnet: an efficient and robust detection approach that models relevant capabilities accrued over time and across agentic conversations, aggregated at a higher-level correlator (in this case, a user ID) rather than per-conversation state. The main challenge is assembling the evidence bundle Magnet reasons over. The incriminating artifacts may be needles scattered through a haystack of benign sessions that are individually harmless, dangerous only once collected. Rather than searching the haystack straw-by-straw (i.e. per-session inspection), Magnet does what its name implies: it attracts the relevant needles out of the hay, across sessions and across time, into a compact evidence bundle a detector can act on.

</details>


### [106] [A Taxonomy of Cognitive Capability Gaps in Generative and Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02553)
*Taye Akinrele,Sindhuja Penchala,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 系统梳理生成式与 agentic AI 的认知能力缺口并给出架构和评测方向，对通用 agent 能力改进有直接参考价值。

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于分类法的调查，系统梳理认知人工智能的五大能力差距，并给出ACIA架构和评估框架，以促进长期可靠推理和持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式和智能体AI虽能力强，但缺乏持久状态建模、目标导向自主性、自我监控等基础认知功能，无法在长时间跨度内可靠运行。需要统一框架来组织研究并指明差距。

Method: 采用分类学驱动的方法，将文献按五个维度（持久状态建模、目标导向自主性、自我监控与控制、环境交互、学习与适应）组织，总结进展、局限和开放挑战，并提出ACIA架构和认知中心评估方向。

Result: 给出了一个统一的分类法、ACIA概念架构和评估框架，识别出各维度中的反复局限，并指出了通往AGI的关键研究机会。

Conclusion: 该分类法、架构和评估框架共同构成路线图，支持未来设计更可靠、自适应、持续学习的认知AI系统，为最终实现AGI奠定基础。

中文摘要：认知人工智能旨在超越语言生成和自主任务执行，向能够持续推理、自适应行为、持久记忆和自我调节的系统发展。尽管生成式人工智能和智能体人工智能已在广泛任务中展现出令人印象深刻的能力，但许多基本认知功能仍然分散或发展薄弱，限制了在长时间跨度内的可靠运行。本文提出了一种基于分类法的调查，梳理了继续制约认知人工智能发展的主要认知能力差距。文献围绕五个维度进行组织：持久状态建模、目标导向自主性、自我监控与控制、环境交互以及学习与适应。针对每个维度，我们回顾了近期进展，识别了反复出现的局限性，并讨论了开放的研究挑战。基于这些见解，我们勾勒出一个概念性的自适应认知智能架构（ACIA），并审视了以认知为中心评估的新兴方向。所提出的分类法为组织现有研究、识别未解决的挑战以及指导未来认知能力系统的设计提供了一个统一框架。总之，分类法、架构视角和评估框架为推进人工智能系统提供了路线图，使其展现出更可靠的长期推理、自适应决策和持续学习。该调查突出了朝向更自适应、更可靠、更具认知能力的人工智能系统的关键研究机会，为未来迈向认知人工智能乃至最终实现通用人工智能（AGI）的进展奠定了基础。

Original abstract: Cognitive AI seeks to move beyond language generation and autonomous task execution toward systems capable of sustained reasoning, adaptive behavior, persistent memory, and self-regulation. While generative and agentic AI have demonstrated impressive capabilities across a wide range of tasks, many fundamental cognitive functions remain fragmented or weakly developed, limiting reliable operation over extended time horizons. This paper presents a taxonomy-driven survey of the major cognitive capability gaps that continue to constrain the development of Cognitive AI. The literature is organized around five dimensions: persistent state modeling, goal-directed autonomy, self-monitoring and control, environment interaction, and learning and adaptation. For each dimension, we review recent advances, identify recurring limitations, and discuss open research challenges. Building on these insights, we outline a conceptual Adaptive Cognitive Intelligence Architecture (ACIA) and examine emerging directions in cognition-centric evaluation. The proposed taxonomy provides a unified framework for organizing existing research, identifying unresolved challenges, and guiding the design of future cognitively capable systems. Together, the taxonomy, architectural perspective, and evaluation framework offer a roadmap for advancing AI systems that exhibit more reliable long-term reasoning, adaptive decision-making, and continual learning. The survey highlights key research opportunities toward more adaptive, reliable, and cognitively capable AI systems, providing a foundation for future progress toward Cognitive AI and, ultimately, Artificial General Intelligence (AGI).

</details>


### [107] [AtumAI: A Principled Framework for Agentic Generation of Datacenter Control-Plane Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02569)
*Qiushi Lin,Chaojie Zhang,Íñigo Goiri,Aditya Akella,Ricardo Bianchini,Jovan Stojkovic*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用 agentic AI 自动生成数据中心控制策略，结合形式化编译、扩散模型与进化搜索，对代码/系统优化 agent 的搜索与验证设计有较强迁移性。

TL;DR: AtumAI是一个利用智能体AI自动生成数据中心控制平面策略的框架，通过形式化任务编译和进化搜索，在三个任务上超越专家基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计数据中心控制平面策略困难且耗时，现成的智能体AI存在不正式、不可迁移、不系统三大缺陷，无法高效探索庞大设计空间。

Method: AtumAI包含两个组件：数据中心任务编译器将自然语言目标转为正式可搜索规范；进化设计发现循环结合扩散模型、进化算法和替代模型搜索候选策略，实现自动提出、测试和细化。

Result: 在工作负载放置、资源扩展和电源管理三个控制平面任务上，AtumAI生成的策略一致优于专家设计的基线。

Conclusion: AtumAI展示了智能体AI结合正式规范与进化搜索的潜力，可将控制平面策略设计的入门时间从数月缩短至描述编写，提升策略性能。

中文摘要：数据中心的效率取决于其控制平面策略。设计这些策略变得越来越困难：硬件软件栈增长迅速，设计空间庞大且相互依赖，而原型设计单个策略需要数月时间。智能体人工智能有望自动化这一搜索过程。然而，现成的方案在三个方面存在不足：它不正式——由于没有结构化、可搜索的问题表述，搜索缺乏可利用的结构，且硬约束无法得到保证；它不可迁移——每个任务都是从零开始解决，因此在一个任务上学到的东西不会传递到下一个任务；最后，它不系统——依赖大语言模型作为唯一候选来源，只探索了设计空间的狭窄部分，并陷入局部最优。我们引入了AtumAI，一个利用智能体人工智能生成数据中心控制平面策略的框架，使该过程正式、可迁移且系统化。根据用自然语言陈述的目标，AtumAI自主地提出、测试并细化候选策略，直到满足请求。它通过两个组件实现这一目标。数据中心任务编译器自动化问题表述：它将请求编译成关于任务目标、约束、决策变量和评估方法的正式、可机器检查且可搜索的规范。进化设计发现循环随后搜索该规范，通过扩散模型、进化算法和替代模型将搜索扩展到LLM之外。它们共同将新任务的入门时间从数月工程缩减为编写其描述。我们在三个具有不同问题范围、设计空间和权衡的控制平面任务上评估AtumAI：工作负载放置、资源扩展和电源管理。在所有任务中，AtumAI生成的策略始终优于专家设计的基线。

Original abstract: The efficiency of a datacenter rests on its control plane policies. Designing these policies is increasingly hard: the hardware-software stack grows fast, the design space is vast and interdependent, and prototyping a single policy takes months. Agentic AI promises to automate this search. Off the shelf, however, it falls short on three fronts. It is not formal: with no structured, searchable statement of the problem, the search has little structure to exploit and hard constraints are not guaranteed. It is not transferable: each task is solved from scratch, so nothing learned on one task carries to the next. Finally, it is not systematic: relying on the LLM as the sole source of candidates, it explores a narrow slice of the design space and settles into local optima. We introduce AtumAI, a framework that generates datacenter control-plane policies with agentic AI, making the process formal, transferable, and systematic. From a goal stated in plain language, AtumAI autonomously proposes, tests, and refines candidate policies until one satisfies the request. It does so through two components. The Datacenter Task Compiler automates problem formulation: it compiles the request into a formal, machine-checkable, and searchable specification of the task's objectives, constraints, decision variables, and evaluation methodology. The Evolutionary Design Discovery Loop then searches this specification, expanding the search beyond the LLM itself via a diffusion model, an evolutionary algorithm, and a surrogate model. Together, they reduce onboarding a new task from months of engineering to writing its description. We evaluate AtumAI on three control-plane tasks with distinct problem scopes, design spaces, and trade-offs: workload placement, resource scaling, and power management. Across all tasks, the policies generated by AtumAI consistently outperform expert-engineered baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Cross-Benchmark Generalization in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00181)
*Sushant Mehta,Logan Ritchie,Liudas Panavas,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted at the COLM 2026 Workshop on Agent Behavior. 11 pages, 4 tables

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 在363个MCP任务上对Qwen3.5-122B-A10B进行SFT+RL后训练，未使用外部基准数据，显著提升了五个外部基准的性能（最高+9.6pp），并揭示了四种可迁移的行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL评估中，策略可能利用环境特定规律而非可迁移技能，分布内保留集无法区分。需要跨基准迁移的行为证据来证明智能体获得了通用能力。

Method: 采用两阶段SFT-然后-RL流程，在363个长视野MCP任务（27类）上后训练Qwen3.5-122B-A10B，训练数据不含外部基准任务或评分器，奖励和超参数选择均不依赖外部得分。

Result: 贪婪pass@1下，五个外部评估全部提升：Toolathlon +9.6pp、τ²-Bench +5.3pp、BFCL-V4 +3.5pp、SWE-Bench Pro +5.8pp、Terminal-Bench 2 +2.8pp；软件工程基准在无相关训练数据下也提升。轨迹分析发现四种行为差异。

Conclusion: 长视野多工具后训练能改变智能体的工作方式，并迁移到训练领域之外，为通用性的行为证据提供了描述性支持。

中文摘要：对于自包含环境中的强化学习（RL），策略可以通过利用环境特定的规律性（工具模式、评分器解析、任务模板）来获得奖励，而不是通过获取可迁移的技能，并且分布内的保留集共享这些规律性。我们认为，具有区分性的问题是行为层面的，即训练后的智能体如何行动，而跨基准迁移是寻找这一答案的正确场所。我们使用两阶段SFT-然后-RL流程，在27个类别的363个长视野模型上下文协议（MCP）任务上对开放权重混合专家模型（Qwen3.5-122B-A10B）进行后训练。Toolathlon的性能影响了初始基础模型家族和SFT教师的选择，但没有任何外部基准任务或评分器进入训练，也没有外部得分影响奖励、训练超参数、训练检查点选择或停止。在贪婪pass@1下，训练后的模型在五个报告的外部评估上相对于基础模型有所改进：Toolathlon（+9.6个百分点）、τ²-Bench（+5.3个百分点）、BFCL-V4（+3.5个百分点）、SWE-Bench Pro（+5.8个百分点）和Terminal-Bench 2（+2.8个百分点）。尽管训练集不包含软件工程任务，两个软件工程基准仍然有所改进。一项探索性的成对轨迹分析识别出四种反复出现的行为差异（更仔细的局部目标形成、构建与目标相关的工作状态、通过局部修复保持父目标稳定、以及验证完成），这些差异在办公工作流和代码中以类似形式出现。这些结果提供了描述性证据，表明长视野多工具后训练可以改变工作方式，并迁移到其训练领域之外。

Original abstract: For reinforcement learning (RL) in self-contained environments, a policy can get rewards by exploiting environment-specific regularities (tool schemas, grader parsing, task templates) rather than by acquiring transferable skill, and an in-distribution holdout shares those regularities. We argue that the discriminating question is behavioral, namely how a trained agent acts, and that cross-benchmark transfer is the right place to look for it. We post-train an open-weight mixture-of-experts model (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) on 363 long-horizon Model Context Protocol (MCP) tasks across 27 categories, using a two-stage SFT-then-RL pipeline. Toolathlon performance informed the initial base-family and SFT-teacher choices, but no external-benchmark task or grader entered training and no external score informed the reward, training hyperparameters, trained-checkpoint selection, or stopping. At greedy pass@1, the trained model improves over the base on five reported external evaluations: Toolathlon (+9.6 pp), $τ^2$-Bench (+5.3 pp), BFCL-V4 (+3.5 pp), SWE-Bench Pro (+5.8 pp), and Terminal-Bench 2 (+2.8 pp). Both software-engineering benchmarks improve despite the training collection containing no software-engineering tasks. An exploratory paired-trajectory analysis identifies four recurring behavioral differences (more careful local-goal formation, building goal-relevant working state, keeping parent goals stable through local repairs, and verifying completion) that appear in analogous forms across office workflows and code. These results provide descriptive evidence that long-horizon multi-tool post-training can change ways of working that transfer beyond its training domain.

</details>


### [109] [Source Code Authorship Attribution Does Not Generalize from Competitions to Classrooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00227)
*Serhii Yemets,Marek Horváth*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: CodeBERT 在 GCJ 基准上表现优异，但在真实课程作业数据集上接近随机基线，说明现有基准高估了源代码作者归属的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码作者归属在教育场景（如检测抄袭或身份验证）中有潜在应用，但现有研究多基于 GCJ 等竞赛数据，缺乏对真实课程作业场景的验证。

Method: 微调 CodeBERT，并在 GCJ Kaggle 数据、GCJ 精选存档及机构课程作业数据上评估；同时构建多模型基准以验证跨模型一致性。

Result: GCJ 上 10 作者 Top-1 92.6%，1000 作者 Top-1 70.7%；课程作业上封闭数据集 Top-1 0.2%，开放数据集 Top-1 0.06%，与随机基线相当。

Conclusion: GCJ 基准会高估作者归属的实际性能，必须结合目标课程环境进行验证，否则模型在真实教育场景中缺乏可用性。

中文摘要：源代码作者归属旨在根据程序的写作风格识别程序片段的作者。我们在三种数据来源上微调预训练的 transformer CodeBERT：来自开放 Kaggle 仓库的公开 Google Code Jam (GCJ) 提交、精选的 GCJ 存档以及在一所技术大学收集的机构课程作业数据集。在多轮 GCJ 数据上，CodeBERT 在 10 位作者的情况下达到 92.6% 的 Top-1 准确率，并在 1000 位作者的情况下保持 70.7% 的 Top-1（88.2% 的 Top-10）。在所考察的课程作业数据集上，同一流程达到或低于相应的随机基线：在包含 690 位作者的封闭作业数据集上 Top-1 为 0.2%，在包含 812 位作者的开放式作业上 Top-1 为 0.06%。一个配套的多模型基准在额外的数据集配置上提供了一致的跨模型证据，表明观察到的性能差距并非 CodeBERT 特有，而是持续存在于所评估的模型家族中。我们分析了可能解释这一差距的数据集和任务属性，并认为基于 GCJ 的基准高估了作者归属在教育场景中的实际适用性，除非在目标课程作业环境中得到验证。

Original abstract: Source code authorship attribution aims to identify the author of a program fragment from its writing style. We fine-tune the pre-trained transformer CodeBERT on three sources of data: publicly available Google Code Jam (GCJ) submissions from an open Kaggle repository, a curated GCJ archive, and institutional coursework datasets collected at a technical university. On multi-round GCJ data, CodeBERT reaches 92.6% Top-1 accuracy for 10 authors and retains 70.7% Top-1 (88.2% Top-10) for 1000 authors. On the examined coursework datasets, the same pipeline performs at or below the corresponding chance baselines: 0.2% Top-1 on a closed-assignment dataset of 690 authors and 0.06% Top-1 on open-ended assignments evaluated over 812 authors. A companion multi-model benchmark provides consistent cross-model evidence across additional dataset configurations, indicating that the observed performance gap is not specific to CodeBERT and persists across the evaluated model families. We analyze dataset and task properties that plausibly explain this gap and argue that GCJ-based benchmarks overestimate the practical applicability of authorship attribution in educational settings unless they are validated on the target coursework context.

</details>


### [110] [Modelling Android applications through static analysis and systematic exploratory testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00228)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

Journal reference: 10th International Conference on Dependable Systems and Their Applications (DSA), pp. 94-104. IEEE, 2023

DOI: [10.1109/DSA59317.2023.00022](https://doi.org/10.1109/DSA59317.2023.00022)

Author affiliations: Jordan Doyle: University College Dublin; Thomas Laurent: National Institute of Informatics; Anthony Ventresque: Trinity College Dublin

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出DroidGraph框架，结合静态分析与高效探索性测试生成Android应用控制流模型，提升测试生成效率和应用覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用开发快节奏，需要自动化测试生成；现有静态分析难以处理Android事件驱动特性，动态随机测试效率低且缺乏应用知识，因此需要新方法。

Method: DroidGraph结合传统静态分析与高效系统性探索性测试，生成从低层方法到高层UI结构的综合控制流模型。

Result: 在19个应用上，探索性测试比随机探索平均多交互18%应用，且交互次数少345次；结合动态分析后平均多发现51个组件和49%的界面回调链接。

Conclusion: DroidGraph能有效生成Android应用模型，补充静态分析不足，支持自动化测试生成。

中文摘要：移动应用开发是一个快节奏的行业，发布频繁。随着开发节奏的加快，对自动化测试生成的需求也在增加。基于模型的测试生成是支持这种需求的最常见且最成功的方法之一。理解并建模被测应用对于生成全面、可靠且有效的测试至关重要。不幸的是，像Android这样的移动平台引入了许多困难。静态分析难以应对Android基于事件的特性以及开发者可用于实现不同功能的日益多样的机制。此外，由流行的随机测试生成器实现的动态分析速度慢、效率低，并且由于缺乏应用知识而受到限制。本文介绍了DroidGraph，一个利用传统静态分析和高效的系统性探索性测试来生成Android应用综合控制流模型的框架。DroidGraph提供了Android应用的详细模型，从低层方法语句到高层用户界面结构。该模型可用于支持自动化测试生成。我们将DroidGraph应用于19个不同的应用，并表明我们高效的探索性测试平均与应用交互的比例比常用随机探索多18%，且交互次数少345次。将这些测试提供的动态分析结果与静态分析相结合，平均在应用代码中多发现51个组件和49%的界面回调链接。

Original abstract: Mobile application development is a fast paced industry with frequent releases. While the development pace increases, so too does the need for automated test generation. Model-based test generation is one of the most common and successful approaches to support this need. Understanding and modelling the application under test is integral to producing comprehensive, dependable and effective tests. Unfortunately, mobile platforms such as Android, introduce a host of difficulties. Static analysis struggles with Android's event-based nature and the growing variety of mechanisms available for developers to implement different features. Additionally, dynamic analysis, implemented by popular random test generators, is slow, inefficient, and limited by a lack of application knowledge.
  This paper introduces DroidGraph, a framework to generate a comprehensive control flow model of Android applications using traditional static analysis and efficient systematic exploratory tests. DroidGraph provides a detailed model of an Android application, from low level method statements to high level user interface structures. This model can be used to support automated test generation. We apply DroidGraph to 19 diverse apps and show that our efficient exploratory tests, on average, interact with 18% more of the app than commonly used random exploration in 345 less interactions. Integrating the dynamic analysis results provided by these tests complements our static analysis, and uncovers on average 51 more components and 49% more interface callback links in the application code.

</details>


### [111] [PADRAIG: Precise Android Automated Input Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00230)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

Journal reference: 24th International Conference on Software Quality, Reliability and Security (QRS), pp. 72-83. IEEE, 2024

DOI: [10.1109/QRS62785.2024.00017](https://doi.org/10.1109/QRS62785.2024.00017)

Author affiliations: Jordan Doyle: Trinity College Dublin; Thomas Laurent: Trinity College Dublin; Anthony Ventresque: Trinity College Dublin

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: PADRAIG是一个基于模型并使用详细控制流图的Android测试输入生成框架，相比现有技术平均提高16%行覆盖率并减少84%生成运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Android测试输入生成方法各有不足：随机方法效率低，系统化与搜索方法运行耗时，模型方法建模开销大。需要一种能同时提高覆盖率并降低生成运行时间的方法。

Method: 提出了PADRAIG，一个基于模型的框架，利用被测应用（AUT）的详细控制流模型生成测试，并与三种采用不同输入生成技术的现有工具进行对比。

Result: 在从F-Droid随机选择的19个Android应用上，PADRAIG平均比现有技术多覆盖16%的AUT，且测试生成运行时间平均减少84%。

Conclusion: PADRAIG通过详细控制流建模，在行覆盖率和测试生成效率上均显著优于现有技术，展示了基于模型的方法在Android测试输入生成中的有效性。

中文摘要：Android自动化测试输入生成已经是一个被高度研究超过十年的课题，并且通过各种方法展现出了有希望的结果。随机输入生成被普遍使用且最容易维护，但最终效率低下。系统化和基于搜索的方法能产生有效的测试，但需要不成比例的大规模生成运行时间。基于模型的方法有额外的建模被测应用（AUT）的开销，但最终能实现更快的测试生成。在本文中，我们提出了精确的Android自动化输入生成（PADRAIG），一个基于模型的测试输入生成框架，它使用AUT的详细控制流模型来生成测试，能够实现更高的行覆盖率，并且比现有技术的测试生成运行时间更低。我们将PADRAIG实现的行覆盖率和生成运行时间与三种现有技术工具进行比较，每种工具使用不同的测试输入生成技术。我们使用从F-Droid应用商店随机选择的19个Android应用的结果表明，PADRAIG平均比现有技术多覆盖AUT的16%，并且平均能减少84%的运行时间生成测试。

Original abstract: Android automated test input generation has been a highly researched topic for over a decade and has shown promising results with a variety of approaches. Random input generation is commonly used and the easiest to maintain, but ultimately inefficient. Systematic and search-based approaches produce effective tests but require a disproportionally large generation runtime. Model-based approaches have the additional overhead of modelling the application under test (AUT) but they result in a faster test generation.
  In this paper we present Precise AnDRoid Automated Input Generation (PADRAIG), a model-based test input generation framework that uses a detailed control flow model of the AUT to generate tests that can achieve higher line coverage, with a lower test generation runtime than the state of the art. We compare the line coverage achieved, and the generation runtime of PADRAIG against 3 state of the art tools, each of which uses a different test input generation technique. Our results, using 19 randomly selected Android apps from the F-Droid application store, show that PADRAIG achieves, on average, 16% more coverage of the AUT than the state of the art and it can generate tests with, on average, 84% less runtime.

</details>


### [112] [Improving Mobile User Interface Testing with Model Driven Monkey Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00236)
*Jordan Doyle,Takfarinas Saber,Paolo Arcaini,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

Journal reference: International Conference on Software Testing, Verification and Validation Workshops (ICSTW), pp. 138-145. IEEE, 2021

DOI: [10.1109/ICSTW52544.2021.00034](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICSTW52544.2021.00034)

Author affiliations: Jordan Doyle: University College Dublin; Takfarinas Saber: University College Dublin; Paolo Arcaini: National Institute of Informatics; Anthony Ventresque: University College Dublin

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的控制流结构表示Android应用，通过深度优先搜索生成测试，相比随机测试的Exerciser Monkey，覆盖率更高且速度快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Android测试工具如Exerciser Monkey随机生成事件，不了解应用结构，导致测试速度慢、覆盖率低、难以发现bug。因此需要一种能引导测试工具的结构化方法。

Method: 提出一种包含所有交互元素的控制流结构，并用深度优先搜索遍历该结构，生成测试用例，赋予Exerciser Monkey对环境的知识。

Result: 实验表明，基于深度优先搜索的Monkey++相比原始Exerciser Monkey，测试套件速度快一个数量级，并实现用户交互元素的完全覆盖，而随机测试覆盖率低且速度慢。

Conclusion: 该研究有望提升Exerciser Monkey的有效性和效率，并为自动化Android测试提供更好的定向搜索技术。

中文摘要：测试移动应用通常依赖于工具，例如用于Android系统的Exerciser Monkey，这些工具模拟用户输入。例如，Exerciser Monkey生成随机事件（如触摸、手势、导航键），让开发者了解他们的应用在真实手机上被真实用户交互时的行为。然而，这些工具不了解底层应用的结构，只能随机或按预定义方式与之交互（例如，如果开发者设计场景，这是一项劳动密集型的任务）——这使得它们速度慢且不擅长发现错误。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的控制流结构，能够表示Android应用的代码，包括所有交互元素。我们表明，我们的结构可以通过赋予Exerciser Monkey对测试环境的知识来提升其有效性（更高的覆盖率）和效率（移除重复/冗余测试）。我们比较了Exerciser Monkey与我们新的Monkey++（使用对控制流结构的深度优先搜索）所达到的界面覆盖率，并表明虽然Exerciser Monkey的随机性导致了速度慢且覆盖率差的测试套件，但深度优先搜索创建的测试套件速度快一个数量级，并实现了用户交互元素的完全覆盖。我们相信这项研究将带来更有效和高效的Exerciser Monkey，以及更好的针对性搜索技术用于自动化Android测试。

Original abstract: Testing mobile applications often relies on tools, such as Exerciser Monkey for Android systems, that simulate user input. Exerciser Monkey, for example, generates random events (e.g., touches, gestures, navigational keys) that give developers a sense of what their application will do when deployed on real mobile phones with real users interacting with it. These tools, however, have no knowledge of the underlying applications' structures and only interact with them randomly or in a predefined manner (e.g., if developers designed scenarios, a labour-intensive task) -- making them slow and poor at finding bugs.
  In this paper, we propose a novel control flow structure able to represent the code of Android applications, including all the interactive elements. We show that our structure can increase the effectiveness (higher coverage) and efficiency (removing duplicate/redundant tests) of the Exerciser Monkey by giving it knowledge of the test environment. We compare the interface coverage achieved by the Exerciser Monkey with our new Monkey++ using a depth first search of our control flow structure and show that while the random nature of Exerciser Monkey creates slow test suites of poor coverage, the test suite created by a depth first search is one order of magnitude faster and achieves full coverage of the user interaction elements. We believe this research will lead to a more effective and efficient Exerciser Monkey, as well as better targeted search based techniques for automated Android testing.

</details>


### [113] [SMS Opt-In/Opt-Out Consent Record Architecture in Enterprise CRM Systems: Compliance Patterns for Multi-Tenant Managed Packages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00248)
*Devam Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 8 pages - Companion to arXiv:2607.12943 and arXiv:2607.15529

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在CRM原生多租户托管包中实现的生产级同意记录架构，用于满足TCPA等监管要求，涵盖数据模型、关键词同意捕获、哈希去重、发送时抑制及多租户约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监管法规要求企业消息系统必须管理用户同意，但CRM平台未将同意状态作为一等记录类型，尤其是在多租户托管包中无法修改安装组织架构，导致同意管理存在架构缺口。

Method: 从实际CRM原生消息传递托管包中概括设计模式，包括数据模型设计、基于关键字的同意捕获、基于哈希的唯一性去重策略、消息发送时的抑制执行，并考虑多租户约束。

Result: 形成了一套生产级的同意记录架构，成功应用于医疗保健、金融服务和销售运营等多个独立企业组织，实现合规的同意管理和消息发送抑制。

Conclusion: 该架构通过将同意状态作为一等记录并采用多租户友好的设计，有效弥补了CRM平台的缺口，为受监管行业的企业消息系统提供了可复用的生产级解决方案。

中文摘要：诸如《电话消费者保护法》（TCPA）之类的监管框架对企业消息系统施加了严格的同意要求：组织必须在发送短信通信之前获得并记录明确的opt-in同意，并且必须立即处理opt-out请求。虽然CRM平台提供了丰富的联系人和线索数据模型，但它们并未将同意状态作为一流的记录类型进行原生建模。这一差距在多租户托管包中在架构上变得显著，这些包通过企业应用市场分发，且无法假设或修改安装组织的模式。本文提出了一种生产级的同意记录架构，该架构从CRM原生消息传递托管包中实现的模式概括而来，该包服务于跨越医疗保健、金融服务和销售运营的独立企业组织。我们描述了数据模型设计、基于关键字的同意捕获、用于去重的基于哈希的唯一性策略、在消息发送时的抑制执行，以及影响这些设计决策的多租户约束。

Original abstract: Regulatory frameworks such as the Telephone Consumer Protection Act (TCPA) impose strict consent requirements on enterprise messaging systems: organizations must obtain and record explicit opt-in consent before sending SMS communications, and must immediately honor opt-out requests. While CRM platforms provide rich contact and lead data models, they do not natively model consent state as a first-class record type. This gap becomes architecturally significant in multi-tenant managed packages distributed via enterprise application marketplaces, where the package cannot assume or modify the installing organization's schema. This paper presents a production consent record architecture, generalized from patterns implemented within a CRM-native messaging managed package serving independent enterprise organizations spanning healthcare, financial services, and sales operations. We describe the data model design, keyword-based consent capture, a hash-based uniqueness strategy for deduplication, suppression enforcement at message send time, and the multi-tenant constraints that shaped these design decisions.

</details>


### [114] [LoopsBench: From Harness Engineering to Loop Engineering in Benchmarking Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00267)
*Han Li,Zhemin Fang,Rili Feng,Yingqi Zhao,Jiaheng Liu,Pengfei Gao,He Ye,Dayi Lin,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Project page: https://loopsbench.ai/

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 引入LOOPSBENCH，一个用于评估编码智能体长周期循环工程能力的基准，包含112个任务和5300多个开发单元，最强配置仅解决25%任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准聚焦于局部任务或最终状态，难以评估长时间持续执行能力；需要针对循环工程的基准。

Method: 提出LOOPSBENCH，每个任务是依赖DAG，用流感知运行时释放测试并保留回归义务，评估多个前沿编码智能体与循环实现的组合。

Result: 最强配置Opus-4.7+Claude Code+外部延续仅解决25.00%任务；记录的计划只能恢复部分DAG，回归事件仍然可见。

Conclusion: 当前编码智能体在长周期循环工程任务上表现有限，需要进一步改进循环设计与持续执行能力；基准已开源以促进研究。
