Our work represents a first step toward a systematic understanding of automated taxonomy generation in SE, offering actionable insights for future research and practice.

</details>


### [141] [AI-assisted Script Management for Requirements Elicitation Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01640)
*Anmol Singhal,Paulo Carvalho,Travis Breaux*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 12 pages, 3 figures, 3 tables

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一种AI辅助需求获取工作流，结合理论引导的脚本生成、实时主题覆盖跟踪和按需后续问题生成，通过准实验发现与仅培训条件相比，AI辅助条件产生不同的访谈轨迹和更精炼的需求工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求获取访谈中访谈者需实时平衡主题覆盖、倾听和追问，但现有AI支持仅针对孤立任务，集成支持的影响未知，且脚本管理未被充分探索。

Method: 提出AI辅助需求获取工作流，包含基于业务目标的理论引导脚本生成、实时主题覆盖跟踪和按需后续问题生成；采用受试者间准实验，比较无培训AI辅助条件与有培训无AI辅助条件，并基于需求获取最佳实践评分标准进行评估。

Result: AI生成脚本得分高于仅培训脚本（92.8 vs 74.8）；AI辅助访谈覆盖主题更少（9.6 vs 14.5），脚本化问题覆盖率更高（86% vs 69%），每主题后续问题更多（3.43 vs 1.15），目标模型更精炼（最低层比例0.653 vs 0.598）；86%参与者认为主题跟踪最有用。

Conclusion: AI辅助条件与不同的访谈轨迹和需求工件相关，可作为需求获取的支架，为未来研究提供基础。

中文摘要：需求获取访谈要求访谈者在实时回应利益相关者的同时，平衡主题覆盖、积极倾听和自适应追问。尽管先前的工作已探索了对孤立访谈任务（如脚本生成和后续问题生成）的AI支持，但关于集成支持如何影响访谈以及会涌现出什么需求工件，目前知之甚少。此外，脚本管理——它帮助访谈者实时跟踪主题覆盖并决定何时进一步追问——仍未得到充分探索。本文提出了一种AI辅助的需求获取工作流，该工作流将基于理论、以业务目标为导向的脚本生成与实时主题覆盖跟踪及按需后续问题生成相结合。我们在一项受试者间准实验中评估了该工作流，比较了无培训、AI辅助条件与有培训、无AI辅助条件。基于由需求获取最佳实践衍生的评分标准，AI生成的脚本得分高于仅培训的脚本（92.8对74.8，满分100）。AI辅助访谈覆盖的主题更少（9.6对14.5），覆盖更多脚本化问题（86%对69%），每个主题的后续问题更多（3.43对1.15），并产生更精炼的目标模型（最低层目标比例0.653对0.598）。参与者认为脚本管理有用，将主题跟踪评为最有用的工作流功能（86%认同）。总体而言，这些结果表明，与仅培训条件相比，AI辅助条件与不同的访谈轨迹和不同的需求获取结果相关联，将AI辅助工作流定位为未来研究的需求获取支架。

Original abstract: Requirements elicitation interviews require interviewers to balance topic coverage, active listening, and adaptive probing while responding to stakeholders in real time. Although prior work has explored AI support for isolated interviewing tasks, such as script generation and follow-up question generation, little is known about how integrated support affects the interview and what requirements artifacts emerge. Furthermore, script management---which helps the interviewer track topic coverage in real time and decide when to probe further---remains underexplored. This paper presents an AI-assisted elicitation workflow that combines theory-guided script generation grounded in business goals with live support for topic coverage tracking and on-demand follow-up question generation. We evaluate the workflow in a between-subjects quasi-experimental study comparing a no-training, AI-assisted condition with a training, AI-unassisted condition. Based on a rubric derived from elicitation best practices, the AI-generated scripts score higher than training-only scripts (92.8 vs. 74.8 out of 100). AI-assisted interviews cover fewer topics (9.6 vs. 14.5), cover more scripted questions (86% vs. 69%), ask more follow-ups per topic (3.43 vs. 1.15), and produce more refined goal models (lowest-level goal fraction 0.653 vs. 0.598). Participants find script management useful, rating topic tracking as the most useful workflow feature (86% agreement). Collectively, these results show that the AI-assisted condition is associated with a different interview trajectory and different elicited requirements than a training-only condition, positioning AI-assisted workflows as elicitation scaffolds for future studies.

</details>


### [142] [Coding Agents as Test-Suite Auditors: Finding What Official Suites Miss While Approaching What They Catch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01715)
*Shuyang Xie,Shuxiao Xie,Feng Zhu,Yanli Ji,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 24 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 一种使用现成编码智能体构建对抗性测试套件并通过认证链验证缺陷提交的方法，在AtCoder和Codeforces上发现了大量官方评测遗漏的缺陷提交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 官方在线评测的判定被当作黄金标准，但已有审计发现官方测试套件会接受有缺陷的提交，且未提供修复方案。

Method: 利用现成编码智能体作为测试套件审计者，构建对抗性测试套件；通过多个独立接受解、暴力解和按题验证器组成的认证链，不依赖官方评测器来确认提交是否有缺陷。

Result: 在AtCoder的20,375个已接受提交中，单个智能体验证了589个有缺陷提交，五个智能体联合下限为906个；五个智能体在官方套件捕获的逻辑错误上的覆盖率差距均在1.7个百分点以内；在无官方套件的Codeforces问题上，该方法在所有测试预算下优于五个复现基线。

Conclusion: 该方法既能审计现有官方套件的充分性，也能在缺少官方套件时构建有效测试套件，为评测代码模型提供了更可靠的缺陷检测手段。

中文摘要：在线评测的判定结果以及基于它们构建的数据集和基准，被视为用于评估和训练代码大语言模型的黄金标准。然而，先前的审计已经发出警告：官方测试套件会接受有缺陷的提交。但这些审计止步于警告，并未提供实用的补救措施。我们的补救措施包含两个部分：一个现成的编码智能体作为测试套件审计者，既构建对抗性测试套件以暴露官方套件遗漏的问题，也在没有官方套件的地方提供这些套件；一个认证链决定每个被智能体标记的提交是否真正有缺陷，而不依赖官方评测器：多个独立编写的被接受解法对每个测试的期望输出达成一致，暴力解法解决分歧，以及一个按问题配置的验证器认证每个失败的输入是合法的。这样一个智能体在AtCoder的20,375个被审计的已接受提交中识别出589个已验证的已接受但有缺陷的提交；将相同的认证扩展到所有五个智能体，得到的联合下限为906个这样的提交。五个智能体分别评分，在官方套件捕获的逻辑错误上，每个智能体的覆盖率与官方套件的差距都在1.7个百分点以内。在没有可用官方套件的截止日期后的Codeforces问题上，相同的测试构建方法在所有五个复现的基线上、在每个测试输入预算下都领先。在存在官方套件的地方，智能体审计套件的充分性而不是假定其充分；在没有官方套件的地方，智能体套件在我们复现和测试的方法中捕获了最多有缺陷的提交。

Original abstract: Online-judge verdicts and the datasets and benchmarks built on them are treated as ground truth for evaluating and training large language models for code. Yet prior audits have sounded a warning: official suites accept buggy submissions. These audits, however, stop at the warning and offer no practical remedy. Our remedy has two parts: an off-the-shelf coding agent, serving as a test-suite auditor, both builds adversarial test suites to expose what official suites miss and supplies these suites where no official suite exists; a certification chain determines whether each agent-flagged submission is genuinely buggy without relying on the official judge: multiple independently written accepted solutions agree on the expected output for every test, brute-force solutions settle disagreements, and a per-problem validator certifies each failing input legal. One such agent identifies 589 verified accepted-but-buggy submissions among AtCoder's 20,375 audited accepted submissions; extending the same certification to all five agents yields a union floor of 906 such submissions. Five agents, scored separately, each stay within 1.7pp of official-suite coverage on logic bugs those suites catch. On post-cutoff Codeforces problems with no available official suites, the same test-building method leads all five reproduced baselines at every tested input budget. Where an official suite exists, the agent audits suite adequacy instead of assuming it; where none exists, agent suites catch the most buggy submissions among methods we reproduced and tested.

</details>


### [143] [Effective and Efficient Context Retrieval via Partial Dependency Graph for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01927)
*Zhongxin Liu,Zhonghao Jiang,Zhifan Ye,Haoye Wang,Jiakun Liu,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted by ASE 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出DyRetriever，一种基于部分依赖图的高效上下文检索方法，通过LLM进行多跳推理，显著提升仓库级代码生成的Pass@1并降低检索开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法在仓库级代码生成中检索相关代码时，基于相似性的方法难以捕捉依赖关系，而基于图的方法依赖人工规则和静态全局图，灵活性和成本都不理想。人类开发者通过部分依赖图迭代探索的方式启发了DyRetriever的设计。

Method: DyRetriever利用LLM选择入口点函数，沿代码依赖图进行多跳推理，并用LLM的语义判断函数相关性；它按需构建部分依赖图，用后即弃，避免全局静态图。

Result: 在CoderEval和DevEval上，DyCoder的Pass@1相对提升25.63%和59.73%，同时比静态依赖图方法快7.4倍。

Conclusion: DyRetriever通过模拟人类开发者的检索行为，实现了动态、灵活且低成本的上下文检索，显著改进了仓库级代码生成的准确性和效率。

中文摘要：基于LLM的仓库级代码生成旨在利用软件仓库中的可用上下文生成代码，要求LLM对复杂的代码依赖进行推理。由于上下文窗口有限且对仓库特定理解不足，LLM通常依赖检索增强生成（RAG）来整合相关代码。早期的RAG方法主要采用基于相似性的检索，这往往无法检索到目标函数所依赖的代码片段。最近的工作引入了基于图的检索来建模此类依赖，但通常依赖人工设计的规则和静态全局图，导致灵活性有限且构建和维护成本高。相比之下，人类开发者通过隐式构建部分依赖图并沿其迭代检查来收集有用的上下文。受此行为启发，我们提出了DyRetriever，一种通过部分依赖图进行高效上下文检索的方法。DyRetriever首先使用LLM选择一组入口点函数，然后沿代码依赖图进行多跳推理。在多跳推理过程中，它利用LLM的语义理解来验证一个函数是否有助于生成目标函数，从而消除了人工设计的规则，并实现了跨场景的灵活性。DyRetriever不是静态构建全局依赖图，而是按需构建部分图并在使用后丢弃，从而降低了构建和维护成本。我们将DyRetriever与基于相似性的代码检索器集成，构建了DyCoder，并在CoderEval和DevEval上进行了评估。实验结果表明，与现有的基于RAG的方法相比，DyCoder在CoderEval和DevEval上的相对Pass@1分别提高了25.63%和59.73%，同时比基于静态依赖图构建的基线快7.4倍。

Original abstract: LLM-based repository-level code generation aims to generate code using the context available in a software repository, requiring LLMs to reason over complex code dependencies. Due to limited context windows and insufficient repository-specific understanding, LLMs typically rely on retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to incorporate relevant code. Early RAG approaches primarily employ similarity-based retrieval, which often fails to retrieve code snippets that the target function depends on. Recent work introduces graph-based retrieval to model such dependencies, but typically relies on manually designed rules and static global graphs, leading to limited flexibility and high construction and maintenance costs. In contrast, human developers collect helpful context by implicitly constructing a partial dependency graph and iteratively inspecting along it. Inspired by this behavior, we propose DyRetriever, an efficient context retrieval method via partial dependency graphs. DyRetriever uses an LLM to first select a set of entry-point functions and then perform multi-hop reasoning along the code dependency graph. During multi-hop reasoning, it uses the LLM's semantic understanding to validate whether a function can help generate the target function, eliminating manually designed rules and enabling flexibility across scenarios. Instead of statically constructing a global dependency graph, DyRetriever builds a partial graph on demand and discards it after use, reducing construction and maintenance costs. We integrate DyRetriever with a similarity-based code retriever to build DyCoder and evaluate it on CoderEval and DevEval. Experimental results show that DyCoder achieves relative Pass@1 improvements of 25.63% and 59.73% on CoderEval and DevEval, respectively, compared with existing RAG-based methods, while being 7.4x faster than baselines based on static dependency graph construction.

</details>


### [144] [TELLER: Non-intrusive Cross-Layer Root-Cause Analysis for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01975)
*Ruilin Xu,Junyi Li,Pengfei Chen,Zongxuan Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 12 pages, 1 figure, 9 tables. Accepted to the 41st IEEE/ACM International Conference on Automated Software Engineering (ASE 2026)

DOI: [10.1145/3832783.3837440](https://doi.org/10.1145/3832783.3837440)

Author affiliations: Ruilin Xu: Sun Yat-sen University; Junyi Li: Sun Yat-sen University; Pengfei Chen: Sun Yat-sen University; Zongxuan Xie: Sun Yat-sen University

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出TELLER框架，利用NVTX/CUPTI跟踪和服务日志，通过依赖感知切片、TPE压缩和多模态根因模型，实现LLM推理的跨层根因分析，并在多节点GPU负载上验证了压缩与准确率的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理已成为持续运行的软件服务，但单请求跨越多个层级，根因分析困难；现有分析器暴露原始时间线，日志诊断缺乏跨层语义和请求级结构。

Method: 非侵入式收集NVTX/CUPTI跟踪和服务日志，重建请求级调用链树并对齐日志；引入依赖感知的因果上下文切片和TPE分词器压缩为结构令牌；结合数值候选定位与多模态模型联合预测异常步骤、定位可疑算子并生成自然语言解释。

Result: 多节点GPU实验显示，适度TPE词汇表减少80%以上跟踪长度并取得最佳水平/垂直视图性能，过度压缩显著降低诊断质量；进一步分析表明其在实际场景下具有实用性和低开销。

Conclusion: TELLER为LLM推理RCA提供了实用的分诊和证据定位基础，能够有效支持跨层异常诊断与解释生成。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）推理已从离线工作负载演变为持续运行的软件服务，然而根因分析仍然困难，因为单个请求跨越推理引擎、Python/C++后端、主机CUDA API、GPU内核和分布式通信。现有分析器暴露原始时间线，而基于日志的诊断往往缺失跨层执行语义和请求级结构。我们提出了TELLER，一个非侵入式的、基于跟踪和日志的LLM推理根因分析框架。TELLER首先在无需修改模型二进制文件的情况下收集NVTX/CUPTI跟踪和服务日志，然后重建每个请求的调用链树，并将日志行与相应的执行步骤对齐。我们引入了一种依赖感知的因果上下文切片，保留父子结构、时间顺序和通信关系，以及一种跟踪对编码（TPE）分词器，将此类切片压缩为具有父、深度和持续时间属性的紧凑结构令牌序列。在这些表示之上，TELLER将数值候选定位与多模态根因模型相结合，该模型联合预测异常步骤、定位可疑算子并生成自然语言解释。在多节点GPU推理工作负载上的实验显示了明确的压缩-准确率权衡：适度的TPE词汇表将每步跟踪长度减少80%以上，同时在水平（跨节点通信）和垂直（节点内执行栈）视图上均达到最佳整体性能，而更激进的压缩则显著降低诊断质量。在低故障先验、强化基线、模态消融、解释质量检查和跟踪开销下的进一步分析表明，TELLER为LLM推理RCA提供了一个实用的分诊和证据定位基础。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference has evolved from an offline workload into a continuously operated software service, yet root-cause analysis remains difficult because a single request spans the inference engine, Python/C++ backend, host CUDA APIs, GPU kernels, and distributed communication. Existing profilers expose raw timelines, while log-based diagnosis often misses cross-layer execution semantics and request-level structure. We present TELLER, a non-intrusive Trace- and Log-aware LLM inference Root-cause analysis framework. TELLER first collects NVTX/CUPTI traces and service logs without modifying model binaries, then reconstructs per-request call-chain trees and aligns log lines with the corresponding execution steps. We introduce a dependency-aware causal-context slice that preserves parent-child structure, temporal order, and communication relations, and a Trace Pair Encoding (TPE) tokenizer that compresses such slices into compact structural token sequences with parent, depth, and duration attributes. On top of these representations, TELLER combines numeric candidate localization with a multimodal root-cause model that jointly predicts abnormal steps, localizes suspicious operators, and generates natural-language explanations. Experiments on multi-node GPU inference workloads show a clear compression-accuracy trade-off: a moderate TPE vocabulary reduces per-step trace length by more than 80% while achieving the best overall performance on both horizontal (cross-node communication) and vertical (within-node execution stack) views, whereas more aggressive compression substantially degrades diagnosis quality. Further analyses under low-fault priors, strengthened baselines, modality ablations, explanation-quality checks, and tracing overhead show that TELLER provides a practical triage and evidence-localization substrate for LLM inference RCA.

</details>


### [145] [VulnGym: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Repository-Level Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02001)
*Kexing Ji,Jiachen Liu,Enze Hu,Cuiyun Gao,Keke Lian,Yongheng Liu,Lei Zhang,Tian Dong,Hao Chen,Wang Bin*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: VulnGym是一个真实仓库级基准，用于评估编码智能体的漏洞检测能力，包含细粒度痕迹注释，实验发现现有智能体在端到端检测和证据构建上仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要基于预选代码片段进行漏洞分类，无法评估编码智能体在仓库级自主定位漏洞的能力，且缺少细粒度痕迹注释难以诊断检测过程的局限性。

Method: 构建VulnGym基准，将GitHub审查公告与对应漏洞版本仓库对齐，包含184个公告和408个漏洞条目，标注行级入口点、关键操作和漏洞痕迹，并设计端到端任务及三个oracle子任务。

Result: 评估表明当前编码智能体在端到端仓库级漏洞检测和构造准确支持痕迹方面均表现有限。

Conclusion: VulnGym揭示了现有编码智能体的不足，为未来提升仓库级漏洞检测能力提供了诊断基础和评估基准。

中文摘要：基于LLM的漏洞检测近期取得了显著进展，而编码智能体进一步将这一能力从孤立的代码片段扩展到完整代码仓库。这种转变要求智能体自主探索仓库并定位与漏洞相关的代码，而不是对预选函数进行检测。然而，现有基准主要侧重于对预选代码片段进行漏洞分类，限制了其评估编码智能体在仓库级漏洞检测中的能力。此外，由于缺乏细粒度的漏洞痕迹注释，检测过程中的能力局限性仍难以探究。为解决这些局限，我们提出了VulnGym，一个用于评估编码智能体漏洞检测的真实仓库级基准。VulnGym将经过审查的GitHub公告与其对应的受影响版本仓库对齐。它包含23个仓库中的184个公告和408个漏洞条目，每个条目均标注了行级入口点、关键操作和漏洞痕迹。利用这一细粒度真值，VulnGym定义了一个端到端检测任务和三个基于oracle的子任务，以联合评估漏洞检测并诊断代码定位和证据构建中的局限性。我们的评估表明，当前的编码智能体在端到端仓库级漏洞检测以及构建准确的支持痕迹方面仍存在不足。

Original abstract: Recent advances in LLM-based vulnerability detection have shown promising results, while coding agents further extend this capability from isolated code snippets to complete repositories. This shift requires agents to autonomously explore repositories and locate vulnerability-relevant code, instead of performing detection on preselected functions. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on vulnerability classification over preselected code snippets, limiting their ability to evaluate coding agents in repository-level vulnerability detection. Moreover, without fine-grained vulnerability trace annotations, the capability limitations underlying the detection process remain difficult to explore. To address these limitations, we present \textbf{VulnGym}, a real-world repository-level benchmark for evaluating vulnerability detection by coding agents. VulnGym aligns reviewed GitHub advisories with their corresponding vulnerable version repositories. It contains 184 advisories and 408 vulnerability entries across 23 repositories, with each entry annotated with line-level entry points, critical operations, and vulnerability traces. Using this fine-grained ground truth, VulnGym defines an end-to-end detection task and three oracle-based subtasks to jointly evaluate vulnerability detection and diagnose limitations in code localization and evidence construction. Our evaluation indicates that current coding agents remain limited in both end-to-end repository-level vulnerability detection and the construction of accurate supporting traces.

</details>


### [146] [Pretraining on Call Graphs: When Binary Analysis Tasks Profit From Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02084)
*Samuel Valenzuela,Johannes Kinder*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures. Accepted at ICPC '26

Journal reference: Proceedings of the 34th IEEE/ACM International Conference on Program Comprehension, pp. 14-25, 2026

DOI: [10.1145/3794763.3794795](https://doi.org/10.1145/3794763.3794795)

Author affiliations: Samuel Valenzuela: Ludwig-Maximilians-Universität München; Johannes Kinder: Ludwig-Maximilians-Universität München

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本研究剖析了调用图增强对二进制函数嵌入的影响，发现其对BCSD的改进无法泛化到下游任务，且语义与语法性能存在权衡，但调用图上下文能增强鲁棒性，尤其在上下文依赖场景中有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多二进制函数嵌入模型仅使用函数自身作为输入，但已有研究尝试通过调用图改进嵌入。然而，这些改进对下游任务的实际影响尚不明确，因此需要系统剖析其在不同任务上的泛化能力和潜在权衡。

Method: 使用两个最先进的二进制函数嵌入模型生成嵌入，并应用一系列基于图的模型整合调用图上下文。在二进制代码相似性检测（BCSD）及多个下游任务（语义和语法）上评估性能，并进行解释性分析以观察鲁棒性和上下文依赖效果。

Result: 调用图增强在BCSD上带来改进，但这些改进不泛化到下游任务；语义与语法任务性能之间存在负相关；调用图增强显著提升嵌入鲁棒性，尤其是在困难场景；对命名空间相关函数的提升大于个体逻辑函数。

Conclusion: 调用图信息对二进制函数嵌入的优化需谨慎使用，其收益主要局限于特定场景（如上下文依赖的BCSD），不应期望其自动提升所有下游任务；在不同任务类型间存在固有权衡。

中文摘要：二进制函数嵌入模型被训练用于编码二进制代码的语义，以便能够推广到各种逆向工程任务，如二进制代码搜索、漏洞检测或恶意软件分类。虽然许多模型仅将所讨论的函数作为上下文输入，但已有成功的尝试通过利用调用图的信息来改进函数嵌入。在本研究中，我们剖析了这些嵌入优化的含义。我们使用一系列基于图的模型，对两个最先进的二进制函数嵌入模型生成的嵌入进行了实验。整合过程间上下文后，我们表明，二进制代码相似性检测（BCSD）的改进不一定会推广到下游任务，无论是语义性质还是语法性质的任务。更一般地，我们发现，针对语义相似性任务进行优化与在语法任务上表现较差相关。通过对数据集进行解释性分析，我们发现基于调用图的增强显著提高了嵌入的鲁棒性，特别是在初始模型难以处理的场景中。此外，我们观察到，添加的上下文对与命名空间相关的函数比对专注于个体逻辑的函数更有益，这证实了调用图在上下文依赖的场景中可以最有效地被利用。

Original abstract: Binary function embedding models are trained to encode the semantics of binary code in such a way that they can be generalized to a variety of reverse engineering tasks, such as binary code search, vulnerability detection, or malware classification. While many models only take the function in question as contextual input, there have been successful attempts to improve function embeddings by leveraging information from the call graph. In this study, we dissect the implications of these embedding refinements. We conduct experiments using a range of graph-based models on the embeddings generated by two state-of-the-art binary function embedding models. Integrating inter-procedural context, we show that improvements on binary code similarity detection (BCSD) will not necessarily generalize to downstream tasks, neither of semantic nor of syntactic nature. More generally, we find that optimizing for semantic similarity tasks correlates with worse performance on syntactic tasks. By conducting an explanatory analysis on the dataset, we find that the call graph-based enhancements significantly enhance the robustness of embeddings, particularly in scenarios where the initial models struggle. Furthermore, we observe that the added context is more beneficial for namespace-related functions than for those focused on individual logic, confirming that the call graph can be leveraged most effectively in context-dependent scenarios.

</details>


### [147] [Lossless Tensor Compression as Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02162)
*Jieke Shi,Junda He,Wenjia Jiang,Weifeng Sun,Shidong Pan,Zhensu Sun,Chengran Yang,Peixin Zhang,Yifan Jia,Zhou Yang,Thong Hoang,Xiwei Xu,Zhenchang Xing,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: Brevis通过程序合成实现无损张量压缩，在10个公开检查点上实现33.93%的存储缩减，优于通用和专用压缩器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型检查点数量和规模增长导致存储和传输成本上升；现有通用压缩器忽略张量结构，专用压缩器依赖固定流水线，缺乏通用性和紧凑性。

Method: 将无损张量压缩视为程序合成，设计类型化DSL和可逆算子捕获重复区域和浮点字段，利用检查点特定生成先验引导有界A*搜索合成紧凑的自包含程序，解码时直接执行实现位精确重建。

Result: 在10个公开检查点（共2.13 TB）上缩减至1.41 TB（存储缩减33.93%）；比四种通用压缩器（含zstd和gzip）存档最多小30.87%，并胜过ZipNN和DFloat11；实际并发下压缩3.60 GB/s、解压缩6.61 GB/s，且逐位无损。

Conclusion: Brevis证明了基于程序合成的张量压缩方法能够利用张量结构实现高压缩率和高吞吐，同时保持无损，为大规模模型检查点的高效存储和传输提供了有效方案。

中文摘要：模型检查点的数量和规模都在不断增长，这使得存档、传输和部署的成本日益增加。通用压缩器可以减少存储需求，但忽略了张量结构，而现有的张量专用压缩器则依赖于固定的、特定于格式的流水线。我们提出了Brevis，它将无损张量压缩表述为程序合成。我们设计了一种类型化的领域特定语言（DSL），通过一组可逆算子捕获重复出现的张量结构，例如重复区域和浮点字段。给定一个张量，Brevis合成一个自包含的DSL程序，以逐位精确的方式重建它。从一小部分具有代表性的张量样本中学习到的检查点特定生成先验，引导有界A*搜索来合成紧凑程序，这些程序随后可以直接执行以进行逐位精确解压缩。在涵盖语言、音频和图像生成模型的10个公开检查点上，Brevis将2.13 TB的检查点数据缩减为1.41 TB，存储缩减率达33.93%。与包括zstd和gzip在内的四种通用压缩器相比，它生成的存档最多小30.87%，并且比张量专用压缩器ZipNN和DFloat11生成的存档更小。在实际并发配置下，Brevis实现了3.60 GB/s的压缩速度和6.61 GB/s的解压缩速度，同时保留每个源字节。

Original abstract: Model checkpoints are growing in both number and size, which makes archival, transfer, and deployment increasingly costly. General-purpose compressors can reduce storage requirements but ignore tensor structure, whereas existing tensor-specific compressors rely on fixed and format-specific pipelines. We present Brevis, which formulates lossless tensor compression as program synthesis. We design a typed domain-specific language (DSL) that captures recurring tensor structures, such as repeated regions and floating-point fields, through a set of reversible operators. Given a tensor, Brevis synthesizes a self-contained DSL program that reconstructs it bit-exactly. A checkpoint-specific production prior, learned from a small representative sample of tensors, guides a bounded A* search to synthesize compact programs, which can later be executed directly for bit-exact decompression. On 10 public checkpoints spanning language, audio, and image generation models, Brevis reduces 2.13 TB of checkpoint data to 1.41 TB, a 33.93% storage reduction. It produces archives up to 30.87% smaller than those of four general-purpose compressors, including zstd and gzip, and smaller archives than the tensor-specific compressors ZipNN and DFloat11. Under a practical concurrency configuration, Brevis achieves 3.60 GB/s compression and 6.61 GB/s decompression while preserving every source byte.

</details>


### [148] [ECLAIR: A Causally-Grounded AI Framework for Scientific Discovery in Empirical Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02323)
*Alejandro Velasco,Daniel Rodriguez-Cardenas,Dipin Khati,David N. Palacio,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

DOI: [10.1145/3832783.3834544](https://doi.org/10.1145/3832783.3834544)

Author affiliations: Alejandro Velasco: Colonial Williamsburg Foundation / Williamsburg Regional Library; Daniel Rodriguez-Cardenas: Colonial Williamsburg Foundation / Williamsburg Regional Library; Dipin Khati: William & Mary; David N. Palacio: Microsoft (United States); Denys Poshyvanyk: Colonial Williamsburg Foundation / Williamsburg Regional Library

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: ECLAIR 是一个因果基础的 AI 框架，将 LLM 作为科学代理整合到软件工程实证研究的全过程，并通过案例研究发现提示设计对代码生成准确性有较小的负面因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统过于复杂，导致科学方法在软件工程实证研究中难以系统应用，需要一种因果推理支持的 AI 框架来增强研究的严谨性和可解释性。

Method: 提出 ECLAIR 框架，将 LLM 视为在因果推断原则下工作的主动科学代理，采用人在环路设计，覆盖从假设生成到分析解释的科学过程，并通过案例研究验证。

Result: 在两项 LLM 的代码生成实验中，指令式提示、更长少样本提示和签名增强提示均对准确性产生较小的负面因果效应，展示了因果推理在解释软件现象中的价值。

Conclusion: 该研究首次为在软件工程科学方法中嵌入 LLM 提供了因果基础的、结构化方法论，满足了实证 SE 研究的认识论要求，为严格的 AI 辅助研究奠定了基础。

中文摘要：科学方法长期以来一直指导着软件工程（SE）中的实证研究，但现代软件系统的复杂性常常阻碍其系统化应用。本文介绍了 _ECLAIR_，一个因果基础的 AI 框架，将大型语言模型（_LLMs_）整合到科学过程的每个阶段，从假设生成到分析和解释。_ECLAIR_ 将 _LLMs_ 视为在因果推断原理下运行的主动**科学代理**，并采用人在环路中的设计，以防止不健全的自动推理带来的风险。我们通过一个案例研究演示了该框架，该研究考察了提示设计如何影响两个 _LLMs_ 中的代码生成准确性。结果表明，对于这两种模型，指令式、更长的少样本和签名增强的提示对准确性产生了较小的负面因果效应，说明了因果推理如何为解释软件现象发生的原因提供有原则的基础。这项研究提出了第一个因果基础的、结构化的方法论，用于将 _LLMs_ 嵌入 SE 中的科学方法，围绕实证 SE 研究的认识论要求而设计，为严格的 AI 辅助研究奠定了基础。

Original abstract: The scientific method has long guided empirical research in Software Engineering (SE), but the complexity of modern software systems often hinders its systematic application. This paper introduces _ECLAIR_, a causally grounded AI framework that integrates Large Language Models (_LLMs_) into every stage of the scientific process, from hypothesis generation to analysis and interpretation. _ECLAIR_ treats _LLMs_ as active **scientific agents** operating under the principles of causal inference, within a human-in-the-loop design that safeguards against the risks of unsound automated reasoning. We demonstrate the framework through a case study examining how prompt design influences code generation accuracy in two _LLMs_. Results show that, for both models, instruction-style, longer few-shot, and signature-augmented prompts yield small negative causal effects on accuracy, illustrating how causal reasoning provides a principled foundation for explaining _why_ software phenomena occur. This study presents the first causally grounded structured methodology for embedding _LLMs_ within the scientific method in SE, designed around the epistemological demands of empirical SE research, establishing a basis for rigorous AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [149] [On Automated and Explainable Provenance of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02329)
*Alejandro Velasco,Nathan Wintersgill,Trevor Stalnaker,Oscar Chaparro,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出一个研究愿景，主张下一代代码生成AI工具应内置可解释的来源追踪能力，以解决AI生成代码的不透明性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成AI缺乏对输出来源的解释，导致开发者、组织和合规人员难以理解和信任生成结果；现有缓解措施仅事后标记，无法解释原因或指导改进。

Method: 基于NSF资助的研究项目，结合对开发者、模型用户及合规/法律人员的实证研究，提出四个追溯维度，并规划结合大规模实证研究与事后因果和可解释性技术的研究方案。

Result: 实证证据表明来源信息是实际需求但当前工具未提供；问题被刻画为四个追溯维度，并识别出关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: 下一代CodeGenAI工具必须建立在可解释来源的基础上，社区需共同努力推进这一愿景。

中文摘要：生成式AI用于代码生成已彻底改变了软件开发，但也引入了一个关键的可解释性问题：AI生成代码的来源对于使用它的开发者、部署它的组织以及负责确保其合法性和质量标准的合规专业人员来说是不透明的。现有的缓解措施在事后标记有问题的输出，却不解释模型为何产生这些输出，或如何改进未来的生成。我们提出一个研究愿景，基于美国国家科学基金会资助的研究项目，主张下一代CodeGenAI工具必须建立在可解释的来源（explainable provenance）基础上：即自动的、事后追溯能力，将生成的代码与其生成的提示组件、训练数据实例、全局数据特征以及内部模型组件联系起来。我们通过对软件开发者、模型用户和合规/法律专业人员的研究所获得的实证证据支撑了这一愿景，这些证据表明来源信息是当前工具并未提供的实际需求。我们沿四个追溯维度刻画了该问题，概述了一个将大规模实证研究与事后因果分析和可解释性技术相结合的研究计划，并确定了社区为实现这一愿景必须解决的关键开放挑战。

Original abstract: Generative AI for code generation has transformed software development, but it has also introduced a critical transparency problem: the origins of AI-generated code are opaque to the developers who use it, the organizations that deploy it, and the compliance professionals responsible for ensuring its legal and quality standards. Existing mitigations flag problematic outputs after the fact without explaining why a model produced them or how future generation could be improved. We present a research vision, grounded in a U.S. NSF-funded research grant, that argues that the next generation of CodeGenAI tools must be built on a foundation of explainable provenance: automated, post-hoc traceability that links generated code back to the prompt components, training data instances, global data features, and internal model components that caused its generation. We grounded this vision in empirical evidence from studies of software developers, model users, and compliance/legal professionals, which show that provenance information is a practical necessity that current tools do not provide. We characterize the problem across four traceability dimensions, outline a research program combining large-scale empirical studies with post-hoc causal and interpretability techniques, and identify the key open challenges that the community must address to realize this vision.

</details>


### [150] [Profitability of Open-Source Software Product Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02398)
*Shivendu P. Singh,Narayan Ramasubbu,Chris F. Kemerer,M. Zia Hydari*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 基于977家美国高科技企业2001-2025年的数据，发现开源采用平均提高毛利率4-5%，主要源于劳动生产率提升；但需外部贡献达到约35%且依赖公司资源配置，且高盈利均需与内部研发结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科技公司采用开源模式开发产品，但开放性是否真正带来经济回报缺乏系统的实证证据。本研究旨在量化开源采用对企业盈利能力的影响，并揭示其作用机制和条件。

Method: 分析2001年至2025年间977家美国高科技企业的数据，采用实证研究方法（如回归分析）评估开源采用对毛利率的影响，并检验劳动生产率、外部贡献比例及资源配置的调节作用。

Result: 开源采用平均使毛利率提高4-5%，主要通过提高劳动生产率实现；收益仅在外部志愿者贡献约35%工作时出现，且高盈利配置中必须包含持续的内部研发。

Conclusion: 开源应被视为知识整合战略，需与公司资源匹配；政策应支持开源生态和志愿者社区，以释放其广泛经济价值。

中文摘要：许多科技公司现在以开放方式构建软件产品，邀请外部开发者在GitHub等平台上与员工一同贡献。这种产品开发的开放性是否带来回报？本研究分析了2001年至2025年间977家美国高科技企业，发现采用开源软件平均使企业的毛利率提高了4-5%。这些收益主要源于更高的劳动生产率，因为企业将外部贡献者的多元知识整合到内部工作流程中，在不相应增加劳动力成本的情况下拓宽了组织的知识基础。然而，这种回报仅在外部志愿者承担了相当比例的工作（本样本中约为35%）时才显现，并取决于企业的资源配置。尽管存在多种盈利路径，但将开源产品开发与持续的内部研发相结合是所有高盈利配置中的核心条件。对于管理者而言，启示是应将开源视为一种与公司资源相匹配的知识整合战略，而不仅仅是以更低成本获取外部努力的途径。对于政策制定者，研究结果强调了开源生态系统的广泛经济价值，以及培育熟练志愿者社区和公私合作开源基础设施的政策的必要性。

Original abstract: Many technology firms now build software products in the open, inviting outside developers to contribute alongside their employees on platforms like GitHub. Does this openness in product development pay off? Analyzing 977 U.S. high-tech firms from 2001 to 2025, this study finds that open-source adoption raised firms' gross margins by 4-5% on average. These gains flow largely through higher labor productivity, as firms integrate external contributors' diverse knowledge into internal workflows, broadening the organizational knowledge base without a commensurate rise in labor costs. However, the payoff emerges only when outside volunteers supply a meaningful share of the work (around 35% in this sample), and hinges on the firm's resource configuration. While there are multiple pathways to profitability, pairing open-source product development with sustained internal R&D is a core condition present in all high-profitability configurations. For managers, the lesson is to treat open source as a knowledge-integration strategy matched to the firm's resources, not merely a means of sourcing external effort at lower cost. For policymakers, the findings underscore the broad economic value of open-source ecosystems and the need for policies that foster skilled volunteer communities and public-private open-source infrastructure.

</details>


### [151] [SWE-Touch: Benchmarking Coding Agents When Users Touch the Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02499)
*Yuqiao Tan,Jinxiang Meng,Fangyu Lei,Minzheng Wang,Shizhu He,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Preprint. Our code is available at https://github.com/Trae1ounG/SWE-Touch

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出SWE-Touch框架，通过对抗性编辑测试编码代理在共享工作区中的表现，发现现有模型解决率显著下降，主要原因是缺乏对工作区变化的感知和自适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未模拟共享工作区中用户的实时代码修改，本文旨在探究编码代理理解和响应此类代码变更的能力。

Method: SWE-Touch从修复轨迹中挖掘任务关键区域，用用户补丁生成器构造与任务冲突的对抗性编辑，并在代理触达相关代码时注入上下文消息；在SWE-bench Verified、SWE-Bench Pro和DeepSWE上评估九个模型。

Result: Counter-Edit使平均解决率下降7.7个百分点，且在更长周期基准上仍退化；轨迹分析显示代理保留冲突代码或未充分验证修改。

Conclusion: 强自主性能不足以保证共享工作区协作，需提升检测工作区变化、协调冲突编辑和验证受影响行为的能力。

中文摘要：现实世界的软件开发要求编码代理在共享工作区中操作，用户可能在任务进行中检查并修改代码，然而现有的仓库级基准通常评估单独工作的代理或将用户参与限制为消息。这引出了一个问题：编码代理如何理解并响应共享工作区中的代码变更？我们提出了SWE-Touch，一个通过验证过的对抗性编辑（Counter-Edits）来压力测试这一设置的框架：这些编辑是对任务相关代码的合理修改，但与任务完成相冲突。SWE-Touch从多个修复轨迹中挖掘任务关键区域，使用独立的用户补丁生成器（User Patch Generator）构建编辑，并在代理到达相关代码时注入带有上下文的用户消息。我们在SWE-bench Verified上评估了九个编码模型，并额外在来自SWE-Bench Pro和DeepSWE的更长周期任务上进行了实验。Counter-Edit将SWE-bench Verified上的平均解决率降低了7.7个百分点，并且在两个更长周期的基准上退化仍然存在。轨迹分析将这些失败归因于对不断演变的工作区感知有限：代理可能保留冲突代码，或者在未充分重新检查仓库并通过针对性测试验证修改后代码的情况下替换它。这些发现表明，强大的自主性能尚不能确保共享工作区协作所需的状态感知和自适应行为，并指出检测工作区变更、将冲突编辑与任务协调以及验证受影响行为是未来优化的关键能力。

Original abstract: Real-world software development requires coding agents to operate in shared workspaces where users may inspect and modify code during an ongoing task, yet existing repository-level benchmarks typically evaluate agents working alone or restrict user participation to messages. This leads us to ask: how do coding agents understand and respond to code changes in a shared workspace? We introduce SWE-Touch, a framework that stress-tests this setting through validated Counter-Edits: plausible edits to task-relevant code that conflict with task completion. SWE-Touch mines task-critical regions from multiple repair trajectories, uses a separate User Patch Generator to construct the edits, and injects them with contextual user messages when agents reach the relevant code. We evaluate nine coding models on SWE-bench Verified, with additional experiments on longer-horizon tasks from SWE-Bench Pro and DeepSWE. Counter-Edit lowers average resolve rate by 7.7 percentage points on SWE-bench Verified, with degradation also persisting on both longer-horizon benchmarks. Trajectory analysis links these failures to limited awareness of the evolving workspace: agents may retain conflicting code or replace it without sufficiently re-inspecting the repository and validating the revised code with targeted tests. These findings show that strong autonomous performance does not yet ensure the state awareness and adaptive behavior needed for shared-workspace collaboration, and point to detecting workspace changes, reconciling conflicting edits with the task, and verifying the affected behavior as key capabilities for future optimization.

</details>


### [152] [Decomposing the Doer Effect in Programming Practice: Code Writing Stands Out Among Active Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02541)
*Arun Balajiee Lekshmi Narayanan,Gillian Gold,Jordan Barria-Pineda,Quinn K Wolter,Peter Brusilovsky,Paulo Carvalho*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 研究发现“做者效应”在计算机科学教育中成立，主动练习的效果是被动的3.2倍，且其中代码编写效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究“做者效应”是否适用于计算机科学教学，并区分不同主动练习形式（如代码编写、追踪等）的有效性差异。

Method: 分析334名学生在11个学期中使用交互式练习系统（含五种内容类型）的日志数据，对比主动与被动活动的学习成果。

Result: 主动练习活动关联的学习成果是被动活动的3.2倍；其中代码编写与后测成绩提升的关联最强，其他类型无显著关联。

Conclusion: 挑战性且带反馈的练习活动（如代码编写）对学习效果至关重要，建议在教学中优先采用此类形式。

中文摘要：“做者效应”表明，积极参与练习活动比被动观看内容与学习成果的关联更强。在“做者效应”文献中，“做”特指主动练习。然而，这种分类将不同形式的主动练习视为等同，尚未明确某些类型的主动练习是否比其他类型更有效。在本文中，我们研究了“做者效应”是否适用于计算机科学教学，以及某些“做”的形式是否比其他形式更突出。我们分析了来自11个学期、334名学习入门和中级Java课程学生的日志数据，这些学生使用了一个包含五种内容类型的交互式练习系统：代码编写、代码追踪、代码补全、代码可视化和代码解释。与先前“做者效应”研究一致，我们发现主动练习活动与学习成果的关联是被动活动的3.2倍。有趣的是，在主动练习中，代码编写与后测成绩提高的关联最强，而其他活动类型均未显示出类似的关联。这些结果强调了具有挑战性、有反馈支持的练习活动（如代码编写问题）的重要性。

Original abstract: The "doer effect" suggests that actively doing practice activities is more strongly associated with learning outcomes than passively viewing content. In the doer effect literature, "doing" refers specifically to active practice. However, this categorization treats different forms of active practice as equivalent, leaving open whether some types of active practice are more effective than others. In this paper, we investigate whether the doer effect extends to computer science instruction and whether some forms of doing stand out compared to other forms. We analyze log data from 334 students across 11 semesters of introductory and intermediate Java who used an interactive practice system with five content types: Code Writing, Code Tracing, Code Completion, Code Visualizations, and Code Explanations. Consistent with prior doer effect work, we find that active practice activities were associated with 3.2 times better learning outcomes than passive activities. Interestingly, among the active practice, code writing was the most strongly associated with improved posttest performance, while no other activity type showed a comparable association. These results highlight the importance of challenging, feedback-supported practice activities, such as code writing problems.

</details>


### [153] [ACEM: A Cost Estimation Model for Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02582)
*Mohammad El-Ramly*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 27 pages, under journal publication

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出ACEM模型，将智能体软件开发成本分解为LLM、HITL和基础设施三个维度，并引入RF、CF、HIS等因子刻画非确定性成本，映射常用规模度量以支持成本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件成本估算模型假设开发工作量主要由人力驱动，而智能体软件工程中AI智能体承担主要实现工作，导致产生新的非确定性成本维度（LLM令牌消耗、HITL监督、基础设施），现有模型无法适用，需要新框架衔接标准规模度量与这种成本结构。

Method: ACEM将总成本分解为LLM、HITL和基础设施三个可加维度；定义修订因子（RF）建模重试开销、上下文因子（CF）建模上下文累积影响、HITL强度评分（HIS）四级监督分类；并将用例点、故事点、功能点映射到令牌消耗，给出完整的模型结构与校准方法，但常数保留为符号以等待经验标定。

Result: 提出了一个完全指定的模型结构和校准方法论，但所有常数留待经验数据标定；作为早期提案，尚未给出实证结果，需真实项目数据检验其有效性。

Conclusion: ACEM为智能体软件工程的成本估算提供了新的理论框架，可以复用现有的项目规模数据，但需要研究社区通过实际项目数据进行校准、测试和扩展，以完善模型并验证其适用性。

中文摘要：传统的软件成本估算模型（如COCOMO II、功能点、故事点）假设开发工作主要受设计、编码和测试中的人力劳动驱动。自主AI智能体承担大量实现工作而人类专注于规划、规格说明和验证的智能体软件工程挑战了这一假设。新的成本维度随之出现：大规模语言模型（LLM）在智能体动作中的令牌消耗、人在回路（HITL）监督工作，以及智能体编排和工具的基础设施成本。这些成本是非确定性的：相同的任务可能消耗不同的令牌、遵循不同的推理路径，并需要不同的人类纠正，这些现象在传统开发中并不存在。需要一个新的框架来将标准规模度量与这种成本结构衔接起来。本文提出了ACEM（智能体成本估算模型），它将智能体开发总成本分解为三个可加维度：LLM、HITL和基础设施成本。ACEM引入了三个用于智能体动态的构造：修订因子（RF），对输出拒绝和重试带来的令牌开销进行建模；上下文因子（CF），捕捉上下文累积导致的令牌消耗上升；以及HITL强度评分（HIS），一种四级监督分类方案。它还进一步将用例点、故事点和功能点映射到估计的令牌消耗，使组织能够重用现有的项目范围界定数据进行智能体成本预测。ACEM作为一个完全指定的模型结构和校准方法被提出，其中的常数在经验基础确立之前保持符号化。作为一个早期阶段的提议，它邀请研究社区通过真实项目数据来校准、测试和扩展该模型。

Original abstract: Traditional software cost estimation models, such as COCOMO II, Function Points, and Story Points, assume that development effort is primarily driven by human labor in design, coding, and testing. Agentic software engineering, where autonomous AI agents perform substantial implementation work and humans focus on planning, specification, and validation, challenges this assumption. New cost dimensions arise: large language model (LLM) token consumption across agent actions, Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) oversight effort, and infrastructure costs for agent orchestration and tooling. These costs are nondeterministic: identical tasks may consume different tokens, follow divergent reasoning paths, and require varying human correction, phenomena absent in traditional development. A new framework is needed to bridge standard sizing metrics with this cost structure.
  This paper proposes ACEM (Agentic Cost Estimation Model), which decomposes total agentic development cost into three additive dimensions: LLM, HITL, and infrastructure cost. ACEM introduces three constructs for agentic dynamics: the Revision Factor (RF), modeling token overhead from output rejection and retries; the Context Factor (CF), capturing rising token consumption as context accumulates; and the HITL Intensity Score (HIS), a four-level oversight classification scheme. It further maps Use Case Points, Story Points, and Function Points to estimated token consumption, enabling organizations to reuse existing project-scoping data for agentic cost forecasting.
  ACEM is presented as a fully specified model structure and calibration methodology, with constants left symbolic pending empirical grounding. As an early-stage proposal, it invites the research community to calibrate, test, and extend the model through real project data.
